if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is not set');

export default defineConfig({
	schema: './src/lib/server/db/schema.ts',
	dialect: 'sqlite',
	dbCredentials: { url: process.env.DATABASE_URL },
	verbose: true,
	strict: true
});


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: .github/workflows/deploy.yml
================================================================

name: CI/CD — Check, Build & Deploy to VPS

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
      - master
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  packages: write

env:
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io

jobs:
  quality-and-build:
    name: Type-Check & Build Docker Image
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    outputs:
      image_tag: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_lowercase }}
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup pnpm
        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          version: latest

      - name: Setup Node.js 22
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: pnpm install

      - name: Run Svelte & TypeScript Check
        run: pnpm run check

      - name: Normalize image name to lowercase
        id: meta
        run: |
          IMAGE_LC=$(echo "${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ github.repository }}:latest" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          echo "image_lowercase=${IMAGE_LC}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - name: Log in to GitHub Container Registry (GHCR)
        uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Build and push Docker image
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          push: true
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_lowercase }}
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max

  deploy-to-vps:
    name: Deploy to Personal VPS
    needs: quality-and-build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Copy docker-compose.yml to VPS
        uses: appleboy/scp-action@v0.1.7
        with:
          host: ${{ secrets.VPS_HOST }}
          username: ${{ secrets.VPS_USER || 'root' }}
          key: ${{ secrets.VPS_SSH_KEY }}
          port: ${{ secrets.VPS_PORT || 22 }}
          source: 'docker-compose.yml'
          target: '/opt/new-life'

      - name: Pull latest image and restart container on VPS
        uses: appleboy/ssh-action@v1.1.0
        with:
          host: ${{ secrets.VPS_HOST }}
          username: ${{ secrets.VPS_USER || 'root' }}
          key: ${{ secrets.VPS_SSH_KEY }}
          port: ${{ secrets.VPS_PORT || 22 }}
          script: |
            mkdir -p /opt/new-life/data
            cd /opt/new-life
            echo "${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}" | docker login ghcr.io -u "${{ github.actor }}" --password-stdin
            cat << 'EOF' > .env
            IMAGE_NAME=${{ needs.quality-and-build.outputs.image_tag }}
            ORIGIN=${{ secrets.APP_ORIGIN || 'http://localhost:3000' }}
            EOF
            docker compose pull
            docker compose up -d --remove-orphans
            docker image prune -f

----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: Dockerfile
================================================================

# Stage 1: Build application & native better-sqlite3 addon
FROM node:22-bookworm-slim AS builder

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
	python3 \
	make \
	g++ \
	&& rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

RUN corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@latest --activate

WORKDIR /app

COPY package.json pnpm-workspace.yaml ./
RUN pnpm install

COPY . .

ENV DATABASE_URL=/tmp/build.db
RUN pnpm run build

# Stage 2: Minimal production runtime
FROM node:22-bookworm-slim AS runner

WORKDIR /app

ENV NODE_ENV=production
ENV PORT=3000
ENV HOST=0.0.0.0
ENV DATABASE_URL=/app/data/local.db

RUN mkdir -p /app/data

COPY --from=builder /app/build ./build
COPY --from=builder /app/package.json ./package.json
COPY --from=builder /app/node_modules ./node_modules

EXPOSE 3000

CMD ["node", "build/index.js"]

================================================================
PATH: src/service-worker.ts
================================================================

/// <reference no-default-lib="true"/>
/// <reference lib="esnext" />
/// <reference lib="webworker" />

import { assets, immutable } from '$app/manifest';
import { version } from '$app/env';

const sw = self as unknown as ServiceWorkerGlobalScope;

const CACHE_NAME = `newlife-pwa-v-${version}`;
const CORE_STATIC_FILES = ['/', '/manifest.webmanifest', '/favicon.svg', '/Vazirmatn.woff2'];
const PRECACHE_ASSETS = Array.from(new Set([...immutable, ...assets, ...CORE_STATIC_FILES]));
const STATIC_ASSET_SET = new Set(PRECACHE_ASSETS);

const APP_ROUTES_TO_WARM = [
	'/',
	'/manifest.webmanifest',
	'/favicon.svg',
	'/Vazirmatn.woff2'
];

let lastMutationAtMs = 0;

function isCacheableAppResponse(response: Response): boolean {
	if (!response.ok || response.status !== 200 || response.redirected) {
		return false;
	}
	if (response.url && new URL(response.url).pathname.startsWith('/login')) {
		return false;
	}
	return true;
}

async function warmUpAppShell(cache: Cache): Promise<void> {
	try {
		const htmlResp = await fetch('/', {
			credentials: 'same-origin',
			headers: { accept: 'text/html' }
		});
		if (isCacheableAppResponse(htmlResp)) {
			await cache.put('/', htmlResp.clone());
		}
	} catch {
		// ignore offline warm-up error
	}
}

sw.addEventListener('install', (event: ExtendableEvent) => {
	event.waitUntil(
		(async () => {
			const cache = await caches.open(CACHE_NAME);

			await Promise.allSettled(
				PRECACHE_ASSETS.map(async (assetUrl) => {
					try {
						const resp = await fetch(assetUrl, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
						if (resp.ok) {
							await cache.put(assetUrl, resp);
						}
					} catch {
						// ignore single asset precache failure
					}
				})
			);

			await warmUpAppShell(cache);
		})()
	);
});

sw.addEventListener('activate', (event: ExtendableEvent) => {
	event.waitUntil(
		(async () => {
			const keys = await caches.keys();
			for (const key of keys) {
				if (key !== CACHE_NAME && key.startsWith('newlife-pwa-')) {
					await caches.delete(key);
				}
			}
			await sw.clients.claim();
			const cache = await caches.open(CACHE_NAME);
			await warmUpAppShell(cache);
		})()
	);
});

sw.addEventListener('message', (event: ExtendableMessageEvent) => {
	const payload = event.data as { type?: string } | null;
	if (payload?.type === 'WARMUP_ALL_ROUTES') {
		event.waitUntil(
			(async () => {
				const cache = await caches.open(CACHE_NAME);
				await warmUpAppShell(cache);
			})()
		);
	} else if (payload?.type === 'SKIP_WAITING') {
		sw.skipWaiting();
	}
});

async function fetchWithTimeout(request: Request, timeoutMs = 1800): Promise<Response> {
	const controller = new AbortController();
	const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
	try {
		return await fetch(request, { signal: controller.signal });
	} finally {
		clearTimeout(timer);
	}
}

async function updateCacheInBackground(
	cache: Cache,
	request: Request,
	url: URL
): Promise<Response | null> {
	try {
		const networkResponse = await fetchWithTimeout(request, 4000);
		if (isCacheableAppResponse(networkResponse)) {
			await cache.put(request, networkResponse.clone());
			if (!url.search || !(await cache.match(url.pathname))) {
				await cache.put(url.pathname, networkResponse.clone());
			}
		}
		return networkResponse;
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}

sw.addEventListener('fetch', (event: FetchEvent) => {
	const { request } = event;
	const url = new URL(request.url);

	if (url.origin !== sw.location.origin) return;

	if (request.method !== 'GET') {
		if (!url.pathname.startsWith('/api/sync')) {
			lastMutationAtMs = Date.now();
		}
		return;
	}

	if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/') || url.pathname.startsWith('/login')) return;

	const isDataJsonRequest = url.pathname.endsWith('/__data.json');
	const isHtmlNavigation =
		request.mode === 'navigate' ||
		request.headers.get('accept')?.includes('text/html') ||
		false;

	event.respondWith(
		(async () => {
			const cache = await caches.open(CACHE_NAME);

			// 1. Static Assets, Hashed Chunks & Fonts: Strict Cache-First (Zero Network Consumption)
			const isStaticResource =
				STATIC_ASSET_SET.has(url.pathname) ||
				url.pathname.startsWith('/_app/immutable/') ||
				url.pathname.endsWith('.woff2') ||
				url.pathname.endsWith('.svg') ||
				url.pathname.endsWith('.webmanifest');

			if (isStaticResource) {
				const cachedStatic =
					(await cache.match(request, { ignoreSearch: true })) ||
					(await cache.match(url.pathname, { ignoreSearch: true }));
				if (cachedStatic) return cachedStatic;

				try {
					const netRes = await fetch(request);
					if (netRes.ok) {
						await cache.put(request, netRes.clone());
						await cache.put(url.pathname, netRes.clone());
					}
					return netRes;
				} catch {
					// Fall through to offline handling
				}
			}

			// 2. HTML Navigation requests: Strict Instant App Shell from Cache (SPA Mode)
			if (isHtmlNavigation) {
				const cachedShell =
					(await cache.match(url.pathname, { ignoreSearch: true })) ||
					(await cache.match('/', { ignoreSearch: true }));

				if (cachedShell) {
					return cachedShell;
				}

				try {
					const networkResponse = await fetchWithTimeout(request, 2000);
					if (isCacheableAppResponse(networkResponse)) {
						await cache.put('/', networkResponse.clone());
						await cache.put(url.pathname, networkResponse.clone());
						return networkResponse;
					}
				} catch {
					const fallbackRoot = await cache.match('/', { ignoreSearch: true });
					if (fallbackRoot) return fallbackRoot;
				}
			}

			// 4. Handle SvelteKit __data.json requests safely without crashing router
			if (isDataJsonRequest) {
				const cachedData =
					(await cache.match(request, { ignoreSearch: true })) ||
					(await cache.match(url.pathname, { ignoreSearch: true }));
				if (cachedData) return cachedData;

				try {
					const networkResponse = await fetchWithTimeout(request, 1500);
					if (isCacheableAppResponse(networkResponse)) {
						await cache.put(request, networkResponse.clone());
						return networkResponse;
					}
				} catch {
					return new Response(
						JSON.stringify({
							type: 'data',
							nodes: [{ type: 'data', data: {}, uses: {} }]
						}),
						{
							status: 200,
							headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }
						}
					);
				}
			}

			// 5. General assets fallback
			try {
				const networkResponse = await fetchWithTimeout(request, 1800);
				if (isCacheableAppResponse(networkResponse)) {
					await cache.put(request, networkResponse.clone());
				}
				return networkResponse;
			} catch {
				const cached = await cache.match(request, { ignoreSearch: true });
				if (cached) return cached;

				return new Response(JSON.stringify({ offline: true }), {
					status: 200,
					headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }
				});
			}
		})()
	);
});

================================================================
PATH: src/hooks.server.ts
================================================================

import { redirect } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import type { Handle } from '@sveltejs/kit/hooks';
import { dev } from '$app/env';
import {
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	isPasswordConfigured,
	validateAndExtendSession
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/auth.repo';

export const handle: Handle = async ({ event, resolve }) => {
	const passwordConfigured = isPasswordConfigured();
	const rawToken = event.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME) ?? null;

	let isAuthenticated = false;

	const isSecureRequest =
		!dev &&
		(event.url.protocol === 'https:' ||
			event.request.headers.get('x-forwarded-proto') === 'https');

	if (passwordConfigured && rawToken) {
		const sessionStatus = validateAndExtendSession(rawToken);
		isAuthenticated = sessionStatus.valid;

		if (sessionStatus.valid && sessionStatus.shouldRefreshCookie) {
			event.cookies.set(
				SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
				rawToken,
				getSessionCookieOptions(isSecureRequest)
			);
		} else if (!sessionStatus.valid) {
			event.cookies.delete(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, { path: '/' });
		}
	}

	event.locals.isPasswordConfigured = passwordConfigured;
	event.locals.isAuthenticated = isAuthenticated;
	event.locals.sessionToken = isAuthenticated ? rawToken : null;

	const pathname = event.url.pathname;
	const isLoginPage = pathname.startsWith('/login');
	const isStaticAsset =
		pathname.startsWith('/_app') ||
		pathname.endsWith('.webmanifest') ||
		pathname.endsWith('.woff2') ||
		pathname.endsWith('.svg') ||
		pathname.endsWith('.png') ||
		pathname.endsWith('.ico');

	if (!isStaticAsset && !isLoginPage && (!passwordConfigured || !isAuthenticated)) {
		if (pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
			return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }), {
				status: 401,
				headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }
			});
		}
		const redirectTarget = pathname + event.url.search;
		const query =
			redirectTarget && redirectTarget !== '/'
				? `?redirectTo=${encodeURIComponent(redirectTarget)}`
				: '';
		redirect(303, `/login${query}`);
	}

	if (isLoginPage && passwordConfigured && isAuthenticated) {
		const hasSecurityAction =
			event.request.method === 'POST' &&
			(event.url.searchParams.has('/logout') ||
				event.url.searchParams.has('/changePassword'));

		if (!hasSecurityAction) {
			redirect(303, '/');
		}
	}

	return resolve(event);
};


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/core/habit-engine.ts

import { COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL, TIER_SCORES } from '@domain/constants';
import type {
	DayStatusColor,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitHistoricalSpec,
	HabitLogItemInput,
	HabitTreeNode
} from '@domain/schemas';

export function resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(
	log:
		| Pick<
				HabitLogItemInput,
				'actualMinutesPerInterval' | 'totalActualMinutes' | 'completedIntervals'
		  >
		| null
		| undefined
): number {
	if (!log) return 0;
	const perInterval = log.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? 0;
	const intervals = log.completedIntervals;
	if (intervals !== null && intervals !== undefined && intervals > 0 && perInterval > 0) {
		return Number((intervals * perInterval).toFixed(1));
	}
	if (log.totalActualMinutes !== null && log.totalActualMinutes !== undefined && log.totalActualMinutes > 0) {
		return log.totalActualMinutes;
	}
	return perInterval;
}

export function serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit: HabitEntity): string {
	const spec: HabitHistoricalSpec = {
		title: habit.title,
		category: habit.category,
		scheduleLabel: habit.scheduleLabel,
		notes: habit.notes,
		bronzeDesc: habit.bronzeDesc,
		silverDesc: habit.silverDesc,
		goldDesc: habit.goldDesc,
		emergencyMinDesc: habit.emergencyMinDesc,
		isSituational: habit.isSituational
	};
	return JSON.stringify(spec);
}

export function parseHabitSpecSnapshot(
	rawJson: string | null | undefined,
	fallbackHabit: HabitEntity | undefined,
	fallbackTitle: string
): HabitHistoricalSpec {
	let parsed: Partial<HabitHistoricalSpec> = {};
	if (rawJson) {
		try {
			parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson) as Partial<HabitHistoricalSpec>;
		} catch {
			parsed = {};
		}
	}

	return {
		title: parsed.title || fallbackTitle,
		category: parsed.category || fallbackHabit?.category || 'عمومی',
		scheduleLabel: parsed.scheduleLabel ?? fallbackHabit?.scheduleLabel ?? null,
		notes: parsed.notes ?? fallbackHabit?.notes ?? null,
		bronzeDesc: parsed.bronzeDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.bronzeDesc ?? null,
		silverDesc: parsed.silverDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.silverDesc ?? null,
		goldDesc: parsed.goldDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.goldDesc ?? null,
		emergencyMinDesc: parsed.emergencyMinDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.emergencyMinDesc ?? null,
		isSituational: Boolean(parsed.isSituational ?? fallbackHabit?.isSituational)
	};
}

export function resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(
	habit: Pick<
		HabitEntity,
		| 'trackingMode'
		| 'scheduleLabel'
		| 'bronzeDesc'
		| 'silverDesc'
		| 'goldDesc'
		| 'emergencyMinDesc'
	>,
	achievedTier: number | null | undefined
): string | null {
	if (habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
		return habit.scheduleLabel?.trim() || null;
	}
	if (achievedTier === null || achievedTier === undefined) {
		return null;
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
		return 'امروز موقعیتش پیش نیامد';
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) {
		const minRule = habit.bronzeDesc?.trim();
		return minRule ? `عدم انجام (${minRule})` : 'انجام نشد';
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		return (
			habit.goldDesc?.trim() ||
			habit.silverDesc?.trim() ||
			habit.bronzeDesc?.trim() ||
			null
		);
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		return habit.silverDesc?.trim() || habit.bronzeDesc?.trim() || null;
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE) {
		return habit.bronzeDesc?.trim() || habit.emergencyMinDesc?.trim() || null;
	}
	return null;
}

export function getApplicableHabitsForDate(
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	options?: {
		dateIso?: string;
		hasExistingEntry?: boolean;
		loggedHabitIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
	}
): HabitEntity[] {
	const loggedSet = options?.loggedHabitIds ?? new Set<string>();
	const dateIso = options?.dateIso;
	const hasExistingEntry = Boolean(options?.hasExistingEntry);

	const parentIdsWithLoggedChildren = new Set<string>();
	for (const h of habits) {
		if (h.parentId && loggedSet.has(h.id)) {
			parentIdsWithLoggedChildren.add(h.parentId);
		}
	}

	return habits.filter((h) => {
		if (loggedSet.has(h.id) || parentIdsWithLoggedChildren.has(h.id)) {
			return true;
		}
		if (h.status === 'archived') {
			return false;
		}
		if (hasExistingEntry && dateIso && h.createdAtIso > dateIso) {
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	});
}

export function buildHabitTree(
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	options?: {
		dateIso?: string;
		hasExistingEntry?: boolean;
		loggedHabitIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
	}
): {
	tieredRoots: HabitTreeNode[];
	cadenceHabits: HabitEntity[];
} {
	const applicableHabits = getApplicableHabitsForDate(habits, options);
	const childrenByParent = new Map<string, HabitEntity[]>();

	for (const h of applicableHabits) {
		if (h.parentId) {
			const list = childrenByParent.get(h.parentId) ?? [];
			list.push(h);
			childrenByParent.set(h.parentId, list);
		}
	}

	const tieredRoots: HabitTreeNode[] = [];
	const cadenceHabits: HabitEntity[] = [];

	for (const h of applicableHabits) {
		if (h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
			cadenceHabits.push(h);
		} else if (!h.parentId) {
			const children = (childrenByParent.get(h.id) ?? []).sort(
				(a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder
			);
			tieredRoots.push({
				...h,
				children,
				timeSlot: h.timeSlot
			});
		}
	}

	tieredRoots.sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);
	cadenceHabits.sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);

	return { tieredRoots, cadenceHabits };
}

export function getMaxSupportedTier(
	habit: Pick<HabitEntity, 'bronzeDesc' | 'silverDesc' | 'goldDesc'>
): 1 | 2 | 3 {
	if (habit.goldDesc && habit.goldDesc.trim() !== '' && habit.goldDesc.trim() !== '~') {
		return TIER_SCORES.GOLD;
	}
	if (habit.silverDesc && habit.silverDesc.trim() !== '' && habit.silverDesc.trim() !== '~') {
		return TIER_SCORES.SILVER;
	}
	return TIER_SCORES.BRONZE;
}

export function computeCompositeParentDisplay(
	children: HabitEntity[],
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>
): string {
	if (children.length === 0) return '-';
	const tokens = children.map((child) => {
		const log = logsByHabitId[child.id];
		if (!log || log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined) {
			return '0';
		}
		if (log.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
			return '-';
		}
		return String(log.achievedTier);
	});
	const allMax = tokens.every((t) => t === String(TIER_SCORES.GOLD));
	if (allMax) return COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL;
	return tokens.join('/');
}

export function isHabitNodeCompleted(params: {
	habitId: string;
	habits: HabitEntity[];
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>;
	hasExistingEntry: boolean;
	isDirty: boolean;
}): boolean {
	const { habitId, habits, logsByHabitId, hasExistingEntry, isDirty } = params;
	const habit = habits.find((h) => h.id === habitId);
	if (!habit) return false;

	const children = habits.filter((h) => h.parentId === habitId && h.status === 'active');
	if (children.length > 0) {
		return children.every((c) => {
			const tier = logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier;
			return tier !== null && tier !== undefined;
		});
	}

	if (habit.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
		const tier = logsByHabitId[habit.id]?.achievedTier;
		return tier !== null && tier !== undefined;
	}

	const actual = resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(logsByHabitId[habit.id]);
	return actual > 0 && (hasExistingEntry || isDirty);
}

export function computeDailyCompletionStats(params: {
	applicableHabits: HabitEntity[];
	loggedHabitIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>;
	hasExistingEntry: boolean;
	isDirty: boolean;
}): {
	completed: number;
	goldCount: number;
	total: number;
	percent: number;
} {
	const { applicableHabits, loggedHabitIds, logsByHabitId, hasExistingEntry, isDirty } =
		params;

	const trackable = applicableHabits.filter((h) => {
		if (h.status === 'paused' && !loggedHabitIds.has(h.id)) return false;
		const hasChildren = applicableHabits.some((c) => c.parentId === h.id);
		return !hasChildren;
	});

	const total = trackable.length;
	if (total === 0) return { completed: 0, goldCount: 0, total: 0, percent: 100 };

	let completed = 0;
	let goldCount = 0;

	for (const h of trackable) {
		const log = logsByHabitId[h.id];
		if (!log) continue;

		if (h.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
			const tier = log.achievedTier;
			if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
				completed++;
			} else if (tier !== null && tier !== undefined && tier > 0) {
				completed++;
				const maxTier = getMaxSupportedTier(h);
				if (tier === maxTier) goldCount++;
			}
		} else {
			const actual = resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log);
			const planned = log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? 0;
			if (actual > 0 && (hasExistingEntry || isDirty)) {
				completed++;
				if (planned <= 0 || actual >= planned) goldCount++;
			}
		}
	}

	return {
		completed,
		goldCount,
		total,
		percent: Math.round((completed / total) * 100)
	};
}

export function evaluateDayStatusColor(
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	logs: HabitLogItemInput[]
): DayStatusColor {
	if (logs.length === 0) return 'failed';

	const logMap = new Map(logs.map((l) => [l.habitId, l]));
	const loggedIds = new Set(
		logs
			.filter(
				(l) =>
					(l.achievedTier !== null && l.achievedTier !== undefined) ||
					resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(l) > 0
			)
			.map((l) => l.habitId)
	);
	const trackable = habits.filter((h) => {
		const isLogged = loggedIds.has(h.id);
		if (h.status !== 'active' && !isLogged) return false;
		const hasChildren = habits.some(
			(child) =>
				child.parentId === h.id &&
				(child.status === 'active' || loggedIds.has(child.id))
		);
		return !hasChildren;
	});

	if (trackable.length === 0) return 'optimal';

	let optimalCount = 0;
	let zeroCount = 0;

	for (const habit of trackable) {
		const log = logMap.get(habit.id);
		if (!log) {
			zeroCount++;
			continue;
		}

		if (habit.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
			const tier = log.achievedTier ?? 0;
			if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
				optimalCount++;
			} else if (tier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) {
				zeroCount++;
			} else {
				optimalCount++;
			}
		} else {
			const actualMinutes = resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log);
			const plannedMinutes = log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval;
			if (actualMinutes <= 0) {
				zeroCount++;
			} else if (plannedMinutes <= 0 || actualMinutes >= plannedMinutes) {
				optimalCount++;
			}
		}
	}

	if (zeroCount === trackable.length) return 'failed';
	if (optimalCount === trackable.length && zeroCount === 0) return 'optimal';
	return 'partial';
}

import { normalizeDigits } from '@domain/calendar';

const LINK_PATTERN =
	/https?:\/\/[^\s]+|www\.[^\s]+|\b[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\.(com|ir|org|net|io|dev|app|me|co|ai|gg|xyz)(\/[^\s]*)?/i;
const TEN_DIGIT_NUMBER_PATTERN = /\d{10,}/;

export function detectNoteSmartSignals(text: string): {
	hasLink: boolean;
	hasNumber: boolean;
	firstUrl: string | null;
} {
	const normalized = normalizeDigits(text);
	const linkMatch = normalized.match(LINK_PATTERN);
	const rawLink = linkMatch ? linkMatch[0] : null;
	const firstUrl = rawLink
		? /^https?:\/\//i.test(rawLink)
			? rawLink
			: `https://${rawLink}`
		: null;

	return {
		hasLink: Boolean(firstUrl),
		hasNumber: TEN_DIGIT_NUMBER_PATTERN.test(normalized),
		firstUrl
	};
}

import {
	formatJalaliHuman,
	getDaysUntilIso,
	getPersianWeekdayIndex,
	getTodayIso,
	isoToDate,
	isoToJalaliSlash,
	JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS,
	shiftIsoDate
} from '@domain/calendar';
import {
	CADENCE_SUCCESS_RATIOS,
	LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META,
	QUALITY_SCORES,
	TIER_SCORES
} from '@domain/constants';
import type {
	AnalyticsRawEntry,
	AnalyticsRawLog,
	AnalyticsTimeRange,
	DayHabitOutcome,
	FullAnalyticsReport,
	HabitComparisonRow,
	HabitDailyPoint,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitEvolutionItem,
	HabitRuleDiffItem,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	SelectedHabitAnalytics,
	TagCorrelationStat,
	WeekdayPerformanceStat
} from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	getMaxSupportedTier,
	resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
} from './habit-engine';

function resolveDateRangeWindow(
	range: AnalyticsTimeRange,
	entries: AnalyticsRawEntry[],
	logs: AnalyticsRawLog[],
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	fallbackEndIso: string
): { startIso: string; endIso: string; dateList: string[] } {
	let maxIso = fallbackEndIso;
	let minIso = fallbackEndIso;

	for (const e of entries) {
		if (e.dateIso > maxIso) maxIso = e.dateIso;
		if (e.dateIso < minIso) minIso = e.dateIso;
	}
	for (const l of logs) {
		if (l.dateIso > maxIso) maxIso = l.dateIso;
		if (l.dateIso < minIso) minIso = l.dateIso;
	}
	for (const h of habits) {
		if (h.createdAtIso && h.createdAtIso < minIso) {
			minIso = h.createdAtIso;
		}
	}

	const endIso = maxIso;
	let startIso = endIso;

	if (range === '7d') {
		startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -6);
	} else if (range === '30d') {
		startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -29);
	} else if (range === '90d') {
		startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -89);
	} else {
		const totalSpan = getDaysUntilIso(minIso, endIso) + 1;
		if (totalSpan < 14) {
			startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -13);
		} else {
			startIso = minIso;
		}
	}

	const daysCount = Math.max(1, getDaysUntilIso(startIso, endIso) + 1);
	const dateList: string[] = [];
	for (let i = 0; i < daysCount; i++) {
		dateList.push(shiftIsoDate(startIso, i));
	}

	return { startIso, endIso, dateList };
}

function evaluateSingleLeafHabitDay(
	habit: HabitEntity,
	log: AnalyticsRawLog | undefined,
	hasDailyEntry: boolean
): {
	outcome: DayHabitOutcome;
	tierLevel: number | null;
	score: number | null;
	ruleSnapshot: string | null;
	actualMinutes: number | null;
	plannedMinutes: number | null;
	completedIntervals: number | null;
} {
	if (habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
		const plannedMinutes =
			log?.plannedMinutesSnapshot && log.plannedMinutesSnapshot > 0
				? log.plannedMinutesSnapshot
				: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval > 0
					? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
					: 10;

		if (!log && !hasDailyEntry) {
			return {
				outcome: 'unlogged',
				tierLevel: null,
				score: null,
				ruleSnapshot: null,
				actualMinutes: null,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals: null
			};
		}

		const actualMinutes = log ? resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log) : 0;
		const completedIntervals = log?.completedIntervals ?? null;

		if (actualMinutes <= 0) {
			if (!log) {
				return {
					outcome: 'unlogged',
					tierLevel: null,
					score: null,
					ruleSnapshot: null,
					actualMinutes: null,
					plannedMinutes,
					completedIntervals: null
				};
			}
			return {
				outcome: 'failed',
				tierLevel: 0,
				score: 0,
				ruleSnapshot: `۰ دقیقه از هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه`,
				actualMinutes: 0,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals
			};
		}

		const ratio = plannedMinutes > 0 ? actualMinutes / plannedMinutes : 1;
		const score = Math.min(100, Math.round(ratio * 100));

		if (ratio >= CADENCE_SUCCESS_RATIOS.GOLD_THRESHOLD) {
			return {
				outcome: 'gold',
				tierLevel: 3,
				score: QUALITY_SCORES.GOLD,
				ruleSnapshot: `${actualMinutes} دقیقه (تکمیل هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه)`,
				actualMinutes,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals
			};
		}
		if (ratio >= CADENCE_SUCCESS_RATIOS.SILVER_THRESHOLD) {
			return {
				outcome: 'silver',
				tierLevel: 2,
				score,
				ruleSnapshot: `${actualMinutes} دقیقه از هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه`,
				actualMinutes,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals
			};
		}
		return {
			outcome: 'bronze',
			tierLevel: 1,
			score: Math.max(QUALITY_SCORES.CADENCE_MIN_NONZERO, score),
			ruleSnapshot: `${actualMinutes} دقیقه از هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه`,
			actualMinutes,
			plannedMinutes,
			completedIntervals
		};
	}

	if (!log || log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined) {
		return {
			outcome: 'unlogged',
			tierLevel: null,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: null,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	const tier = log.achievedTier;
	const maxTier = getMaxSupportedTier(habit);
	const ruleSnapshot =
		log.achievedRuleSnapshot || resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, tier) || habit.scheduleLabel;

	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
		return {
			outcome: 'skipped',
			tierLevel: -1,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: ruleSnapshot || 'امروز موقعیتش پیش نیامد',
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) {
		return {
			outcome: 'failed',
			tierLevel: 0,
			score: QUALITY_SCORES.FAILED,
			ruleSnapshot: ruleSnapshot || 'انجام نشد',
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	if (tier >= maxTier || tier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		return {
			outcome: 'gold',
			tierLevel: 3,
			score: QUALITY_SCORES.GOLD,
			ruleSnapshot,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		return {
			outcome: 'silver',
			tierLevel: 2,
			score: QUALITY_SCORES.SILVER,
			ruleSnapshot,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	return {
		outcome: 'bronze',
		tierLevel: 1,
		score: maxTier === 1 ? QUALITY_SCORES.BRONZE_MAX : QUALITY_SCORES.BRONZE_DEFAULT,
		ruleSnapshot,
		actualMinutes: null,
		plannedMinutes: null,
		completedIntervals: null
	};
}

function evaluateHabitDay(
	habit: HabitEntity,
	children: HabitEntity[],
	logsByHabitOnDate: Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog> | undefined,
	hasDailyEntry: boolean
) {
	if (children.length === 0) {
		const log = logsByHabitOnDate?.get(habit.id);
		return evaluateSingleLeafHabitDay(habit, log, hasDailyEntry);
	}

	const childResults = children.map((child) => ({
		child,
		evalResult: evaluateSingleLeafHabitDay(
			child,
			logsByHabitOnDate?.get(child.id),
			hasDailyEntry
		)
	}));

	const loggedChildren = childResults.filter((r) => r.evalResult.outcome !== 'unlogged');
	if (loggedChildren.length === 0) {
		return {
			outcome: 'unlogged' as DayHabitOutcome,
			tierLevel: null,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: null,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	const scoredChildren = loggedChildren.filter((r) => r.evalResult.score !== null);
	if (scoredChildren.length === 0) {
		return {
			outcome: 'skipped' as DayHabitOutcome,
			tierLevel: -1,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: 'موقعیت زیرطرح‌ها پیش نیامد',
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	const avgScore = Math.round(
		scoredChildren.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.evalResult.score ?? 0), 0) /
			scoredChildren.length
	);

	let outcome: DayHabitOutcome = 'bronze';
	let tierLevel = 1;
	if (avgScore === 0) {
		outcome = 'failed';
		tierLevel = 0;
	} else if (avgScore >= 88) {
		outcome = 'gold';
		tierLevel = 3;
	} else if (avgScore >= 55) {
		outcome = 'silver';
		tierLevel = 2;
	}

	const summaryTokens = loggedChildren.map((r) => {
		const label =
			r.evalResult.outcome === 'gold'
				? 'طلا'
				: r.evalResult.outcome === 'silver'
					? 'نقره'
					: r.evalResult.outcome === 'bronze'
						? 'برنز'
						: r.evalResult.outcome === 'skipped'
							? 'پیش نیامد'
							: 'نشد';
		return `${r.child.title}: ${label}`;
	});

	return {
		outcome,
		tierLevel,
		score: avgScore,
		ruleSnapshot: summaryTokens.join(' • '),
		actualMinutes: null,
		plannedMinutes: null,
		completedIntervals: null
	};
}

function extractLifecycleRuleDiffs(
	prevJson: string | null,
	newJson: string | null
): HabitRuleDiffItem[] {
	if (!prevJson || !newJson) return [];
	try {
		const prev = JSON.parse(prevJson) as Record<string, unknown>;
		const next = JSON.parse(newJson) as Record<string, unknown>;
		const diffs: HabitRuleDiffItem[] = [];

		const fields: Array<{ key: string; label: string }> = [
			{ key: 'title', label: 'عنوان طرح' },
			{ key: 'scheduleLabel', label: 'زمان انجام' },
			{ key: 'bronzeDesc', label: 'شرط برنز (حداقل)' },
			{ key: 'silverDesc', label: 'شرط نقره' },
			{ key: 'goldDesc', label: 'شرط طلا' },
			{ key: 'emergencyMinDesc', label: 'حداقل اضطراری' },
			{ key: 'defaultMinutesPerInterval', label: 'دقیقه هدف در هر بازه' },
			{ key: 'cadenceWindowMinutes', label: 'پنجره زمانی تکرار (دقیقه)' },
			{ key: 'status', label: 'وضعیت فعال‌بودن' },
			{ key: 'notes', label: 'تبصره طرح' }
		];

		for (const f of fields) {
			const oldVal = String(prev[f.key] ?? '').trim();
			const newVal = String(next[f.key] ?? '').trim();
			if (oldVal !== newVal && (oldVal !== '' || newVal !== '')) {
				diffs.push({
					fieldLabel: f.label,
					previousValue: oldVal || 'تعریف‌نشده',
					newValue: newVal || 'حذف‌شده'
				});
			}
		}

		if (
			typeof prev.minutes === 'number' &&
			typeof next.minutes === 'number' &&
			prev.minutes !== next.minutes
		) {
			diffs.push({
				fieldLabel: 'هدف روزانه (دقیقه)',
				previousValue: `${prev.minutes} دقیقه`,
				newValue: `${next.minutes} دقیقه`
			});
		}

		return diffs;
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}

function buildEvolutionTimelineForHabit(
	habit: HabitEntity,
	children: HabitEntity[],
	allEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[],
	allLogsByDate: Map<string, Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog>>,
	entryByDate: Map<string, AnalyticsRawEntry>,
	referenceTodayIso: string
): HabitEvolutionItem[] {
	const relevantIds = new Set<string>([habit.id, ...children.map((c) => c.id)]);
	const relevantEvents = allEvents
		.filter((ev) => ev.habitId && relevantIds.has(ev.habitId))
		.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAtIso > a.createdAtIso ? 1 : b.createdAtIso < a.createdAtIso ? -1 : 0));

	return relevantEvents.map((ev) => {
		const meta = LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META[ev.eventType];
		const human = formatJalaliHuman(ev.effectiveDateIso, referenceTodayIso);
		const diffs = extractLifecycleRuleDiffs(ev.previousStateJson, ev.newStateJson);

		let beforeScores: number[] = [];
		let afterScores: number[] = [];

		for (let offset = -14; offset < 0; offset++) {
			const dIso = shiftIsoDate(ev.effectiveDateIso, offset);
			const res = evaluateHabitDay(
				habit,
				children,
				allLogsByDate.get(dIso),
				entryByDate.has(dIso)
			);
			if (res.score !== null) beforeScores.push(res.score);
		}

		for (let offset = 0; offset <= 14; offset++) {
			const dIso = shiftIsoDate(ev.effectiveDateIso, offset);
			const res = evaluateHabitDay(
				habit,
				children,
				allLogsByDate.get(dIso),
				entryByDate.has(dIso)
			);
			if (res.score !== null) afterScores.push(res.score);
		}

		const beforeSuccessRate =
			beforeScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(beforeScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / beforeScores.length)
				: null;

		const afterSuccessRate =
			afterScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(afterScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / afterScores.length)
				: null;

		const impactDelta =
			beforeSuccessRate !== null && afterSuccessRate !== null
				? afterSuccessRate - beforeSuccessRate
				: null;

		return {
			id: ev.id,
			habitId: ev.habitId,
			habitTitle: ev.habitTitleSnapshot,
			eventType: ev.eventType,
			eventTypeLabel: meta.label,
			badgeClasses: meta.badgeClasses,
			effectiveDateIso: ev.effectiveDateIso,
			jalaliSlash: isoToJalaliSlash(ev.effectiveDateIso),
			humanDateLabel: human.fullLabel,
			summary: ev.summary,
			reason: ev.reason,
			diffs,
			beforeSuccessRate,
			afterSuccessRate,
			impactDelta
		};
	});
}

function computeStreaksFromSeries(
	series: ReadonlyArray<{ outcome: DayHabitOutcome }>
): {
	currentStreak: number;
	bestStreak: number;
} {
	let bestStreak = 0;
	let running = 0;

	for (const pt of series) {
		if (pt.outcome === 'gold' || pt.outcome === 'silver' || pt.outcome === 'bronze') {
			running++;
			if (running > bestStreak) bestStreak = running;
		} else if (pt.outcome === 'failed') {
			running = 0;
		}
	}

	let currentStreak = 0;
	for (let i = series.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
		const out = series[i].outcome;
		if (out === 'gold' || out === 'silver' || out === 'bronze') {
			currentStreak++;
		} else if (out === 'failed') {
			break;
		}
	}

	return { currentStreak, bestStreak };
}

function computeTrendDelta(validScores: ReadonlyArray<number>, fallbackAvg: number): number {
	if (validScores.length < 2) return 0;
	const mid = Math.floor(validScores.length / 2);
	const firstHalf = validScores.slice(0, mid);
	const secondHalf = validScores.slice(mid);
	const firstAvg =
		firstHalf.length > 0
			? firstHalf.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / firstHalf.length
			: fallbackAvg;
	const secondAvg =
		secondHalf.length > 0
			? secondHalf.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / secondHalf.length
			: fallbackAvg;
	return Math.round(secondAvg - firstAvg);
}

export function buildFullAnalyticsReport(params: {
	habits: HabitEntity[];
	entries: AnalyticsRawEntry[];
	logs: AnalyticsRawLog[];
	lifecycleEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[];
	selectedHabitId?: string | null;
	range: AnalyticsTimeRange;
	fallbackEndDateIso: string;
}): FullAnalyticsReport {
	const {
		habits,
		entries,
		logs,
		lifecycleEvents,
		selectedHabitId,
		range,
		fallbackEndDateIso
	} = params;

	const habitById = new Map(habits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));
	const childrenByParentId = new Map<string, HabitEntity[]>();
	for (const h of habits) {
		if (h.parentId) {
			const list = childrenByParentId.get(h.parentId) ?? [];
			list.push(h);
			childrenByParentId.set(h.parentId, list);
		}
	}

	const entryByDate = new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e]));
	const logsByDate = new Map<string, Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog>>();
	for (const log of logs) {
		let dayMap = logsByDate.get(log.dateIso);
		if (!dayMap) {
			dayMap = new Map();
			logsByDate.set(log.dateIso, dayMap);
		}
		dayMap.set(log.habitId, log);
	}

	const { startIso, endIso, dateList } = resolveDateRangeWindow(
		range,
		entries,
		logs,
		habits,
		fallbackEndDateIso
	);

	const sortedHabits = [...habits].sort((a, b) => {
		if (a.status === 'archived' && b.status !== 'archived') return 1;
		if (a.status !== 'archived' && b.status === 'archived') return -1;
		return a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder;
	});

	const selectableHabits = sortedHabits.map((h) => {
		const parent = h.parentId ? habitById.get(h.parentId) : null;
		const children = childrenByParentId.get(h.id) ?? [];
		const isComposite = children.length > 0;
		const prefix = parent ? `↳ ${h.title} (${parent.title})` : h.title;
		const suffix =
			h.status === 'archived'
				? ' [بایگانی]'
				: isComposite
					? ' [مجموعه‌طرح]'
					: h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
						? ' [تکرارشونده]'
						: '';

		return {
			habit: h,
			parentTitle: parent?.title ?? null,
			isComposite,
			displayLabel: `${prefix}${suffix}`
		};
	});

	const targetHabit =
		(selectedHabitId ? habitById.get(selectedHabitId) : null) ??
		selectableHabits[0]?.habit ??
		null;

	let optimalDaysCount = 0;
	let partialDaysCount = 0;
	let failedDaysCount = 0;
	for (const dIso of dateList) {
		const entry = entryByDate.get(dIso);
		if (!entry) continue;
		if (entry.dayStatus === 'optimal') optimalDaysCount++;
		else if (entry.dayStatus === 'partial') partialDaysCount++;
		else if (entry.dayStatus === 'failed') failedDaysCount++;
	}
	const totalLoggedDays = optimalDaysCount + partialDaysCount + failedDaysCount;
	const overallConsistencyRate =
		totalLoggedDays > 0
			? Math.round(((optimalDaysCount + partialDaysCount) / totalLoggedDays) * 100)
			: 0;

	const eventsInWindow = lifecycleEvents.filter(
		(e) => e.effectiveDateIso >= startIso && e.effectiveDateIso <= endIso
	);

	const comparisonRows: HabitComparisonRow[] = selectableHabits.map((item) => {
		const h = item.habit;
		const children = childrenByParentId.get(h.id) ?? [];
		const seriesScores: Array<{ iso: string; outcome: DayHabitOutcome; score: number | null }> =
			[];

		let goldCount = 0;
		let silverCount = 0;
		let bronzeCount = 0;
		let failedCount = 0;
		let skippedCount = 0;

		for (const dIso of dateList) {
			const res = evaluateHabitDay(
				h,
				children,
				logsByDate.get(dIso),
				entryByDate.has(dIso)
			);
			seriesScores.push({ iso: dIso, outcome: res.outcome, score: res.score });
			if (res.outcome === 'gold') goldCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'silver') silverCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'bronze') bronzeCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'failed') failedCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'skipped') skippedCount++;
		}

		const loggedDays = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount + failedCount;
		const successDays = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount;
		const consistencyRate =
			loggedDays > 0 ? Math.round((successDays / loggedDays) * 100) : 0;

		const validScores = seriesScores
			.map((s) => s.score)
			.filter((s): s is number => s !== null);
		const avgQualityScore =
			validScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(validScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / validScores.length)
				: 0;

		const trendDelta = computeTrendDelta(validScores, avgQualityScore);
		const { currentStreak, bestStreak } = computeStreaksFromSeries(seriesScores);

		const relevantIds = new Set([h.id, ...children.map((c) => c.id)]);
		const lifecycleChangesCount = lifecycleEvents.filter(
			(ev) => ev.habitId && relevantIds.has(ev.habitId)
		).length;

		return {
			habit: h,
			parentTitle: item.parentTitle,
			isComposite: item.isComposite,
			loggedDays,
			successDays,
			goldCount,
			silverCount,
			bronzeCount,
			failedCount,
			skippedCount,
			consistencyRate,
			avgQualityScore,
			currentStreak,
			bestStreak,
			trendDelta,
			lifecycleChangesCount
		};
	});

	let selectedHabitReport: SelectedHabitAnalytics | null = null;

	if (targetHabit) {
		const todayIso = getTodayIso();
		const children = childrenByParentId.get(targetHabit.id) ?? [];
		const parent = targetHabit.parentId ? habitById.get(targetHabit.parentId) : null;
		const evolutionTimeline = buildEvolutionTimelineForHabit(
			targetHabit,
			children,
			lifecycleEvents,
			logsByDate,
			entryByDate,
			todayIso
		);

		const eventsByDate = new Map<string, HabitEvolutionItem[]>();
		for (const ev of evolutionTimeline) {
			const list = eventsByDate.get(ev.effectiveDateIso) ?? [];
			list.push(ev);
			eventsByDate.set(ev.effectiveDateIso, list);
		}

		const dailySeries: HabitDailyPoint[] = [];
		const recentValidScores: number[] = [];

		let goldCount = 0;
		let silverCount = 0;
		let bronzeCount = 0;
		let failedCount = 0;
		let skippedCount = 0;
		let totalActualMinutes = 0;
		let totalPlannedMinutes = 0;
		let cadenceLoggedDays = 0;

		for (const dIso of dateList) {
			const dt = isoToDate(dIso);
			const weekdayIndex = getPersianWeekdayIndex(dt);
			const weekdayLabel = JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[weekdayIndex] ?? '';
			const human = formatJalaliHuman(dIso, todayIso);
			const entry = entryByDate.get(dIso);

			const evalRes = evaluateHabitDay(
				targetHabit,
				children,
				logsByDate.get(dIso),
				Boolean(entry)
			);

			if (evalRes.score !== null) {
				recentValidScores.push(evalRes.score);
			}
			const windowSlice = recentValidScores.slice(-7);
			const rollingAvgScore =
				windowSlice.length > 0
					? Math.round(windowSlice.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / windowSlice.length)
					: null;

			if (evalRes.outcome === 'gold') goldCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'silver') silverCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'bronze') bronzeCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'failed') failedCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'skipped') skippedCount++;

			if (evalRes.actualMinutes !== null) {
				totalActualMinutes += evalRes.actualMinutes;
				totalPlannedMinutes += evalRes.plannedMinutes ?? 0;
				cadenceLoggedDays++;
			}

			dailySeries.push({
				dateIso: dIso,
				jalaliSlash: isoToJalaliSlash(dIso),
				humanLabel: human.fullLabel,
				weekdayLabel,
				weekdayIndex,
				outcome: evalRes.outcome,
				tierLevel: evalRes.tierLevel,
				score: evalRes.score,
				rollingAvgScore,
				ruleSnapshot: evalRes.ruleSnapshot,
				actualMinutes: evalRes.actualMinutes,
				plannedMinutes: evalRes.plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals: evalRes.completedIntervals,
				dayTags: entry?.tags ?? [],
				lifecycleEventsOnDay: eventsByDate.get(dIso) ?? []
			});
		}

		const loggedDaysCount = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount + failedCount;
		const successDaysCount = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount;
		const consistencyRate =
			loggedDaysCount > 0 ? Math.round((successDaysCount / loggedDaysCount) * 100) : 0;
		const goldRate =
			loggedDaysCount > 0 ? Math.round((goldCount / loggedDaysCount) * 100) : 0;

		const avgQualityScore =
			recentValidScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(
						recentValidScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / recentValidScores.length
					)
				: 0;

		const trendDelta = computeTrendDelta(recentValidScores, avgQualityScore);

		const { currentStreak, bestStreak } = computeStreaksFromSeries(dailySeries);

		const weekdayBuckets = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, idx) => ({
			weekdayIndex: idx,
			weekdayLabel: JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[idx] ?? '',
			loggedDays: 0,
			successDays: 0,
			goldDays: 0,
			scoreSum: 0
		}));

		for (const pt of dailySeries) {
			if (pt.score === null) continue;
			const b = weekdayBuckets[pt.weekdayIndex];
			if (!b) continue;
			b.loggedDays++;
			b.scoreSum += pt.score;
			if (pt.outcome === 'gold' || pt.outcome === 'silver' || pt.outcome === 'bronze') {
				b.successDays++;
			}
			if (pt.outcome === 'gold') {
				b.goldDays++;
			}
		}

		const weekdayStats: WeekdayPerformanceStat[] = weekdayBuckets.map((b) => ({
			weekdayIndex: b.weekdayIndex,
			weekdayLabel: b.weekdayLabel,
			loggedDays: b.loggedDays,
			successDays: b.successDays,
			goldDays: b.goldDays,
			successRate: b.loggedDays > 0 ? Math.round((b.successDays / b.loggedDays) * 100) : 0,
			avgScore: b.loggedDays > 0 ? Math.round(b.scoreSum / b.loggedDays) : 0
		}));

		const activeWeekdays = weekdayStats.filter((w) => w.loggedDays > 0);
		const bestWeekday =
			activeWeekdays.length > 0
				? [...activeWeekdays].sort((a, b) => b.avgScore - a.avgScore)[0]
				: null;
		const weakestWeekday =
			activeWeekdays.length > 1
				? [...activeWeekdays].sort((a, b) => a.avgScore - b.avgScore)[0]
				: null;

		const tagMap = new Map<
			string,
			{ occurrences: number; scoreSum: number; successCount: number }
		>();
		for (const pt of dailySeries) {
			if (pt.score === null) continue;
			for (const tag of pt.dayTags) {
				const cur = tagMap.get(tag) ?? { occurrences: 0, scoreSum: 0, successCount: 0 };
				cur.occurrences++;
				cur.scoreSum += pt.score;
				if (pt.score > 0) cur.successCount++;
				tagMap.set(tag, cur);
			}
		}

		const tagCorrelations: TagCorrelationStat[] = Array.from(tagMap.entries())
			.map(([tag, stat]) => {
				const avgScoreWithTag = Math.round(stat.scoreSum / stat.occurrences);
				const successRateWithTag = Math.round(
					(stat.successCount / stat.occurrences) * 100
				);
				return {
					tag,
					occurrences: stat.occurrences,
					avgScoreWithTag,
					successRateWithTag,
					impactDelta: avgScoreWithTag - avgQualityScore
				};
			})
			.sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.impactDelta) - Math.abs(a.impactDelta));

		selectedHabitReport = {
			habit: targetHabit,
			parentTitle: parent?.title ?? null,
			isComposite: children.length > 0,
			childrenCount: children.length,
			maxSupportedTier: getMaxSupportedTier(targetHabit),
			dailySeries,
			loggedDaysCount,
			successDaysCount,
			goldCount,
			silverCount,
			bronzeCount,
			failedCount,
			skippedCount,
			consistencyRate,
			goldRate,
			avgQualityScore,
			trendDelta,
			currentStreak,
			bestStreak,
			totalActualMinutes: Number(totalActualMinutes.toFixed(1)),
			totalPlannedMinutes: Number(totalPlannedMinutes.toFixed(1)),
			avgActualMinutes:
				cadenceLoggedDays > 0
					? Number((totalActualMinutes / cadenceLoggedDays).toFixed(1))
					: 0,
			avgPlannedMinutes:
				cadenceLoggedDays > 0
					? Number((totalPlannedMinutes / cadenceLoggedDays).toFixed(1))
					: targetHabit.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
			weekdayStats,
			bestWeekday,
			weakestWeekday,
			tagCorrelations,
			evolutionTimeline
		};
	}

	return {
		range,
		startDateIso: startIso,
		endDateIso: endIso,
		totalDaysInWindow: dateList.length,
		selectableHabits,
		macroStats: {
			totalLoggedDays,
			optimalDaysCount,
			partialDaysCount,
			failedDaysCount,
			overallConsistencyRate,
			totalLifecycleChanges: eventsInWindow.length
		},
		selectedHabitReport,
		comparisonRows
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: src/core/calendar-engine.ts

import {
	buildJalaliMonthGrid,
	formatJalaliHuman,
	getDaysUntilIso,
	isoToJalaliParts,
	jalaliPartsToIso,
	shiftIsoDate
} from '@domain/calendar';
import {
	parseTagsJson,
	type AnalyticsRawLog,
	type BookEntity,
	type CalendarDayAggregate,
	type CalendarEventEntity,
	type DayHabitPerformanceItem,
	type HabitEntity,
	type MediaEntity,
	type RawCalendarDayEntry,
	type UpcomingEventBannerItem
} from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	parseHabitSpecSnapshot,
	resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
} from './habit-engine';

export function computeUpcomingEventsBanner(params: {
	allEvents: CalendarEventEntity[];
	referenceDateIso: string;
	todayIso: string;
	horizonDays?: number;
}): {
	todayAndUpcoming: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
	selectedDayEvents: CalendarEventEntity[];
} {
	const { allEvents, referenceDateIso, todayIso, horizonDays = 7 } = params;

	const selectedParts = isoToJalaliParts(referenceDateIso);
	const selectedDayEvents = allEvents.filter((ev) => {
		if (ev.isAnnual) {
			return ev.jalaliMonth === selectedParts.jm && ev.jalaliDay === selectedParts.jd;
		}
		return ev.dateIso === referenceDateIso;
	});

	const windowIsos = new Map<string, { jm: number; jd: number; diff: number }>();
	for (let offset = 0; offset <= horizonDays; offset++) {
		const iso = shiftIsoDate(todayIso, offset);
		const p = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
		windowIsos.set(iso, { jm: p.jm, jd: p.jd, diff: offset });
	}

	const upcoming: UpcomingEventBannerItem[] = [];

	for (const ev of allEvents) {
		if (!ev.isAnnual && ev.isCompleted) continue;

		if (ev.isAnnual) {
			for (const [iso, meta] of windowIsos.entries()) {
				if (meta.jm === ev.jalaliMonth && meta.jd === ev.jalaliDay) {
					const human = formatJalaliHuman(iso, todayIso);
					upcoming.push({
						id: ev.id,
						title: ev.title,
						eventType: ev.eventType,
						isAnnual: true,
						isCompleted: ev.isCompleted,
						occurrenceDateIso: iso,
						humanDateLabel: human.fullLabel,
						daysRemaining: meta.diff,
						notes: ev.notes
					});
					break;
				}
			}
		} else if (ev.dateIso) {
			const diff = getDaysUntilIso(todayIso, ev.dateIso);
			if (diff >= -1 && diff <= horizonDays) {
				const human = formatJalaliHuman(ev.dateIso, todayIso);
				upcoming.push({
					id: ev.id,
					title: ev.title,
					eventType: ev.eventType,
					isAnnual: false,
					isCompleted: ev.isCompleted,
					occurrenceDateIso: ev.dateIso,
					humanDateLabel: human.fullLabel,
					daysRemaining: diff,
					notes: ev.notes
				});
			}
		}
	}

	upcoming.sort((a, b) => a.daysRemaining - b.daysRemaining);

	return {
		todayAndUpcoming: upcoming,
		selectedDayEvents
	};
}

export function assembleJalaliMonthArchive(params: {
	jy: number;
	jm: number;
	todayIso: string;
	selectedIso?: string;
	allHabits: HabitEntity[];
	entriesInMonth: RawCalendarDayEntry[];
	logsInMonth: AnalyticsRawLog[];
	eventsInMonth: CalendarEventEntity[];
	booksInMonth: BookEntity[];
	mediaInMonth: MediaEntity[];
	upcomingEvents: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
}) {
	const {
		jy,
		jm,
		todayIso,
		selectedIso,
		allHabits,
		entriesInMonth,
		logsInMonth,
		eventsInMonth,
		booksInMonth,
		mediaInMonth,
		upcomingEvents
	} = params;

	const grid = buildJalaliMonthGrid(jy, jm, todayIso);
	const startIso = grid.days[0]?.iso ?? todayIso;

	const habitMap = new Map(allHabits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));
	const parentHabitIds = new Set<string>();
	for (const h of allHabits) {
		if (h.parentId) parentHabitIds.add(h.parentId);
	}

	const entryByIso = new Map(entriesInMonth.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e]));
	const logsByIso = new Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog[]>();
	for (const log of logsInMonth) {
		const list = logsByIso.get(log.dateIso) ?? [];
		list.push(log);
		logsByIso.set(log.dateIso, list);
	}

	const daysAggregate: CalendarDayAggregate[] = grid.days.map((cell) => {
		const rawEntry = entryByIso.get(cell.iso);
		const rawLogs = logsByIso.get(cell.iso) ?? [];
		const human = formatJalaliHuman(cell.iso, todayIso);

		const dayEvents = eventsInMonth.filter((ev) => {
			if (ev.isAnnual) return ev.jalaliMonth === cell.jm && ev.jalaliDay === cell.jd;
			return ev.dateIso === cell.iso;
		});

		const booksActivity: CalendarDayAggregate['booksActivity'] = [];
		for (const b of booksInMonth) {
			if (b.finishedAtIso === cell.iso) {
				booksActivity.push({ book: b, activityKind: 'finished' });
			} else if (b.startedAtIso === cell.iso) {
				booksActivity.push({ book: b, activityKind: 'started' });
			}
		}

		const mediaActivity: CalendarDayAggregate['mediaActivity'] = [];
		for (const m of mediaInMonth) {
			if (m.finishedAtIso === cell.iso) {
				mediaActivity.push({ media: m, activityKind: 'finished' });
			} else if (m.startedAtIso === cell.iso) {
				mediaActivity.push({ media: m, activityKind: 'started' });
			}
		}

		const habitPerformances: DayHabitPerformanceItem[] = [];
		for (const log of rawLogs) {
			const h = habitMap.get(log.habitId);
			if (h && parentHabitIds.has(h.id)) continue;

			const trackingMode = h?.trackingMode ?? 'tiered';
			if (
				trackingMode === 'tiered' &&
				(log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined) &&
				h?.status === 'archived'
			) {
				continue;
			}

			const title = log.habitTitleSnapshot || h?.title || 'طرح حذف‌شده';
			const achievedRule =
				log.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				(h ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(h, log.achievedTier) : null);

			const specSnapshot = parseHabitSpecSnapshot(log.habitSnapshotJson, h, title);

			habitPerformances.push({
				habitId: log.habitId,
				title,
				trackingMode,
				achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
				achievedRule,
				actualMinutes: resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log),
				completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals ?? null,
				plannedMinutes:
					log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? h?.defaultMinutesPerInterval ?? null,
				isArchived: h ? h.status === 'archived' : true,
				specSnapshot
			});
		}

		return {
			iso: cell.iso,
			jy: cell.jy,
			jm: cell.jm,
			jd: cell.jd,
			weekdayLabel: cell.weekdayLabel,
			fullHumanLabel: human.fullLabel,
			isToday: cell.isToday,
			entry: rawEntry
				? {
						dayStatus: rawEntry.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: rawEntry.reflectionNote,
						journalText: rawEntry.journalText ?? null,
						tags: parseTagsJson(rawEntry.tagsJson)
					}
				: null,
			habitPerformances,
			events: dayEvents,
			booksActivity,
			mediaActivity
		};
	});

	const defaultSelectedIso =
		selectedIso && daysAggregate.some((d) => d.iso === selectedIso)
			? selectedIso
			: daysAggregate.some((d) => d.isToday)
				? todayIso
				: (jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd: 1 }) ?? startIso);

	const monthStats = {
		optimalDays: daysAggregate.filter((d) => d.entry?.dayStatus === 'optimal').length,
		partialDays: daysAggregate.filter((d) => d.entry?.dayStatus === 'partial').length,
		failedDays: daysAggregate.filter((d) => d.entry?.dayStatus === 'failed').length,
		totalEvents: eventsInMonth.length,
		finishedBooksCount: daysAggregate.reduce(
			(acc, d) => acc + d.booksActivity.filter((b) => b.activityKind === 'finished').length,
			0
		),
		finishedMediaCount: daysAggregate.reduce(
			(acc, d) => acc + d.mediaActivity.filter((m) => m.activityKind === 'finished').length,
			0
		)
	};

	return {
		year: grid.year,
		month: grid.month,
		monthName: grid.monthName,
		leadingEmptyCells: grid.leadingEmptyCells,
		selectedIso: defaultSelectedIso,
		days: daysAggregate,
		monthStats,
		upcomingEvents
	};
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/core/chart-math.ts
================================================================

import type { HabitDailyPoint } from '@domain/schemas';

export const CHART_DIMENSIONS = {
	SVG_WIDTH: 860,
	SVG_HEIGHT: 250,
	PAD_TOP: 26,
	PAD_BOTTOM: 34,
	PAD_LEFT: 36,
	PAD_RIGHT: 44,
	get PLOT_W() {
		return this.SVG_WIDTH - this.PAD_LEFT - this.PAD_RIGHT;
	},
	get PLOT_H() {
		return this.SVG_HEIGHT - this.PAD_TOP - this.PAD_BOTTOM;
	}
} as const;

export function computeMaxCadenceMinutes(
	dailySeries: ReadonlyArray<HabitDailyPoint>,
	defaultMinutes: number
): number {
	let maxVal = defaultMinutes || 15;
	for (const pt of dailySeries) {
		if ((pt.actualMinutes ?? 0) > maxVal) maxVal = pt.actualMinutes ?? 0;
		if ((pt.plannedMinutes ?? 0) > maxVal) maxVal = pt.plannedMinutes ?? 0;
	}
	return Math.max(10, Math.ceil(maxVal / 5) * 5);
}

export function getChartX(index: number, total: number): number {
	const { PAD_LEFT, PLOT_W } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	if (total <= 1) return PAD_LEFT + PLOT_W / 2;
	const step = PLOT_W / (total - 1);
	return PAD_LEFT + index * step;
}

export function getChartYFromScore(score: number): number {
	const { PAD_TOP, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
	return PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - (clamped / 100) * PLOT_H;
}

export function getChartYFromMinutes(minutes: number, maxCadenceMinutes: number): number {
	const { PAD_TOP, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxCadenceMinutes, minutes));
	return PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - (clamped / maxCadenceMinutes) * PLOT_H;
}

export function buildRollingPolylinePoints(
	series: ReadonlyArray<HabitDailyPoint>,
	isCadence: boolean,
	maxCadenceMinutes: number
): string {
	const pts: string[] = [];
	for (let i = 0; i < series.length; i++) {
		const pt = series[i];
		const x = getChartX(i, series.length);
		if (isCadence) {
			if (pt.plannedMinutes !== null && pt.plannedMinutes > 0) {
				pts.push(
					`${x.toFixed(1)},${getChartYFromMinutes(pt.plannedMinutes, maxCadenceMinutes).toFixed(1)}`
				);
			}
		} else if (pt.rollingAvgScore !== null) {
			pts.push(`${x.toFixed(1)},${getChartYFromScore(pt.rollingAvgScore).toFixed(1)}`);
		}
	}
	return pts.join(' ');
}

export function buildRollingAreaPath(
	series: ReadonlyArray<HabitDailyPoint>,
	isCadence: boolean,
	maxCadenceMinutes: number
): string {
	const { PAD_TOP, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	const coords: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
	for (let i = 0; i < series.length; i++) {
		const pt = series[i];
		const x = getChartX(i, series.length);
		if (!isCadence && pt.rollingAvgScore !== null) {
			coords.push({ x, y: getChartYFromScore(pt.rollingAvgScore) });
		} else if (isCadence && pt.actualMinutes !== null) {
			coords.push({ x, y: getChartYFromMinutes(pt.actualMinutes, maxCadenceMinutes) });
		}
	}
	if (coords.length < 2) return '';
	const baselineY = PAD_TOP + PLOT_H;
	const first = coords[0];
	const last = coords[coords.length - 1];
	const lineSegments = coords.map((c) => `L ${c.x.toFixed(1)} ${c.y.toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
	return `M ${first.x.toFixed(1)} ${baselineY} ${lineSegments} L ${last.x.toFixed(1)} ${baselineY} Z`;
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/seed-runner.ts
================================================================

import type Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {
	getDaysUntilIso,
	isoToJalaliSlash,
	jalaliSlashToIso,
	shiftIsoDate
} from '@domain/calendar';
import { TIER_SCORES } from '@domain/constants';
import type { HabitEntity, HabitLogItemInput } from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	evaluateDayStatusColor,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot,
	serializeHabitSpecSnapshot
} from '@core/habit-engine';
import {
	SEED_BOOKS_ORDER,
	SEED_DAILY_ROWS,
	SEED_HABITS,
	SEED_REFLECTION_TAGS,
	type RawDailySheetRow
} from './seed-data';

function requireIsoFromJalali(jalaliSlash: string): string {
	const iso = jalaliSlashToIso(jalaliSlash);
	if (!iso) {
		throw new Error(`Invalid Jalali date encountered during seed: ${jalaliSlash}`);
	}
	return iso;
}

function mapRawCellToTier(raw: number | '-' | undefined): number | null {
	if (raw === undefined) return null;
	if (raw === '-') return TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED;
	return raw;
}

export function seedDatabaseWithInitialData(
	client: Database.Database,
	options: { wipeExisting: boolean }
): void {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	if (options.wipeExisting) {
		client.exec(`
			DELETE FROM habit_logs;
			DELETE FROM daily_entries;
			DELETE FROM cadence_plans;
			DELETE FROM habit_lifecycle_events;
			DELETE FROM reflection_tags;
			DELETE FROM habits;
			DELETE FROM books;
		`);
	}

	const habitEntities: HabitEntity[] = SEED_HABITS.map((def) => {
		const createdAtIso = requireIsoFromJalali(def.createdAtJalali);
		return {
			id: def.id,
			parentId: def.parentId,
			title: def.title,
			category: def.category,
			trackingMode: def.trackingMode,
			timeSlot: def.timeSlot,
			isSituational: def.isSituational,
			status: 'active',
			scheduleLabel: def.scheduleLabel,
			notes: def.notes,
			bronzeDesc: def.bronzeDesc,
			silverDesc: def.silverDesc,
			goldDesc: def.goldDesc,
			emergencyMinDesc: def.emergencyMinDesc,
			defaultIntervals: 1,
			defaultMinutesPerInterval: 0,
			cadenceWindowMinutes: 60,
			sortOrder: def.sortOrder,
			createdAtIso,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		};
	});

	const habitById = new Map(habitEntities.map((h) => [h.id, h]));

	const insertHabitStmt = client.prepare(`
		INSERT OR IGNORE INTO habits (
			id, parent_id, title, category, tracking_mode, time_slot, is_situational, status,
			schedule_label, notes, bronze_desc, silver_desc, gold_desc,
			emergency_min_desc, default_intervals, default_minutes_per_interval,
			cadence_window_minutes, sort_order, created_at_iso, updated_at_iso
		) VALUES (
			@id, @parentId, @title, @category, @trackingMode, @timeSlot, @isSituational, @status,
			@scheduleLabel, @notes, @bronzeDesc, @silverDesc, @goldDesc,
			@emergencyMinDesc, @defaultIntervals, @defaultMinutesPerInterval,
			@cadenceWindowMinutes, @sortOrder, @createdAtIso, @updatedAtIso
		)
	`);

	const insertLifecycleStmt = client.prepare(`
		INSERT OR IGNORE INTO habit_lifecycle_events (
			id, habit_id, habit_title_snapshot, event_type,
			effective_date_iso, summary, reason, previous_state_json,
			new_state_json, created_at_iso
		) VALUES (
			@id, @habitId, @habitTitleSnapshot, @eventType,
			@effectiveDateIso, @summary, @reason, @previousStateJson,
			@newStateJson, @createdAtIso
		)
	`);

	const insertTagStmt = client.prepare(`
		INSERT OR IGNORE INTO reflection_tags (id, label, sort_order)
		VALUES (@id, @label, @sortOrder)
	`);

	const insertEntryStmt = client.prepare(`
		INSERT OR IGNORE INTO daily_entries (
			id, date_iso, day_status, reflection_note, journal_text, tags_json, updated_at_iso
		) VALUES (
			@id, @dateIso, @dayStatus, @reflectionNote, @journalText, @tagsJson, @updatedAtIso
		)
	`);

	const insertLogStmt = client.prepare(`
		INSERT OR IGNORE INTO habit_logs (
			id, date_iso, habit_id, habit_title_snapshot, achieved_tier,
			achieved_rule_snapshot, habit_snapshot_json, completed_intervals,
			actual_minutes_per_interval, total_actual_minutes,
			planned_intervals_snapshot, planned_minutes_snapshot, updated_at_iso
		) VALUES (
			@id, @dateIso, @habitId, @habitTitleSnapshot, @achievedTier,
			@achievedRuleSnapshot, @habitSnapshotJson, @completedIntervals,
			@actualMinutesPerInterval, @totalActualMinutes,
			@plannedIntervalsSnapshot, @plannedMinutesSnapshot, @updatedAtIso
		)
	`);

	const insertBookStmt = client.prepare(`
		INSERT OR IGNORE INTO books (
			id, title, author, status, started_at_iso, finished_at_iso,
			rating, notes, created_at_iso, updated_at_iso
		) VALUES (
			@id, @title, @author, @status, @startedAtIso, @finishedAtIso,
			@rating, @notes, @createdAtIso, @updatedAtIso
		)
	`);

	function buildLogForHabitOnDay(
		habitId: string,
		dateIso: string,
		rawValue: number | '-' | undefined
	): HabitLogItemInput | null {
		const achievedTier = mapRawCellToTier(rawValue);
		if (achievedTier === null) return null;

		const habit = habitById.get(habitId);
		if (!habit) return null;

		return {
			habitId: habit.id,
			habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
			achievedTier,
			achievedRuleSnapshot: resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, achievedTier),
			completedIntervals: null,
			actualMinutesPerInterval: null,
			totalActualMinutes: null,
			plannedIntervalsSnapshot: null,
			plannedMinutesSnapshot: null,
			updatedAtIso: `${dateIso}T20:00:00.000Z`
		};
	}

	function extractDayLogs(row: RawDailySheetRow, dateIso: string): HabitLogItemInput[] {
		const logs: HabitLogItemInput[] = [];

		const pushIfPresent = (habitId: string, val: number | '-' | undefined) => {
			const item = buildLogForHabitOnDay(habitId, dateIso, val);
			if (item) logs.push(item);
		};

		pushIfPresent('habit-morning-worship', row.scores.morningWorship);
		pushIfPresent('habit-toothbrushing', row.scores.toothbrushing);

		if (row.scores.prayersGroup !== undefined) {
			pushIfPresent('habit-prayers-group', row.scores.prayersGroup);
			pushIfPresent('habit-sub-quran', row.scores.prayersGroup);
			pushIfPresent('habit-sub-nahj', row.scores.prayersGroup);
			pushIfPresent('habit-sub-sahifa', row.scores.prayersGroup);
		}

		pushIfPresent('habit-book', row.scores.bookReading);
		pushIfPresent('habit-study', row.scores.studying);
		pushIfPresent('habit-abstinence', row.scores.abstinence);
		pushIfPresent('habit-work-life', row.scores.workLife);
		pushIfPresent('habit-language', row.scores.languageLearning);

		return logs;
	}

	const firstSheetDateIso = requireIsoFromJalali(SEED_DAILY_ROWS[0].jalaliDate);
	const lastSheetDateIso = requireIsoFromJalali(
		SEED_DAILY_ROWS[SEED_DAILY_ROWS.length - 1].jalaliDate
	);
	const totalDaysSpan = Math.max(1, getDaysUntilIso(firstSheetDateIso, lastSheetDateIso));

	const runTx = client.transaction(() => {
		for (const h of habitEntities) {
			insertHabitStmt.run({
				...h,
				isSituational: h.isSituational ? 1 : 0
			});

			insertLifecycleStmt.run({
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				habitId: h.id,
				habitTitleSnapshot: h.title,
				eventType: h.parentId ? 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT' : 'CREATED',
				effectiveDateIso: h.createdAtIso,
				summary: h.parentId
					? `ثبت زیرطرح «${h.title}» در مجموعه ادعیه (${isoToJalaliSlash(h.createdAtIso)})`
					: `شروع ردیابی طرح «${h.title}» از تاریخ ${isoToJalaliSlash(h.createdAtIso)}`,
				reason: 'ثبت در جدول اصلی ردیابی طرح‌ها',
				previousStateJson: null,
				newStateJson: JSON.stringify(h),
				createdAtIso: `${h.createdAtIso}T08:00:00.000Z`
			});
		}

		SEED_REFLECTION_TAGS.forEach((label, idx) => {
			insertTagStmt.run({
				id: `tag-${idx + 1}`,
				label,
				sortOrder: idx + 1
			});
		});

		for (const row of SEED_DAILY_ROWS) {
			const dateIso = requireIsoFromJalali(row.jalaliDate);
			const dayLogs = extractDayLogs(row, dateIso);

			const applicableHabitsOnDate = habitEntities.filter(
				(h) => h.createdAtIso <= dateIso
			);
			const computedDayStatus = evaluateDayStatusColor(applicableHabitsOnDate, dayLogs);
			const entryUpdatedAtIso = `${dateIso}T21:00:00.000Z`;

			insertEntryStmt.run({
				id: `entry:${dateIso}`,
				dateIso,
				dayStatus: computedDayStatus,
				reflectionNote: '',
				journalText: null,
				tagsJson: '[]',
				updatedAtIso: entryUpdatedAtIso
			});

			for (const log of dayLogs) {
				const habit = habitById.get(log.habitId);
				if (!habit) continue;

				insertLogStmt.run({
					id: `log:${dateIso}:${log.habitId}`,
					dateIso,
					habitId: log.habitId,
					habitTitleSnapshot: log.habitTitleSnapshot,
					achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
					achievedRuleSnapshot: log.achievedRuleSnapshot,
					habitSnapshotJson: serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit),
					completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval,
					totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes,
					plannedIntervalsSnapshot: log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
					plannedMinutesSnapshot: log.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
					updatedAtIso: log.updatedAtIso
				});
			}
		}

		const totalBooks = SEED_BOOKS_ORDER.length;
		const intervalDays = Math.max(5, Math.floor(totalDaysSpan / totalBooks));

		SEED_BOOKS_ORDER.forEach((bookItem, idx) => {
			const startOffset = idx * intervalDays;
			const finishOffset = Math.min(totalDaysSpan, startOffset + intervalDays - 1);

			const startedAtIso = shiftIsoDate(firstSheetDateIso, startOffset);
			const finishedAtIso = shiftIsoDate(firstSheetDateIso, finishOffset);
			const createdAtIso = `${finishedAtIso}T18:30:00.000Z`;

			insertBookStmt.run({
				id: `book-1405-${String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
				title: bookItem.title,
				author: bookItem.author,
				status: 'completed',
				startedAtIso,
				finishedAtIso,
				rating: 5,
				notes: bookItem.notes,
				createdAtIso,
				updatedAtIso: createdAtIso
			});
		});
	});

	runTx();
}

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/seed-data.ts

import type { HabitTimeSlot, TrackingMode } from '@domain/schemas';

export interface SeedHabitDefinition {
	id: string;
	parentId: string | null;
	title: string;
	category: string;
	trackingMode: TrackingMode;
	timeSlot: HabitTimeSlot;
	isSituational: boolean;
	scheduleLabel: string | null;
	notes: string | null;
	bronzeDesc: string | null;
	silverDesc: string | null;
	goldDesc: string | null;
	emergencyMinDesc: string | null;
	sortOrder: number;
	createdAtJalali: string;
}

export interface RawDailySheetRow {
	jalaliDate: string;
	scores: {
		morningWorship?: number | '-';
		toothbrushing?: number | '-';
		prayersGroup?: number | '-';
		bookReading?: number | '-';
		studying?: number | '-';
		abstinence?: number | '-';
		workLife?: number | '-';
		languageLearning?: number | '-';
	};
}

export const SEED_BOOKS_ORDER: ReadonlyArray<{
	title: string;
	author: string | null;
	notes: string | null;
}> = [
	{ title: 'چگونه کمالگرا نباشیم؟', author: 'استیون گایز', notes: 'معتبر از تاریخ ۱۳ اردیبهشت سال ۱۴۰۵' },
	{ title: 'عادت های اتمی', author: 'جیمز کلیر', notes: null },
	{ title: 'انسان در قران', author: 'مرتضی مطهری', notes: null },
	{ title: 'تندتر از عقربه ها حرکت کن', author: null, notes: null },
	{ title: '1984', author: 'جورج اورول', notes: null },
	{ title: 'تشیع علوی تشیع صفوی', author: 'علی شریعتی', notes: null },
	{ title: 'صحیفه ی سجادیه', author: 'امام سجاد (ع)', notes: 'نوبت اول مطالعه در سال ۱۴۰۵' },
	{ title: 'فاطمه فاطمه است', author: 'علی شریعتی', notes: null },
	{ title: 'چگونه ایده های خوب را بدزدیم', author: 'آستین کلئون', notes: null },
	{ title: 'مومن متعادل', author: null, notes: null },
	{ title: 'اقتصاد به زبان ساده', author: null, notes: null },

PATH: src/lib/server/db/index.ts

import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/better-sqlite3';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import * as schema from './schema';
import { seedDatabaseWithInitialData } from './seed-runner';
import { DATABASE_URL } from '$app/env/private';

if (!DATABASE_URL) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is not set');

const client = new Database(DATABASE_URL);
client.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
client.pragma('synchronous = NORMAL');
client.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
client.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');

function ensureProductionSchema( sqlite: Database.Database ): void {
	sqlite.exec(`
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habits (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			parent_id TEXT,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'عمومی',
			tracking_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'tiered',
			time_slot TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'day',
			is_situational INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
			status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
			schedule_label TEXT,
			notes TEXT,
			bronze_desc TEXT,
			silver_desc TEXT,
			gold_desc TEXT,
			emergency_min_desc TEXT,
			default_intervals INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
			default_minutes_per_interval REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
			cadence_window_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
			sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cadence_plans (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			date_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			habit_id TEXT NOT NULL,
			planned_intervals INTEGER NOT NULL,
			planned_minutes_per_interval REAL NOT NULL,
			cadence_window_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_entries (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			date_iso TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
			day_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'optimal',
			reflection_note TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
			journal_text TEXT,
			tags_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
			updated_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habit_logs (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			date_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			habit_id TEXT NOT NULL,
			habit_title_snapshot TEXT,
			achieved_tier INTEGER,
			achieved_rule_snapshot TEXT,
			habit_snapshot_json TEXT,
			completed_intervals INTEGER,
			actual_minutes_per_interval REAL,
			total_actual_minutes REAL,
			planned_intervals_snapshot INTEGER,
			planned_minutes_snapshot REAL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habit_lifecycle_events (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			habit_id TEXT,
			habit_title_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
			event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
			effective_date_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			summary TEXT NOT NULL,
			reason TEXT,
			previous_state_json TEXT,
			new_state_json TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reflection_tags (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			label TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
			sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS books (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			author TEXT,
			status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'completed',
			started_at_iso TEXT,
			finished_at_iso TEXT,
			rating INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
			notes TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media_logs (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'movie',
			status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'completed',
			started_at_iso TEXT,
			finished_at_iso TEXT,
			rating INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
			notes TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS calendar_events (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			event_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'occasion',
			is_annual INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
			date_iso TEXT,
			jalali_month INTEGER NOT NULL,
			jalali_day INTEGER NOT NULL,
			is_completed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
			notes TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notes (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
			content TEXT NOT NULL,
			category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'عمومی',
			is_pinned INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_auth (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
			password_salt TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_sessions (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			expires_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			last_seen_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);
	`);

	const habitColumns = sqlite.pragma('table_info(habits)') as Array<{ name: string }>;
	if (!habitColumns.some((col) => col.name === 'time_slot')) {
		sqlite.exec("ALTER TABLE habits ADD COLUMN time_slot TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'day';");
	}

	sqlite.exec(`
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habits_parent_id ON habits(parent_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habits_updated_at ON habits(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_plans_date_habit ON cadence_plans(date_iso, habit_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_plans_updated_at ON cadence_plans(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_entries_updated_at ON daily_entries(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habit_logs_date_iso ON habit_logs(date_iso);
		CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habit_logs_date_habit ON habit_logs(date_iso, habit_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habit_logs_updated_at ON habit_logs(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_lifecycle_events_habit_id ON habit_lifecycle_events(habit_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_books_updated_at ON books(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_books_dates ON books(started_at_iso, finished_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_logs_updated_at ON media_logs(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_logs_dates ON media_logs(started_at_iso, finished_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_calendar_events_updated_at ON calendar_events(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_calendar_events_date ON calendar_events(date_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_calendar_events_jalali ON calendar_events(jalali_month, jalali_day);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_notes_updated_at ON notes(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_auth_sessions_expires_at ON auth_sessions(expires_at_iso);
	`);
}

ensureProductionSchema(client);

if (DATABASE_URL !== '/tmp/build.db') {
	const row = client
		.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM habits')
		.get() as { count: number } | undefined;
	if (!row || row.count === 0) {
		seedDatabaseWithInitialData(client, { wipeExisting: false });
	}
}

process.once('SIGTERM', () => {
	try {
		client.pragma('wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)');
		client.close();
	} catch {
		// ignore close errors during shutdown
	}
});

export const db = drizzle(client, { schema });
