================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/auth.repo.ts
================================================================

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import util from 'node:util';
import { db } from '../index';
import { appAuth, authSessions } from '../schema';
import { eq, lt } from 'drizzle-orm';

const scryptAsync = util.promisify(crypto.scrypt);

const AUTH_SINGLETON_ID = 'singleton';
const SESSION_DURATION_DAYS = 365;
const SESSION_DURATION_MS = SESSION_DURATION_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export const SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = 'newlife_session';
export const SESSION_MAX_AGE_SECONDS = SESSION_DURATION_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60;

export function getSessionCookieOptions(isSecureCookie: boolean) {
	return {
		path: '/',
		httpOnly: true,
		sameSite: 'lax' as const,
		secure: isSecureCookie,
		maxAge: SESSION_MAX_AGE_SECONDS
	};
}

async function hashPasswordWithSalt(password: string, saltHex: string): Promise<string> {
	const derivedKey = (await scryptAsync(password, Buffer.from(saltHex, 'hex'), 64)) as Buffer;
	return derivedKey.toString('hex');
}

function hashSessionToken(rawToken: string): string {
	return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(rawToken).digest('hex');
}

export function isPasswordConfigured(): boolean {
	const record = db
		.select()
		.from(appAuth)
		.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID))
		.get();
	return Boolean(record);
}

export async function setupInitialPassword(password: string): Promise<void> {
	if (isPasswordConfigured()) {
		throw new Error('رمز عبور قبلاً تنظیم شده است');
	}
	const saltHex = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
	const passwordHash = await hashPasswordWithSalt(password, saltHex);
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	db.insert(appAuth)
		.values({
			id: AUTH_SINGLETON_ID,
			passwordHash,
			passwordSalt: saltHex,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.run();
}

export async function verifyPassword(password: string): Promise<boolean> {
	const record = db
		.select()
		.from(appAuth)
		.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID))
		.get();

	if (!record) return false;

	const candidateHash = await hashPasswordWithSalt(password, record.passwordSalt);
	const expectedBuf = Buffer.from(record.passwordHash, 'hex');
	const candidateBuf = Buffer.from(candidateHash, 'hex');

	if (expectedBuf.length !== candidateBuf.length) return false;
	return crypto.timingSafeEqual(expectedBuf, candidateBuf);
}

export async function changeMasterPassword(currentPassword: string, newPassword: string): Promise<boolean> {
	if (!(await verifyPassword(currentPassword))) {
		return false;
	}
	const saltHex = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
	const passwordHash = await hashPasswordWithSalt(newPassword, saltHex);
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	db.update(appAuth)
		.set({
			passwordHash,
			passwordSalt: saltHex,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID))
		.run();

	return true;
}

export function createAuthSession(): {
	token: string;
	expiresAt: Date;
} {
	const now = new Date();
	const nowIso = now.toISOString();
	const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_DURATION_MS);
	const expiresAtIso = expiresAt.toISOString();

	// Clean up expired sessions
	db.delete(authSessions).where(lt(authSessions.expiresAtIso, nowIso)).run();

	const rawToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
	const sessionId = hashSessionToken(rawToken);

	db.insert(authSessions)
		.values({
			id: sessionId,
			createdAtIso: nowIso,
			expiresAtIso,
			lastSeenAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.run();

	return { token: rawToken, expiresAt };
}

export function validateAndExtendSession(rawToken: string): {
	valid: boolean;
	shouldRefreshCookie: boolean;
	expiresAt: Date;
} {
	const now = new Date();
	const nowIso = now.toISOString();
	const sessionId = hashSessionToken(rawToken);

	const session = db
		.select()
		.from(authSessions)
		.where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId))
		.get();

	if (!session) {
		return { valid: false, shouldRefreshCookie: false, expiresAt: now };
	}

	if (session.expiresAtIso <= nowIso) {
		db.delete(authSessions).where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId)).run();
		return { valid: false, shouldRefreshCookie: false, expiresAt: now };
	}

	const newExpiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_DURATION_MS);
	const lastSeenMs = new Date(session.lastSeenAtIso).getTime();
	const hoursSinceLastSeen = (now.getTime() - lastSeenMs) / (1000 * 60 * 60);

	// Extend session in DB if more than 1 hour has passed since last update
	const shouldRefreshCookie = hoursSinceLastSeen >= 1;
	if (shouldRefreshCookie) {
		db.update(authSessions)
			.set({
				expiresAtIso: newExpiresAt.toISOString(),
				lastSeenAtIso: nowIso
			})
			.where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId))
			.run();
	}

	return {
		valid: true,
		shouldRefreshCookie,
		expiresAt: newExpiresAt
	};
}

export function revokeAuthSession(rawToken: string): void {
	const sessionId = hashSessionToken(rawToken);
	db.delete(authSessions).where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId)).run();
}

PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/calendar.repo.ts

import { db } from '../index';
import {
	books,
	calendarEvents,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	mediaLogs
} from '../schema';
import { and, desc, eq, gte, lte, or } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
	buildJalaliMonthGrid,
	getTodayIso,
	isoToJalaliParts
} from '@domain/calendar';
import {
	assembleJalaliMonthArchive,
	computeUpcomingEventsBanner
} from '@core/calendar-engine';
import type {
	CalendarEventEntity,
	CreateCalendarEventInput,
	UpcomingEventBannerItem
} from '@domain/schemas';

export function createCalendarEvent(input: CreateCalendarEventInput): CalendarEventEntity {
	const parts = isoToJalaliParts(input.dateIso);
	const isAnnual = Boolean(input.isAnnual);
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	const newEvent: CalendarEventEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		title: input.title.trim(),
		eventType: input.eventType,
		isAnnual,
		dateIso: isAnnual ? null : input.dateIso,
		jalaliMonth: parts.jm,
		jalaliDay: parts.jd,
		isCompleted: false,
		notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
		createdAtIso: nowIso,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	db.insert(calendarEvents).values(newEvent).run();
	return newEvent;
}

export function toggleCalendarEventCompletion(eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean): void {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	db.update(calendarEvents)
		.set({ isCompleted, updatedAtIso: nowIso })
		.where(eq(calendarEvents.id, eventId))
		.run();
}

export function deleteCalendarEvent(eventId: string): void {
	db.delete(calendarEvents).where(eq(calendarEvents.id, eventId)).run();
}

export function getUpcomingEventsBannerItems(
	referenceDateIso: string,
	horizonDays = 7
): {
	todayAndUpcoming: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
	selectedDayEvents: CalendarEventEntity[];
} {
	const todayIso = getTodayIso();
	const allEvents = db
		.select()
		.from(calendarEvents)
		.orderBy(desc(calendarEvents.createdAtIso))
		.all();

	return computeUpcomingEventsBanner({
		allEvents,
		referenceDateIso,
		todayIso,
		horizonDays
	});
}

export function getJalaliMonthFullArchive(jy: number, jm: number, selectedIso?: string) {
	const todayIso = getTodayIso();
	const grid = buildJalaliMonthGrid(jy, jm, todayIso);
	const startIso = grid.days[0]?.iso ?? todayIso;
	const endIso = grid.days[grid.days.length - 1]?.iso ?? todayIso;

	const allHabits = db.select().from(habits).orderBy(habits.sortOrder).all();

	const entriesInMonth = db
		.select()
		.from(dailyEntries)
		.where(and(gte(dailyEntries.dateIso, startIso), lte(dailyEntries.dateIso, endIso)))
		.all();

	const logsInMonth = db
		.select()
		.from(habitLogs)
		.where(and(gte(habitLogs.dateIso, startIso), lte(habitLogs.dateIso, endIso)))
		.all();

	const eventsInMonth = db
		.select()
		.from(calendarEvents)
		.where(
			or(
				and(eq(calendarEvents.isAnnual, true), eq(calendarEvents.jalaliMonth, jm)),
				and(
					eq(calendarEvents.isAnnual, false),
					gte(calendarEvents.dateIso, startIso),
					lte(calendarEvents.dateIso, endIso)
				)
			)
		)
		.all();

	const booksInMonth = db
		.select()
		.from(books)
		.where(
			or(
				and(gte(books.startedAtIso, startIso), lte(books.startedAtIso, endIso)),
				and(gte(books.finishedAtIso, startIso), lte(books.finishedAtIso, endIso))
			)
		)
		.all();

	const mediaInMonth = db
		.select()
		.from(mediaLogs)
		.where(
			or(
				and(gte(mediaLogs.startedAtIso, startIso), lte(mediaLogs.startedAtIso, endIso)),
				and(gte(mediaLogs.finishedAtIso, startIso), lte(mediaLogs.finishedAtIso, endIso))
			)
		)
		.all();

	const { todayAndUpcoming } = getUpcomingEventsBannerItems(todayIso, 14);

	return assembleJalaliMonthArchive({
		jy,
		jm,
		todayIso,
		selectedIso,
		allHabits,
		entriesInMonth,
		logsInMonth,
		eventsInMonth,
		booksInMonth,
		mediaInMonth,
		upcomingEvents: todayAndUpcoming
	});
}

import { db } from '../index';

import type { NoteEntity, UpsertNoteInput } from '@domain/schemas';

	return db
		.select()
		.from(notes)
		.orderBy(desc(notes.isPinned), desc(notes.updatedAtIso))
		.all();
}

export function createNoteRecord(input: UpsertNoteInput): NoteEntity {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const newNote: NoteEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		title: input.title.trim(),
		content: input.content.trim(),
		category: input.category.trim() || 'عمومی',
		isPinned: false,
		createdAtIso: nowIso,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	db.insert(notes).values(newNote).run();
	return newNote;
}

		.set({
			title: input.title.trim(),
			content: input.content.trim(),
			category: input.category.trim() || 'عمومی',
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.where(eq(notes.id, noteId))
		.run();
}

}

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/daily-tracker.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import {
	cadencePlans,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLifecycleEvents,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	reflectionTags
} from '../schema';
import { and, asc, desc, eq, isNotNull, ne } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getAllHabits } from './habits.repo';
import { getUpcomingEventsBannerItems } from './calendar.repo';
import {
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot,
	serializeHabitSpecSnapshot
} from '@core/habit-engine';
import { DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS } from '@domain/constants';
import {
	parseTagsJson,
	type DayCommandPayload,
	type HabitEntity,
	type JournalMemoryItem,
	type SaveCadencePlanBatch,
	type SaveDailyEntryInput
} from '@domain/schemas';

export function getJournalMemories(): JournalMemoryItem[] {
	const rows = db
		.select()
		.from(dailyEntries)
		.where(and(isNotNull(dailyEntries.journalText), ne(dailyEntries.journalText, '')))
		.orderBy(desc(dailyEntries.dateIso))
		.all();

	const memories: JournalMemoryItem[] = [];
	for (const row of rows) {
		const text = row.journalText?.trim();
		if (!text) continue;
		memories.push({
			id: row.id,
			dateIso: row.dateIso,
			dayStatus: row.dayStatus,
			journalText: text,
			tags: parseTagsJson(row.tagsJson),
			updatedAtIso: row.updatedAtIso
		});
	}
	return memories;
}

export function getReflectionTags(): string[] {
	const rows = db
		.select()
		.from(reflectionTags)
		.orderBy(asc(reflectionTags.sortOrder))
		.all();

	if (rows.length === 0) {
		return [...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS];
	}
	return rows.map((r) => r.label);
}

export function getDayCommandPayload(
	dateIso: string,
	tomorrowIso: string
): DayCommandPayload {
	const allHabits = getAllHabits();
	const availableTags = getReflectionTags();

	const entry = db
		.select()
		.from(dailyEntries)
		.where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, dateIso))
		.get();

	const logs = db
		.select()
		.from(habitLogs)
		.where(eq(habitLogs.dateIso, dateIso))
		.all();

	const dayCadencePlans = db
		.select()
		.from(cadencePlans)
		.where(eq(cadencePlans.dateIso, dateIso))
		.all();

	const tomorrowCadencePlans = db
		.select()
		.from(cadencePlans)
		.where(eq(cadencePlans.dateIso, tomorrowIso))
		.all();

	const recentEntries = db
		.select()
		.from(dailyEntries)
		.orderBy(desc(dailyEntries.dateIso))
		.limit(35)
		.all();

	const { todayAndUpcoming, selectedDayEvents } = getUpcomingEventsBannerItems(dateIso, 7);

	return {
		dateIso,
		tomorrowIso,
		habits: allHabits,
		availableTags,
		entry: entry
			? {
					id: entry.id,
					dateIso: entry.dateIso,
					dayStatus: entry.dayStatus,
					reflectionNote: entry.reflectionNote,
					journalText: entry.journalText ?? null,
					tags: parseTagsJson(entry.tagsJson),
					updatedAtIso: entry.updatedAtIso
				}
			: null,
		logs,
		dayCadencePlans,
		tomorrowCadencePlans,
		recentEntries: recentEntries.map((r) => ({
			dateIso: r.dateIso,
			dayStatus: r.dayStatus,
			reflectionNote: r.reflectionNote,
			tags: parseTagsJson(r.tagsJson)
		})),
		upcomingBannerItems: todayAndUpcoming,
		selectedDayEvents
	};
}

export function saveDailyEntryAndLogs(
	input: SaveDailyEntryInput,
	preloadedHabitMap?: ReadonlyMap<string, HabitEntity>
): void {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const entryUpdatedAtIso = input.updatedAtIso || nowIso;
	const normalizedJournal = input.journalText?.trim() ? input.journalText.trim() : null;
	const habitMap =
		preloadedHabitMap ?? new Map(getAllHabits().map((h) => [h.id, h]));

	db.transaction((tx) => {
		const existing = tx
			.select()
			.from(dailyEntries)
			.where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, input.dateIso))
			.get();

		if (existing) {
			if (!input.updatedAtIso || entryUpdatedAtIso >= existing.updatedAtIso) {
				tx.update(dailyEntries)
					.set({
						dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
						journalText: normalizedJournal,
						tagsJson: JSON.stringify(input.tags),
						updatedAtIso: entryUpdatedAtIso
					})
					.where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, input.dateIso))
					.run();
			}
		} else {
			tx.insert(dailyEntries)
				.values({
					id: `entry:${input.dateIso}`,
					dateIso: input.dateIso,
					dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
					reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
					journalText: normalizedJournal,
					tagsJson: JSON.stringify(input.tags),
					updatedAtIso: entryUpdatedAtIso
				})
				.run();
		}

		const isCompletelyEmpty =
			(!input.reflectionNote || input.reflectionNote.trim() === '') &&
			(!input.journalText || input.journalText.trim() === '') &&
			input.tags.length === 0 &&
			input.logs.every(
				(l) =>
					(l.achievedTier === null || l.achievedTier === undefined) &&
					(l.totalActualMinutes === null || l.totalActualMinutes === 0 || l.totalActualMinutes === undefined)
			);

		if (isCompletelyEmpty) {
			tx.delete(habitLogs).where(eq(habitLogs.dateIso, input.dateIso)).run();
			tx.delete(dailyEntries).where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, input.dateIso)).run();
			return;
		}

		const previousLogs = tx
			.select()
			.from(habitLogs)
			.where(eq(habitLogs.dateIso, input.dateIso))
			.all();
		const previousLogByHabitId = new Map(previousLogs.map((l) => [l.habitId, l]));

		for (const log of input.logs) {
			const habit = habitMap.get(log.habitId);
			const prev = previousLogByHabitId.get(log.habitId);

			const isUnloggedTiered =
				habit?.trackingMode === 'tiered' &&
				(log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined);

			if (
				!prev &&
				isUnloggedTiered &&
				habit &&
				(habit.status === 'archived' || habit.createdAtIso > input.dateIso)
			) {
				continue;
			}

			if (
				prev?.updatedAtIso &&
				log.updatedAtIso &&
				log.updatedAtIso < prev.updatedAtIso
			) {
				continue;
			}

			const logUpdatedAt = log.updatedAtIso || nowIso;
			const tierUnchanged = prev && prev.achievedTier === log.achievedTier;

			const habitTitleSnapshot =
				(tierUnchanged && prev?.habitTitleSnapshot) ||
				log.habitTitleSnapshot ||
				habit?.title ||
				prev?.habitTitleSnapshot ||
				'طرح';

			const achievedRuleSnapshot =
				(tierUnchanged && prev?.achievedRuleSnapshot) ||
				(habit ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, log.achievedTier) : null) ||
				log.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				prev?.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				null;

			const habitSnapshotJson =
				(tierUnchanged && prev?.habitSnapshotJson) ||
				(habit
					? serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit)
					: (prev?.habitSnapshotJson ?? null));

			if (prev) {
				tx.update(habitLogs)
					.set({
						habitTitleSnapshot,
						achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot,
						habitSnapshotJson,
						completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot:
							log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? prev.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot:
							log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? prev.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
						updatedAtIso: logUpdatedAt
					})
					.where(eq(habitLogs.id, prev.id))
					.run();
			} else {
				tx.insert(habitLogs)
					.values({
						id: `log:${input.dateIso}:${log.habitId}`,
						dateIso: input.dateIso,
						habitId: log.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot,
						achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot,
						habitSnapshotJson,
						completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
						updatedAtIso: logUpdatedAt
					})
					.run();
			}
		}
	});
}

export function saveCadencePlansForDate(input: SaveCadencePlanBatch): void {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const allHabits = getAllHabits();
	const habitMap = new Map(allHabits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));

	db.transaction((tx) => {
		for (const item of input.items) {
			const existing = tx
				.select()
				.from(cadencePlans)
				.where(
					and(
						eq(cadencePlans.dateIso, input.targetDateIso),
						eq(cadencePlans.habitId, item.habitId)
					)
				)
				.get();

			if (existing) {
				tx.update(cadencePlans)
					.set({
						plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					})
					.where(eq(cadencePlans.id, existing.id))
					.run();
			} else {
				tx.insert(cadencePlans)
					.values({
						id: `plan:${input.targetDateIso}:${item.habitId}`,
						dateIso: input.targetDateIso,
						habitId: item.habitId,
						plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					})
					.run();
			}

			const habit = habitMap.get(item.habitId);
			if (
				habit &&
				(habit.defaultIntervals !== item.plannedIntervals ||
					habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval !== item.plannedMinutesPerInterval ||
					habit.cadenceWindowMinutes !== item.cadenceWindowMinutes)
			) {
				tx.update(habits)
					.set({
						defaultIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						defaultMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes
					})
					.where(eq(habits.id, habit.id))
					.run();

				tx.insert(habitLifecycleEvents)
					.values({
						id: crypto.randomUUID(),
						habitId: habit.id,
						habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
						eventType: 'CADENCE_UPDATED',
						effectiveDateIso: input.targetDateIso,
						summary: `تنظیم برای روز بعد: در هر ${item.cadenceWindowMinutes} دقیقه، ${item.plannedMinutesPerInterval} دقیقه (${item.plannedIntervals} بازه)`,
						reason: input.reason || 'تنظیم از شب قبل',
						previousStateJson: JSON.stringify({
							intervals: habit.defaultIntervals,
							minutes: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
							window: habit.cadenceWindowMinutes
						}),
						newStateJson: JSON.stringify({
							intervals: item.plannedIntervals,
							minutes: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
							window: item.cadenceWindowMinutes
						}),
						createdAtIso: nowIso
					})
					.run();
			}
		}
	});
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/analytics.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import { dailyEntries, habitLifecycleEvents, habitLogs, habits } from '../schema';
import { asc, desc, gte } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getYesterdayIso, shiftIsoDate } from '@domain/calendar';
import {
	parseTagsJson,
	type AnalyticsRawEntry,
	type AnalyticsRawLog,
	type HabitEntity,
	type LifecycleEventEntity
} from '@domain/schemas';

export function getAnalyticsDatasetPayload(): {
	habits: HabitEntity[];
	entries: AnalyticsRawEntry[];
	logs: AnalyticsRawLog[];
	lifecycleEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[];
	fallbackEndDateIso: string;
} {
	const fallbackEndDateIso = getYesterdayIso();
	const allHabits = db.select().from(habits).orderBy(asc(habits.sortOrder)).all();

	const defaultHorizonIso = shiftIsoDate(fallbackEndDateIso, -180);
	const earliestHabitDateIso = allHabits.reduce(
		(min, h) => (h.createdAtIso && h.createdAtIso < min ? h.createdAtIso : min),
		defaultHorizonIso
	);
	const horizonStartIso =
		earliestHabitDateIso < defaultHorizonIso ? earliestHabitDateIso : defaultHorizonIso;

	const rawEntries = db
		.select()
		.from(dailyEntries)
		.where(gte(dailyEntries.dateIso, horizonStartIso))
		.orderBy(asc(dailyEntries.dateIso))
		.all();

	const entries: AnalyticsRawEntry[] = rawEntries.map((r) => ({
		dateIso: r.dateIso,
		dayStatus: r.dayStatus,
		reflectionNote: r.reflectionNote,
		tags: parseTagsJson(r.tagsJson)
	}));

	const logs: AnalyticsRawLog[] = db
		.select()
		.from(habitLogs)
		.where(gte(habitLogs.dateIso, horizonStartIso))
		.orderBy(asc(habitLogs.dateIso))
		.all();

	const lifecycleEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[] = db
		.select()
		.from(habitLifecycleEvents)
		.orderBy(desc(habitLifecycleEvents.createdAtIso))
		.all();

	return {
		habits: allHabits,
		entries,
		logs,
		lifecycleEvents,
		fallbackEndDateIso
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/backup.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import {
	books,
	cadencePlans,
	calendarEvents,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLifecycleEvents,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	mediaLogs,
	notes,
	reflectionTags
} from '../schema';
import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliSlash } from '@domain/calendar';
import type { DatabaseBackupPayload } from '@domain/schemas';

export function exportDatabaseToJson(): DatabaseBackupPayload {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const todayIso = getTodayIso();

	const allHabits = db.select().from(habits).all();
	const allEntries = db.select().from(dailyEntries).all();
	const allLogs = db.select().from(habitLogs).all();
	const allCadence = db.select().from(cadencePlans).all();
	const allBooks = db.select().from(books).all();
	const allMedia = db.select().from(mediaLogs).all();
	const allNotes = db.select().from(notes).all();
	const allEvents = db.select().from(calendarEvents).all();
	const allLifecycle = db.select().from(habitLifecycleEvents).all();
	const allTags = db.select().from(reflectionTags).all();

	return {
		version: 1,
		appName: 'new-life',
		exportedAtIso: nowIso,
		jalaliDate: isoToJalaliSlash(todayIso),
		data: {
			habits: allHabits,
			dailyEntries: allEntries,
			habitLogs: allLogs,
			cadencePlans: allCadence,
			books: allBooks,
			media: allMedia,
			notes: allNotes,
			calendarEvents: allEvents,
			lifecycleEvents: allLifecycle,
			reflectionTags: allTags
		}
	};
}

export function importDatabaseFromJson(
	payload: DatabaseBackupPayload,
	mode: 'replace' | 'merge' = 'replace'
): { importedRecordsCount: number } {
	const { data } = payload;
	let totalImported = 0;

	db.transaction((tx) => {
		if (mode === 'replace') {
			tx.delete(habitLogs).run();
			tx.delete(dailyEntries).run();
			tx.delete(cadencePlans).run();
			tx.delete(habitLifecycleEvents).run();
			tx.delete(reflectionTags).run();
			tx.delete(habits).run();
			tx.delete(books).run();
			tx.delete(mediaLogs).run();
			tx.delete(calendarEvents).run();
			tx.delete(notes).run();
		}

		for (const h of data.habits) {
			tx.insert(habits)
				.values(h)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: habits.id,
					set: h
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const e of data.dailyEntries) {
			tx.insert(dailyEntries)
				.values(e)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: dailyEntries.id,
					set: e
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const l of data.habitLogs) {
			tx.insert(habitLogs)
				.values(l)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: habitLogs.id,
					set: l
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const c of data.cadencePlans) {
			tx.insert(cadencePlans)
				.values(c)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: cadencePlans.id,
					set: c
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const b of data.books) {
			tx.insert(books)
				.values(b)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: books.id,
					set: b
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const m of data.media) {
			tx.insert(mediaLogs)
				.values(m)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: mediaLogs.id,
					set: m
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const n of data.notes) {
			tx.insert(notes)
				.values(n)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: notes.id,
					set: n
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const ev of data.calendarEvents) {
			tx.insert(calendarEvents)
				.values(ev)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: calendarEvents.id,
					set: ev
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const lc of data.lifecycleEvents) {
			tx.insert(habitLifecycleEvents)
				.values(lc)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: habitLifecycleEvents.id,
					set: lc
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const tag of data.reflectionTags) {
			tx.insert(reflectionTags)
				.values(tag)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: reflectionTags.id,
					set: tag
				})
				.run();
			totalImported++;
		}
	});

	return { importedRecordsCount: totalImported };
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/sync.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import {
	books,
	cadencePlans,
	calendarEvents,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLifecycleEvents,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	mediaLogs,
	notes
} from '../schema';
import { deleteBookRecord } from './books.repo';
import { deleteCalendarEvent } from './calendar.repo';
import { deleteMediaRecord } from './media.repo';
import { deleteNoteRecord } from './notes.repo';
import { version } from '$app/env';
import { asc, desc, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getAllHabits } from './habits.repo';
import { getReflectionTags, saveCadencePlansForDate, saveDailyEntryAndLogs } from './daily-tracker.repo';
import {
	parseTagsJson,
	type LocalWorkspaceSnapshot,
	type ServerSyncResponse,
	type SyncRequestPayload
} from '@domain/schemas';

export function getServerMaxVersionIso(): string {
	const row = db.get<{ maxIso: string | null }>(sql`
		SELECT MAX(ts) AS maxIso FROM (
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM daily_entries
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM habit_logs
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM habits
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM cadence_plans
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM notes
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM books
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM media_logs
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM calendar_events
		)
	`);

	return row?.maxIso || '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';
}

export function processClientSyncRequest(input: SyncRequestPayload): ServerSyncResponse {
	const versionBeforeMutations = getServerMaxVersionIso();
	const hadRemoteUpdatesBeforePush = Boolean(
		input.knownServerVersionIso && versionBeforeMutations > input.knownServerVersionIso
	);

	const appliedMutationIds: string[] = [];

	if (input.mutations.length > 0) {
		const sortedMutations = [...input.mutations].sort((a, b) =>
			a.queuedAtIso > b.queuedAtIso ? 1 : a.queuedAtIso < b.queuedAtIso ? -1 : 0
		);

		db.transaction((tx) => {
			const preloadedHabitMap = new Map(getAllHabits().map((h) => [h.id, h]));

			for (const mutation of sortedMutations) {
				try {
					if (mutation.kind === 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY') {
						saveDailyEntryAndLogs(mutation.payload, preloadedHabitMap);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN') {
						saveCadencePlansForDate(mutation.payload);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_BOOK') {
						tx.insert(books)
							.values(mutation.payload)
							.onConflictDoUpdate({
								target: books.id,
								set: mutation.payload
							})
							.run();
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_BOOK') {
						deleteBookRecord(mutation.payload.bookId);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_MEDIA') {
						tx.insert(mediaLogs)
							.values(mutation.payload)
							.onConflictDoUpdate({
								target: mediaLogs.id,
								set: mutation.payload
							})
							.run();
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_MEDIA') {
						deleteMediaRecord(mutation.payload.mediaId);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_NOTE') {
						tx.insert(notes)
							.values(mutation.payload)
							.onConflictDoUpdate({
								target: notes.id,
								set: mutation.payload
							})
							.run();
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_NOTE') {
						deleteNoteRecord(mutation.payload.noteId);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT') {
						tx.insert(calendarEvents)
							.values(mutation.payload)
							.onConflictDoUpdate({
								target: calendarEvents.id,
								set: mutation.payload
							})
							.run();
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_CALENDAR_EVENT') {
						deleteCalendarEvent(mutation.payload.eventId);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_HABIT') {
						tx.insert(habits)
							.values(mutation.payload)
							.onConflictDoUpdate({
								target: habits.id,
								set: mutation.payload
							})
							.run();
						preloadedHabitMap.set(mutation.payload.id, mutation.payload);
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					} else if (mutation.kind === 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT') {
						tx.insert(habitLifecycleEvents)
							.values(mutation.payload)
							.onConflictDoUpdate({
								target: habitLifecycleEvents.id,
								set: mutation.payload
							})
							.run();
						appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
					}
				} catch {
					// Single mutation failure does not abort the remaining valid items
				}
			}
		});
	}

	const serverVersionIso = getServerMaxVersionIso();
	const serverTimeIso = new Date().toISOString();

	const allEntriesRaw = db
		.select()
		.from(dailyEntries)
		.orderBy(desc(dailyEntries.dateIso))
		.all();

	const allLogsRaw = db
		.select()
		.from(habitLogs)
		.orderBy(desc(habitLogs.dateIso))
		.all();

	const mappedEntries = allEntriesRaw.map((e) => ({
		id: e.id,
		dateIso: e.dateIso,
		dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
		reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
		journalText: e.journalText ?? null,
		tags: parseTagsJson(e.tagsJson),
		updatedAtIso: e.updatedAtIso
	}));

	const mappedLogs = allLogsRaw.map((l) => ({
		id: l.id,
		dateIso: l.dateIso,
		habitId: l.habitId,
		habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot,
		achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
		achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot,
		habitSnapshotJson: l.habitSnapshotJson ?? null,
		completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
		actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
		totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
		plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
		plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
		updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso ?? null
	}));

	const fullWorkspace: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot = {
		versionIso: serverVersionIso,
		syncedAtIso: serverTimeIso,
		habits: getAllHabits(),
		availableTags: getReflectionTags(),
		entries: mappedEntries,
		logs: mappedLogs,
		cadencePlans: db.select().from(cadencePlans).orderBy(asc(cadencePlans.dateIso)).all(),
		books: db.select().from(books).orderBy(desc(books.createdAtIso)).all(),
		media: db.select().from(mediaLogs).orderBy(desc(mediaLogs.createdAtIso)).all(),
		notes: db
			.select()
			.from(notes)
			.orderBy(desc(notes.isPinned), desc(notes.updatedAtIso))
			.all(),
		calendarEvents: db
			.select()
			.from(calendarEvents)
			.orderBy(desc(calendarEvents.createdAtIso))
			.all(),
		lifecycleEvents: db
			.select()
			.from(habitLifecycleEvents)
			.orderBy(desc(habitLifecycleEvents.createdAtIso))
			.all()
	};

	return {
		ok: true,
		appliedMutationIds,
		serverVersionIso,
		appBuildVersion: version,
		hasRemoteUpdates: hadRemoteUpdatesBeforePush,
		serverTimeIso,
		recentSnapshots: {
			entries: mappedEntries,
			logs: mappedLogs
		},
		fullWorkspace
	};
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/habits.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import { habitLifecycleEvents, habits } from '../schema';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getTodayIso } from '@domain/calendar';
import { DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY } from '@domain/constants';
import type {
	CreateHabitInput,
	HabitEntity,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	MutateHabitStructureInput
} from '@domain/schemas';

export function getAllHabits(): HabitEntity[] {
	return db.select().from(habits).orderBy(habits.sortOrder).all();
}

export function createNewHabit(input: CreateHabitInput): HabitEntity {
	const todayIso = getTodayIso();
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const existing = getAllHabits();
	const maxOrder = existing.reduce((max, h) => Math.max(max, h.sortOrder), 0);

	const newHabit: HabitEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		parentId: input.parentId ?? null,
		title: input.title.trim(),
		category: input.category.trim() || DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY,
		trackingMode: input.trackingMode,
		timeSlot: input.timeSlot ?? 'day',
		isSituational: input.isSituational ?? false,
		status: 'active',
		scheduleLabel: input.scheduleLabel?.trim() || null,
		notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
		bronzeDesc: input.bronzeDesc?.trim() || null,
		silverDesc: input.silverDesc?.trim() || null,
		goldDesc: input.goldDesc?.trim() || null,
		emergencyMinDesc: input.emergencyMinDesc?.trim() || null,
		defaultIntervals: input.defaultIntervals,
		defaultMinutesPerInterval: input.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
		cadenceWindowMinutes: input.cadenceWindowMinutes,
		sortOrder: maxOrder + 1,
		createdAtIso: todayIso,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	db.insert(habits).values(newHabit).run();

	db.insert(habitLifecycleEvents)
		.values({
			id: crypto.randomUUID(),
			habitId: newHabit.id,
			habitTitleSnapshot: newHabit.title,
			eventType: newHabit.parentId ? 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT' : 'CREATED',
			effectiveDateIso: todayIso,
			summary: newHabit.parentId
				? `ایجاد زیرطرح «${newHabit.title}»`
				: `ایجاد طرح «${newHabit.title}»`,
			reason: input.reason?.trim() || 'تعریف طرح جدید',
			previousStateJson: null,
			newStateJson: JSON.stringify(newHabit),
			createdAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.run();

	return newHabit;
}

export function updateHabitStructure(input: MutateHabitStructureInput): void {
	const current = db.select().from(habits).where(eq(habits.id, input.habitId)).get();
	if (!current) throw new Error('طرح یافت نشد');

	const todayIso = getTodayIso();
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	let eventType: LifecycleEventEntity['eventType'] = 'UPDATED_RULES';
	let summary = `ویرایش شرایط طرح «${input.title}»`;

	if (current.parentId !== input.parentId) {
		if (input.parentId) {
			eventType = 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT';
			const parent = db.select().from(habits).where(eq(habits.id, input.parentId)).get();
			summary = `تبدیل «${input.title}» به زیرطرحِ «${parent?.title ?? 'والد'}»`;
		} else {
			eventType = 'PROMOTED_TO_ROOT';
			summary = `تبدیل «${input.title}» به طرح مستقل`;
		}
	} else if (current.status !== input.status) {
		eventType = input.status === 'archived' ? 'DELETED' : 'STATUS_CHANGED';
		summary = `تغییر وضعیت «${input.title}» به ${input.status}`;
	}

	db.update(habits)
		.set({
			title: input.title,
			category: input.category.trim() || current.category,
			parentId: input.parentId,
			timeSlot: input.timeSlot ?? current.timeSlot,
			isSituational: input.isSituational,
			status: input.status,
			scheduleLabel: input.scheduleLabel,
			notes: input.notes,
			bronzeDesc: input.bronzeDesc,
			silverDesc: input.silverDesc,
			goldDesc: input.goldDesc,
			emergencyMinDesc: input.emergencyMinDesc,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.where(eq(habits.id, input.habitId))
		.run();

	db.insert(habitLifecycleEvents)
		.values({
			id: crypto.randomUUID(),
			habitId: input.habitId,
			habitTitleSnapshot: input.title,
			eventType,
			effectiveDateIso: todayIso,
			summary,
			reason: input.reason,
			previousStateJson: JSON.stringify(current),
			newStateJson: JSON.stringify(input),
			createdAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.run();
}

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/media.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import { mediaLogs } from '../schema';
import { desc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getTodayIso, resolveCompletedRangeDates } from '@domain/calendar';
import type { CreateMediaInput, MediaEntity, UpdateMediaInput } from '@domain/schemas';

export function getAllMedia(): MediaEntity[] {
	return db.select().from(mediaLogs).orderBy(desc(mediaLogs.createdAtIso)).all();
}

export function createMediaRecord(input: CreateMediaInput): MediaEntity {
	const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
		startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
		finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
		isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
	});

	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const newRecord: MediaEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		title: input.title.trim(),
		category: input.category,
		status: input.status,
		startedAtIso,
		finishedAtIso,
		rating: input.rating,
		notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
		createdAtIso: nowIso,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	db.insert(mediaLogs).values(newRecord).run();
	return newRecord;
}

export function updateMediaRecord(input: UpdateMediaInput): void {
	const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
		startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
		finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
		isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
	});

	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	db.update(mediaLogs)
		.set({
			title: input.title.trim(),
			category: input.category,
			status: input.status,
			startedAtIso,
			finishedAtIso,
			rating: input.rating,
			notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.where(eq(mediaLogs.id, input.mediaId))
		.run();
}

export function updateMediaStatus(mediaId: string, status: MediaEntity['status']): void {
	const finishedAtIso = status === 'completed' ? getTodayIso() : null;
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	db.update(mediaLogs)
		.set({ status, finishedAtIso, updatedAtIso: nowIso })
		.where(eq(mediaLogs.id, mediaId))
		.run();
}

export function deleteMediaRecord(mediaId: string): void {
	db.delete(mediaLogs).where(eq(mediaLogs.id, mediaId)).run();
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/server/db/repositories/books.repo.ts
================================================================

import { db } from '../index';
import { books } from '../schema';
import { desc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getTodayIso, resolveCompletedRangeDates } from '@domain/calendar';
import type { BookEntity, CreateBookInput, UpdateBookInput } from '@domain/schemas';

export function getAllBooks(): BookEntity[] {
	return db.select().from(books).orderBy(desc(books.createdAtIso)).all();
}

export function createBookRecord(input: CreateBookInput): BookEntity {
	const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
		startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
		finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
		isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
	});

	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const newBook: BookEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		title: input.title.trim(),
		author: input.author?.trim() || null,
		status: input.status,
		startedAtIso,
		finishedAtIso,
		rating: input.rating,
		notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
		createdAtIso: nowIso,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	db.insert(books).values(newBook).run();
	return newBook;
}

export function updateBookRecord(input: UpdateBookInput): void {
	const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
		startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
		finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
		isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
	});

	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	db.update(books)
		.set({
			title: input.title.trim(),
			author: input.author?.trim() || null,
			status: input.status,
			startedAtIso,
			finishedAtIso,
			rating: input.rating,
			notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		})
		.where(eq(books.id, input.bookId))
		.run();
}

export function updateBookStatus(bookId: string, status: BookEntity['status']): void {
	const finishedAtIso = status === 'completed' ? getTodayIso() : null;
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	db.update(books)
		.set({ status, finishedAtIso, updatedAtIso: nowIso })
		.where(eq(books.id, bookId))
		.run();
}

export function deleteBookRecord(bookId: string): void {
	db.delete(books).where(eq(books.id, bookId)).run();
}

import { index, integer, real, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

export const habits = sqliteTable(
	'habits',
	{
		id: text('id').primaryKey(),
		parentId: text('parent_id'),
		title: text('title').notNull(),
		category: text('category').notNull().default('عمومی'),
		trackingMode: text('tracking_mode', { enum: ['tiered', 'interval_cadence'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('tiered'),
		timeSlot: text('time_slot', { enum: ['morning', 'day', 'night'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('day'),
		isSituational: integer('is_situational', { mode: 'boolean' }).notNull().default(false),
		status: text('status', { enum: ['active', 'paused', 'archived'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('active'),
		scheduleLabel: text('schedule_label'),
		notes: text('notes'),
		bronzeDesc: text('bronze_desc'),
		silverDesc: text('silver_desc'),
		goldDesc: text('gold_desc'),
		emergencyMinDesc: text('emergency_min_desc'),
		defaultIntervals: integer('default_intervals').notNull().default(2),
		defaultMinutesPerInterval: real('default_minutes_per_interval').notNull().default(10),
		cadenceWindowMinutes: integer('cadence_window_minutes').notNull().default(60),
		sortOrder: integer('sort_order').notNull().default(0),
		createdAtIso: text('created_at_iso').notNull(),
		updatedAtIso: text('updated_at_iso')
	},
	(t) => [
		index('idx_habits_parent_id').on(t.parentId),
		index('idx_habits_updated_at').on(t.updatedAtIso)
	]
);

export const cadencePlans = sqliteTable(
	'cadence_plans',
	{
		id: text('id').primaryKey(),
		dateIso: text('date_iso').notNull(),
		habitId: text('habit_id').notNull(),
		plannedIntervals: integer('planned_intervals').notNull(),
		plannedMinutesPerInterval: real('planned_minutes_per_interval').notNull(),
		cadenceWindowMinutes: integer('cadence_window_minutes').notNull().default(60),
		updatedAtIso: text('updated_at_iso')
	},
	(t) => [
		uniqueIndex('idx_cadence_plans_date_habit').on(t.dateIso, t.habitId),
		index('idx_cadence_plans_updated_at').on(t.updatedAtIso)
	]
);

export const dailyEntries = sqliteTable(
	'daily_entries',
	{
		id: text('id').primaryKey(),
		dateIso: text('date_iso').notNull().unique(),
		dayStatus: text('day_status', { enum: ['optimal', 'partial', 'failed'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('optimal'),
		reflectionNote: text('reflection_note').notNull().default(''),
		journalText: text('journal_text'),
		tagsJson: text('tags_json').notNull().default('[]'),
		updatedAtIso: text('updated_at_iso').notNull()
	},
	(t) => [index('idx_daily_entries_updated_at').on(t.updatedAtIso)]
);

export const habitLogs = sqliteTable(
	'habit_logs',
	{
		id: text('id').primaryKey(),
		dateIso: text('date_iso').notNull(),
		habitId: text('habit_id').notNull(),
		habitTitleSnapshot: text('habit_title_snapshot'),
		achievedTier: integer('achieved_tier'),
		achievedRuleSnapshot: text('achieved_rule_snapshot'),
		habitSnapshotJson: text('habit_snapshot_json'),
		completedIntervals: integer('completed_intervals'),
		actualMinutesPerInterval: real('actual_minutes_per_interval'),
		totalActualMinutes: real('total_actual_minutes'),
		plannedIntervalsSnapshot: integer('planned_intervals_snapshot'),
		plannedMinutesSnapshot: real('planned_minutes_snapshot'),
		updatedAtIso: text('updated_at_iso')
	},
	(t) => [
		index('idx_habit_logs_date_iso').on(t.dateIso),
		uniqueIndex('idx_habit_logs_date_habit').on(t.dateIso, t.habitId),
		index('idx_habit_logs_updated_at').on(t.updatedAtIso)
	]
);

export const habitLifecycleEvents = sqliteTable(
	'habit_lifecycle_events',
	{
		id: text('id').primaryKey(),
		habitId: text('habit_id'),
		habitTitleSnapshot: text('habit_title_snapshot').notNull(),
		eventType: text('event_type', {
			enum: [
				'CREATED',
				'UPDATED_RULES',
				'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT',
				'PROMOTED_TO_ROOT',
				'CADENCE_UPDATED',
				'STATUS_CHANGED',
				'DELETED'
			]
		}).notNull(),
		effectiveDateIso: text('effective_date_iso').notNull(),
		summary: text('summary').notNull(),
		reason: text('reason'),
		previousStateJson: text('previous_state_json'),
		newStateJson: text('new_state_json'),
		createdAtIso: text('created_at_iso').notNull()
	},
	(t) => [index('idx_lifecycle_events_habit_id').on(t.habitId)]
);

export const reflectionTags = sqliteTable('reflection_tags', {
	id: text('id').primaryKey(),
	label: text('label').notNull().unique(),
	sortOrder: integer('sort_order').notNull().default(0)
});

export const books = sqliteTable(

	{

		author: text('author'),
		status: text('status', { enum: ['reading', 'completed', 'on_hold'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('completed'),
		startedAtIso: text('started_at_iso'),
		finishedAtIso: text('finished_at_iso'),
		rating: integer('rating').default(5),

);

export const mediaLogs = sqliteTable(

export const calendarEvents = sqliteTable(
