import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { SEED_BOOKS_ORDER, SEED_DAILY_ROWS, SEED_HABITS } from './seed-data';
import { seedDatabaseWithInitialData } from './seed-runner';

const dbUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL || 'local.db';
const client = new Database(dbUrl);
client.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
client.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');

seedDatabaseWithInitialData(client, { wipeExisting: true });

console.log(
	`✅ Seed completed: ${SEED_HABITS.length} habits, ${SEED_DAILY_ROWS.length} daily entries (${SEED_DAILY_ROWS[0].jalaliDate} -> ${SEED_DAILY_ROWS[SEED_DAILY_ROWS.length - 1].jalaliDate}), and ${SEED_BOOKS_ORDER.length} books imported.`
);


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/state/sync-engine.svelte.ts
================================================================

import { browser, version } from '$app/env';
import { invalidateAll } from '$app/navigation';
import type { ServerSyncResponse } from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	clearLocalClientData,
	getAllOutboxMutations,
	getFullWorkspaceFromIdb,
	getSyncMetaFromIdb,
	removeAppliedOutboxMutations,
	saveFullWorkspaceToIdb,
	saveRemoteSnapshotsToIdb,
	saveSyncMetaToIdb
} from './idb-sync-store';

export type SyncStatusVisual = 'synced' | 'local_pending' | 'syncing' | 'offline' | 'error';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
	prompt(): Promise<void>;
	readonly userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed' }>;
}

class ClientSyncEngine {
	status = $state<SyncStatusVisual>('synced');
	pendingCount = $state(0);
	isOnline = $state(true);
	offlineReady = $state(false);
	canInstallPwa = $state(false);
	lastSyncedAtIso = $state<string | null>(null);
	knownServerVersionIso = $state<string | null>(null);
	lastErrorMessage = $state<string | null>(null);
	hasAppUpdate = $state(false);
	currentAppVersion = version;

	private clientId = '';
	private isInitialized = false;
	private isSyncInFlight = false;
	private serviceWorkerReg: ServiceWorkerRegistration | null = null;
	private deferredInstallPrompt: BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null = null;
	private debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
	private retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
	private periodicPollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
	private retryAttempt = 0;

	init(isAuthenticated = true) {
		if (!browser) return;

		if (this.isInitialized) {
			if (isAuthenticated && this.isOnline) {
				void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
			}
			return;
		}

		this.isInitialized = true;
		this.isOnline = navigator.onLine;

		void this.registerAndWarmupServiceWorker();
		void this.bootstrapFromIdb(isAuthenticated);

		window.addEventListener('online', this.handleOnline);
		window.addEventListener('offline', this.handleOffline);
		document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this.handleVisibilityChange);
		window.addEventListener('focus', this.handleWindowFocus);
		window.addEventListener('pagehide', this.handlePageHide);
		window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', this.handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
		window.addEventListener('appinstalled', this.handleAppInstalled);

		this.periodicPollTimer = setInterval(() => {
			if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && this.isOnline && !this.isSyncInFlight) {
				void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
				void this.checkForAppUpdates();
			}
		}, 3 * 60 * 1000);
	}

	async clearLocalDataAndCaches(): Promise<void> {
		if (!browser) return;
		await clearLocalClientData();
		this.pendingCount = 0;
		this.knownServerVersionIso = null;
		this.lastSyncedAtIso = null;
		if ('caches' in window) {
			try {
				const keys = await caches.keys();
				await Promise.all(
					keys
						.filter((k) => k.startsWith('newlife-pwa-'))
						.map((k) => caches.delete(k))
				);
			} catch {
				// ignore cache deletion errors
			}
		}
	}

	destroy() {
		if (!browser || !this.isInitialized) return;
		this.isInitialized = false;
		if (this.debounceTimer) clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
		if (this.retryTimer) clearTimeout(this.retryTimer);
		if (this.periodicPollTimer) clearInterval(this.periodicPollTimer);

		window.removeEventListener('online', this.handleOnline);
		window.removeEventListener('offline', this.handleOffline);
		document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', this.handleVisibilityChange);
		window.removeEventListener('focus', this.handleWindowFocus);
		window.removeEventListener('pagehide', this.handlePageHide);
		window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', this.handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
		window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', this.handleAppInstalled);
	}

	private handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
		e.preventDefault();
		this.deferredInstallPrompt = e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent;
		this.canInstallPwa = true;
	};

	private handleAppInstalled = () => {
		this.deferredInstallPrompt = null;
		this.canInstallPwa = false;
	};

	async promptInstallPwa(): Promise<void> {
		if (!this.deferredInstallPrompt) return;
		await this.deferredInstallPrompt.prompt();
		const choice = await this.deferredInstallPrompt.userChoice;
		if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
			this.deferredInstallPrompt = null;
			this.canInstallPwa = false;
		}
	}

	private async registerAndWarmupServiceWorker() {
		if (!browser || !('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return;
		try {
			const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/service-worker.js', {
				scope: '/'
			});
			this.serviceWorkerReg = reg;
			await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
			this.offlineReady = true;

			if (reg.waiting) {
				this.hasAppUpdate = true;
			}

			reg.addEventListener('updatefound', () => {
				const installingWorker = reg.installing;
				if (installingWorker) {
					installingWorker.addEventListener('statechange', () => {
						if (installingWorker.state === 'installed' && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) {
							this.hasAppUpdate = true;
						}
					});
				}
			});

			navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange', () => {
				window.location.reload();
			});

			if (navigator.onLine) {
				const activeWorker = reg.active || navigator.serviceWorker.controller;
				if (activeWorker) {
					activeWorker.postMessage({ type: 'WARMUP_ALL_ROUTES' });
				}
			}
		} catch {
			// ignore service worker registration errors in unsupported environments
		}
	}

	async checkForAppUpdates(): Promise<boolean> {
		if (!browser || !navigator.onLine) return false;

		try {
			if (this.serviceWorkerReg) {
				await this.serviceWorkerReg.update();
			}

			const resp = await fetch('/api/version', {
				cache: 'no-store',
				headers: { accept: 'application/json' }
			});

			if (resp.ok) {
				const data = (await resp.json()) as { version: string };
				if (data.version && data.version !== this.currentAppVersion) {
					this.hasAppUpdate = true;
					return true;
				}
			}
		} catch {
			// ignore offline check failure
		}
		return this.hasAppUpdate;
	}

	async applyAppUpdate(): Promise<void> {
		if (!browser) return;

		if (this.serviceWorkerReg?.waiting) {
			this.serviceWorkerReg.waiting.postMessage({ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' });
			return;
		}

		window.location.reload();
	}

	private async bootstrapFromIdb(isAuthenticated = true) {
		const [meta, existingWorkspace] = await Promise.all([
			getSyncMetaFromIdb(),
			getFullWorkspaceFromIdb()
		]);
		this.clientId = meta.clientId;
		this.knownServerVersionIso = meta.knownServerVersionIso;
		this.lastSyncedAtIso = meta.lastSyncedAtIso;
		if (existingWorkspace) {
			this.offlineReady = true;
		}

		await this.refreshPendingCount();
		if (this.isOnline && isAuthenticated) {
			void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
		} else if (!this.isOnline) {
			this.status = 'offline';
		}
	}

	private async refreshPendingCount(): Promise<number> {
		const list = await getAllOutboxMutations();
		this.pendingCount = list.length;
		return list.length;
	}

	private handleOnline = () => {
		this.isOnline = true;
		this.retryAttempt = 0;
		if (this.retryTimer) clearTimeout(this.retryTimer);
		void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
		void this.registerAndWarmupServiceWorker();
	};

	private handleOffline = () => {
		this.isOnline = false;
		this.status = 'offline';
		if (this.retryTimer) clearTimeout(this.retryTimer);
	};

	private handleVisibilityChange = () => {
		if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && this.isOnline) {
			this.retryAttempt = 0;
			void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
		}
	};

	private handleWindowFocus = () => {
		if (this.isOnline) {
			void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
		}
	};

	private handlePageHide = () => {
		void this.flushKeepAliveOnUnload();
	};

	async notifyLocalMutationStaged(): Promise<void> {
		if (!browser) return;
		await this.refreshPendingCount();

		if (!navigator.onLine) {
			this.isOnline = false;
			this.status = 'offline';
			return;
		}

		if (this.status !== 'syncing') {
			this.status = 'local_pending';
		}

		if (this.debounceTimer) clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
		this.debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			this.debounceTimer = null;
			void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
		}, 350);
	}

	private scheduleRetry() {
		if (!this.isOnline) return;
		if (this.retryTimer) clearTimeout(this.retryTimer);

		this.retryAttempt = Math.min(this.retryAttempt + 1, 5);
		const delayMs = Math.min(30000, 2000 * Math.pow(2, this.retryAttempt - 1));

		this.retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			this.retryTimer = null;
			if (this.isOnline && !this.isSyncInFlight) {
				void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
			}
		}, delayMs);
	}

	async syncNow(options?: { checkRemoteUpdates?: boolean }): Promise<boolean> {
		if (!browser) return false;

		if (!navigator.onLine) {
			this.isOnline = false;
			this.status = 'offline';
			return false;
		}

		if (this.isSyncInFlight) return false;
		this.isSyncInFlight = true;

		try {
			const mutations = await getAllOutboxMutations();
			this.pendingCount = mutations.length;

			if (mutations.length === 0 && !options?.checkRemoteUpdates) {
				this.status = 'synced';
				this.lastErrorMessage = null;
				this.isSyncInFlight = false;
				return true;
			}

			this.status = mutations.length > 0 ? 'syncing' : 'synced';

			const response = await fetch('/api/sync', {
				method: 'POST',
				headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
				body: JSON.stringify({
					clientId: this.clientId || 'web-client',
					knownServerVersionIso: this.knownServerVersionIso,
					mutations
				})
			});

			if (!response.ok) {
				throw new Error(`خطای سرور (${response.status})`);
			}

			const data = (await response.json()) as ServerSyncResponse;
			if (!data.ok) {
				throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در پردازش بسته همگام‌سازی');
			}

			const appliedSet = new Set(data.appliedMutationIds);
			const appliedSnapshots = mutations
				.filter((m) => appliedSet.has(m.id))
				.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, queuedAtIso: m.queuedAtIso }));

			await removeAppliedOutboxMutations(appliedSnapshots);

			if (data.fullWorkspace) {
				await saveFullWorkspaceToIdb(data.fullWorkspace);
				this.offlineReady = true;
			} else if (data.recentSnapshots) {
				await saveRemoteSnapshotsToIdb(data.recentSnapshots);
			}

			const previousKnownVersion = this.knownServerVersionIso;
			this.knownServerVersionIso = data.serverVersionIso;
			this.lastSyncedAtIso = data.serverTimeIso;
			this.lastErrorMessage = null;
			this.retryAttempt = 0;

			if (data.appBuildVersion && data.appBuildVersion !== this.currentAppVersion) {
				this.hasAppUpdate = true;
			}

			await saveSyncMetaToIdb({
				key: 'meta:sync',
				clientId: this.clientId || 'web-client',
				knownServerVersionIso: this.knownServerVersionIso,
				lastSyncedAtIso: this.lastSyncedAtIso
			});

			const remaining = await this.refreshPendingCount();
			this.status = remaining > 0 ? 'local_pending' : 'synced';

			const hasNewRemoteData =
				data.hasRemoteUpdates &&
				previousKnownVersion !== null &&
				data.serverVersionIso > previousKnownVersion;

			if (data.appliedMutationIds.length > 0 || hasNewRemoteData) {
				await invalidateAll();
			}

			this.isSyncInFlight = false;
			if (remaining > 0) {
				setTimeout(() => {
					void this.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
				}, 400);
			}
			return true;
		} catch (err) {
			this.isSyncInFlight = false;
			this.status = navigator.onLine ? 'error' : 'offline';
			this.lastErrorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطای ارتباط با سرور';
			this.scheduleRetry();
			return false;
		}
	}

	private async flushKeepAliveOnUnload() {
		if (!browser || !navigator.onLine) return;
		const mutations = await getAllOutboxMutations();
		if (mutations.length === 0) return;

		try {
			void fetch('/api/sync', {
				method: 'POST',
				headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
				body: JSON.stringify({
					clientId: this.clientId || 'web-client',
					knownServerVersionIso: this.knownServerVersionIso,
					mutations
				}),
				keepalive: true
			});
		} catch {
			// ignore unload errors
		}
	}
}

export const syncEngine = new ClientSyncEngine();


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/state/daily-tracker.svelte.ts
================================================================

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import {
	computeCompositeParentDisplay,
	computeDailyCompletionStats,
	evaluateDayStatusColor,
	getApplicableHabitsForDate,
	getMaxSupportedTier,
	isHabitNodeCompleted,
	resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
} from '@core/habit-engine';
import { DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS, TIER_SCORES } from '@domain/constants';
import type {
	AnalyticsRawEntry,
	DayCommandPayload,
	DayStatusColor,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitLogItemInput,
	SaveDailyEntryInput
} from '@domain/schemas';
import { shiftIsoDate } from '@domain/calendar';
import {
	buildOfflineDayCommandPayload,
	getAllCachedDaysFromIdb,
	getCachedDayFromIdb,
	getFullWorkspaceFromIdb,
	stageDailyEntryInIdb
} from './idb-sync-store';
import { syncEngine } from './sync-engine.svelte';

export interface CadenceDraftItem {
	habitId: string;
	plannedIntervals: number;
	plannedMinutesPerInterval: number;
	cadenceWindowMinutes: number;
}

export type SaveSyncState = 'idle' | 'pending' | 'saving' | 'saved' | 'error';

export class DailyTrackerController {
	dateIso = $state('');
	tomorrowIso = $state('');
	habits = $state<HabitEntity[]>([]);
	availableTags = $state<string[]>([...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS]);
	recentEntries = $state<AnalyticsRawEntry[]>([]);
	logsByHabitId = $state<Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>>({});
	reflectionNote = $state('');
	journalText = $state('');
	selectedTags = $state<string[]>([]);
	manualDayStatus = $state<DayStatusColor | null>(null);
	tomorrowPlansByHabitId = $state<Record<string, CadenceDraftItem>>({});
	isDirty = $state(false);
	hasExistingEntry = $state(false);
	saveState = $state<SaveSyncState>('idle');

	private autoSaveTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
	private savedIndicatorTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
	private activeSaveSequence = 0;
	private lastHydratedPayload: DayCommandPayload | null = null;
	private isDestroyed = false;

	constructor(initial: DayCommandPayload | (() => DayCommandPayload)) {
		this.hydrate(typeof initial === 'function' ? initial() : initial);
	}

	destroy() {
		this.isDestroyed = true;
		if (this.autoSaveTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.autoSaveTimer);
			this.autoSaveTimer = null;
		}
		if (this.savedIndicatorTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.savedIndicatorTimer);
			this.savedIndicatorTimer = null;
		}
	}

	hydrate(data: DayCommandPayload) {
		if (this.lastHydratedPayload === data && this.dateIso === data.dateIso) {
			return;
		}
		this.lastHydratedPayload = data;

		if (this.dateIso && this.dateIso !== data.dateIso && this.isDirty) {
			void this.flushPendingSave();
		}
		if (this.autoSaveTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.autoSaveTimer);
			this.autoSaveTimer = null;
		}

		this.dateIso = data.dateIso;
		this.tomorrowIso = data.tomorrowIso;
		this.habits = data.habits;
		if (data.availableTags && data.availableTags.length > 0) {
			this.availableTags = data.availableTags;
		}
		if (data.recentEntries) {
			this.recentEntries = data.recentEntries;
		}
		this.hasExistingEntry = Boolean(data.entry);
		this.reflectionNote = data.entry?.reflectionNote ?? '';
		this.journalText = data.entry?.journalText ?? '';
		this.selectedTags = data.entry?.tags ?? [];
		this.manualDayStatus = data.entry?.dayStatus ?? null;

		const dayPlanMap = new Map(data.dayCadencePlans.map((p) => [p.habitId, p]));
		const existingLogMap = new Map(data.logs.map((l) => [l.habitId, l]));

		const nextLogs: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = {};

		for (const habit of data.habits) {
			const existing = existingLogMap.get(habit.id);
			const dayPlan = dayPlanMap.get(habit.id);
			const plannedIntervals = dayPlan?.plannedIntervals ?? null;
			const plannedMinutes = dayPlan?.plannedMinutesPerInterval ?? null;

			if (existing) {
				nextLogs[habit.id] = {
					habitId: habit.id,
					habitTitleSnapshot: existing.habitTitleSnapshot ?? habit.title,
					achievedTier: existing.achievedTier,
					achievedRuleSnapshot:
						existing.achievedRuleSnapshot ??
						resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, existing.achievedTier),
					completedIntervals: existing.completedIntervals ?? null,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: existing.actualMinutesPerInterval,
					totalActualMinutes: existing.totalActualMinutes,
					plannedIntervalsSnapshot: existing.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? plannedIntervals,
					plannedMinutesSnapshot: existing.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? plannedMinutes,
					updatedAtIso: existing.updatedAtIso ?? null
				};
			} else {
				const effectivePlannedIntervals =
					habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
						? (plannedIntervals ?? habit.defaultIntervals)
						: plannedIntervals;
				const effectivePlannedMinutes =
					habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
						? (plannedMinutes ?? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval)
						: plannedMinutes;

				nextLogs[habit.id] = {
					habitId: habit.id,
					habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
					achievedTier: null,
					achievedRuleSnapshot: null,
					completedIntervals: null,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
					totalActualMinutes: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
					plannedIntervalsSnapshot: effectivePlannedIntervals,
					plannedMinutesSnapshot: effectivePlannedMinutes,
					updatedAtIso: null
				};
			}
		}

		this.logsByHabitId = nextLogs;

		const tomorrowPlanMap = new Map(data.tomorrowCadencePlans.map((p) => [p.habitId, p]));
		const nextTomorrow: Record<string, CadenceDraftItem> = {};
		for (const habit of data.habits.filter((h) => h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence')) {
			const tPlan = tomorrowPlanMap.get(habit.id);
			const todayPlan = dayPlanMap.get(habit.id);
			nextTomorrow[habit.id] = {
				habitId: habit.id,
				plannedIntervals: 1,
				plannedMinutesPerInterval:
					tPlan?.plannedMinutesPerInterval ?? todayPlan?.plannedMinutesPerInterval ?? 0,
				cadenceWindowMinutes: 60
			};
		}
		this.tomorrowPlansByHabitId = nextTomorrow;
		this.isDirty = false;
		this.isDestroyed = false;
		this.saveState = data.entry ? 'saved' : 'idle';

		if (browser) {
			void this.reconcileWithLocalIdbCache(data.dateIso);
		}
	}

	async switchDateOfflineIfNeeded(requestedDateIso: string, serverDataDateIso: string) {
		if (!browser || requestedDateIso === serverDataDateIso) return;
		const offlinePayload = await buildOfflineDayCommandPayload(requestedDateIso);
		if (offlinePayload) {
			this.hydrate({
				...offlinePayload,
				habits: offlinePayload.habits.length > 0 ? offlinePayload.habits : this.habits,
				availableTags:
					offlinePayload.availableTags && offlinePayload.availableTags.length > 0
						? offlinePayload.availableTags
						: this.availableTags
			});
			return;
		}

		const cached = await getCachedDayFromIdb(requestedDateIso);
		this.hydrate({
			dateIso: requestedDateIso,
			tomorrowIso: shiftIsoDate(requestedDateIso, 1),
			habits: this.habits,
			availableTags: this.availableTags,
			entry: cached
				? {
						dayStatus: cached.payload.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: cached.payload.reflectionNote,
						journalText: cached.payload.journalText ?? null,
						tags: cached.payload.tags,
						updatedAtIso: cached.updatedAtIso
					}
				: null,
			logs: cached
				? cached.payload.logs.map((l) => ({
						habitId: l.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot ?? null,
						achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot ?? null,
						completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
						updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso ?? cached.updatedAtIso
					}))
				: [],
			dayCadencePlans: [],
			tomorrowCadencePlans: [],
			recentEntries: this.recentEntries
		});
	}

	private async reconcileWithLocalIdbCache(targetDateIso: string) {
		const [cached, allCachedDays, ws] = await Promise.all([
			getCachedDayFromIdb(targetDateIso),
			getAllCachedDaysFromIdb(),
			getFullWorkspaceFromIdb()
		]);
		if (this.dateIso !== targetDateIso) return;

		if (allCachedDays.length > 0 || ws) {
			const recentMap = new Map(this.recentEntries.map((r) => [r.dateIso, r]));
			if (ws) {
				for (const e of ws.entries) {
					recentMap.set(e.dateIso, {
						dateIso: e.dateIso,
						dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
						tags: e.tags
					});
				}
			}
			for (const rec of allCachedDays) {
				recentMap.set(rec.dateIso, {
					dateIso: rec.dateIso,
					dayStatus: rec.payload.dayStatus,
					reflectionNote: rec.payload.reflectionNote,
					tags: rec.payload.tags
				});
			}
			this.recentEntries = Array.from(recentMap.values()).sort((a, b) =>
				b.dateIso > a.dateIso ? 1 : b.dateIso < a.dateIso ? -1 : 0
			);
		}

		if (!cached) return;

		const isClearState =
			(!cached.payload.reflectionNote || cached.payload.reflectionNote.trim() === '') &&
			(!cached.payload.journalText || cached.payload.journalText.trim() === '') &&
			cached.payload.tags.length === 0 &&
			cached.payload.logs.every(
				(l) =>
					(l.achievedTier === null || l.achievedTier === undefined) &&
					(l.totalActualMinutes === null || l.totalActualMinutes === 0 || l.totalActualMinutes === undefined)
			);

		if (isClearState) {
			const clearedLogs: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = { ...this.logsByHabitId };
			for (const h of this.habits) {
				const c = clearedLogs[h.id];
				if (c) {
					clearedLogs[h.id] = {
						...c,
						achievedTier: null,
						achievedRuleSnapshot: null,
						completedIntervals: null,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
						totalActualMinutes: h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null
					};
				}
			}
			this.logsByHabitId = clearedLogs;
			this.reflectionNote = '';
			this.journalText = '';
			this.selectedTags = [];
			this.manualDayStatus = null;
			this.hasExistingEntry = false;
			this.syncOptimisticRecentEntry();
			return;
		}

		let hasNewerLocalLogs = !this.hasExistingEntry;
		const mergedLogs: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = { ...this.logsByHabitId };

		for (const localLog of cached.payload.logs) {
			const current = mergedLogs[localLog.habitId];
			const localTime = localLog.updatedAtIso ?? cached.updatedAtIso ?? '';
			const serverTime = current?.updatedAtIso ?? '';

			if (!this.hasExistingEntry || (localTime && localTime >= serverTime)) {
				mergedLogs[localLog.habitId] = {
					...current,
					...localLog
				};
				hasNewerLocalLogs = true;
			}
		}

		if (hasNewerLocalLogs) {
			this.logsByHabitId = mergedLogs;
			this.reflectionNote = cached.payload.reflectionNote;
			this.journalText = cached.payload.journalText ?? '';
			this.selectedTags = cached.payload.tags;
			this.manualDayStatus = cached.payload.dayStatus;
			this.hasExistingEntry = true;
			this.syncOptimisticRecentEntry();
		}
	}

	loggedHabitIds = $derived.by<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => {
		const set = new Set<string>();
		for (const log of Object.values(this.logsByHabitId)) {
			const hasTier = log.achievedTier !== null && log.achievedTier !== undefined;
			const hasMinutes =
				this.hasExistingEntry && resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log) > 0;
			if (hasTier || hasMinutes) {
				set.add(log.habitId);
			}
		}
		return set;
	});

	applicableHabits = $derived.by<HabitEntity[]>(() =>
		getApplicableHabitsForDate(this.habits, {
			dateIso: this.dateIso,
			hasExistingEntry: this.hasExistingEntry,
			loggedHabitIds: this.loggedHabitIds
		})
	);

	computedDayStatus = $derived.by<DayStatusColor>(() => {
		if (this.manualDayStatus) return this.manualDayStatus;
		return evaluateDayStatusColor(this.applicableHabits, Object.values(this.logsByHabitId));
	});

	completionStats = $derived.by(() =>
		computeDailyCompletionStats({
			applicableHabits: this.applicableHabits,
			loggedHabitIds: this.loggedHabitIds,
			logsByHabitId: this.logsByHabitId,
			hasExistingEntry: this.hasExistingEntry,
			isDirty: this.isDirty
		})
	);

	markAllOptimalFiveSecond() {
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const updated: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = { ...this.logsByHabitId };
		for (const habit of this.applicableHabits) {
			if (habit.status !== 'active') continue;
			const current = updated[habit.id];
			if (!current) continue;

			if (habit.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
				const maxTier = getMaxSupportedTier(habit);
				updated[habit.id] = {
					...current,
					habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
					achievedTier: maxTier,
					achievedRuleSnapshot: resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, maxTier),
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
			} else {
				const plannedMinutes =
					current.plannedMinutesSnapshot && current.plannedMinutesSnapshot > 0
						? current.plannedMinutesSnapshot
						: current.actualMinutesPerInterval && current.actualMinutesPerInterval > 0
							? current.actualMinutesPerInterval
							: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval > 0
								? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
								: 10;
				const intervals = current.completedIntervals ?? null;
				updated[habit.id] = {
					...current,
					completedIntervals: intervals,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: plannedMinutes,
					totalActualMinutes:
						intervals !== null ? Number((intervals * plannedMinutes).toFixed(1)) : plannedMinutes,
					achievedTier: TIER_SCORES.GOLD,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
			}
		}
		this.logsByHabitId = updated;
		this.manualDayStatus = 'optimal';
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	markAllFailedZero() {
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const updated: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = { ...this.logsByHabitId };
		for (const habit of this.applicableHabits) {
			if (habit.status !== 'active') continue;
			const current = updated[habit.id];
			if (!current) continue;

			updated[habit.id] = {
				...current,
				habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
				achievedTier: TIER_SCORES.FAILED,
				achievedRuleSnapshot: resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, TIER_SCORES.FAILED),
				completedIntervals: null,
				actualMinutesPerInterval: 0,
				totalActualMinutes: 0,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};
		}
		this.logsByHabitId = updated;
		this.manualDayStatus = 'failed';
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	setTierScore(habitId: string, tier: number) {
		const current = this.logsByHabitId[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		const nextTier = current.achievedTier === tier ? null : tier;
		this.setTierExplicit(habitId, nextTier);
	}

	setTierExplicit(habitId: string, tier: number | null) {
		const current = this.logsByHabitId[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const habit = this.habits.find((h) => h.id === habitId);
		this.logsByHabitId[habitId] = {
			...current,
			habitTitleSnapshot: habit?.title ?? current.habitTitleSnapshot ?? null,
			achievedTier: tier,
			achievedRuleSnapshot:
				habit && tier !== null ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, tier) : null,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		};
		this.manualDayStatus = null;
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	setParentGroupAllTier(parentId: string, tier: number) {
		const children = this.habits.filter((h) => h.parentId === parentId && h.status === 'active');
		const allAlreadySelected =
			children.length > 0 &&
			children.every((child) => this.logsByHabitId[child.id]?.achievedTier === tier);
		const targetTier = allAlreadySelected ? null : tier;

		for (const child of children) {
			const maxChildTier = getMaxSupportedTier(child);
			const effectiveTier =
				targetTier === null
					? null
					: targetTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED || targetTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED
						? targetTier
						: Math.min(targetTier, maxChildTier);
			this.setTierExplicit(child.id, effectiveTier);
		}
		this.setTierExplicit(parentId, targetTier);
	}

	clearDay() {
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const updated: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = { ...this.logsByHabitId };
		for (const habit of this.habits) {
			const current = updated[habit.id];
			if (!current) continue;
			updated[habit.id] = {
				...current,
				achievedTier: null,
				achievedRuleSnapshot: null,
				completedIntervals: null,
				actualMinutesPerInterval: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
				totalActualMinutes: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};
		}
		this.logsByHabitId = updated;
		this.reflectionNote = '';
		this.journalText = '';
		this.selectedTags = [];
		this.manualDayStatus = null;
		this.hasExistingEntry = false;
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	isNodeCompleted(habitId: string): boolean {
		return isHabitNodeCompleted({
			habitId,
			habits: this.habits,
			logsByHabitId: this.logsByHabitId,
			hasExistingEntry: this.hasExistingEntry,
			isDirty: this.isDirty
		});
	}

	getCompositeNotation(parentId: string): string {
		const children = this.habits
			.filter((h) => h.parentId === parentId && h.status === 'active')
			.sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);
		return computeCompositeParentDisplay(children, this.logsByHabitId);
	}

	updateCadenceActual(
		habitId: string,
		intervals: number | null,
		minutesPerInterval: number
	) {
		const current = this.logsByHabitId[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const safeIntervals =
			intervals === null || Number.isNaN(intervals) || intervals <= 0
				? null
				: Math.floor(intervals);
		const safeMinutes = Math.max(0, Number.isNaN(minutesPerInterval) ? 0 : minutesPerInterval);
		this.logsByHabitId[habitId] = {
			...current,
			completedIntervals: safeIntervals,
			actualMinutesPerInterval: safeMinutes,
			totalActualMinutes:
				safeIntervals !== null
					? Number((safeIntervals * safeMinutes).toFixed(1))
					: safeMinutes,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		};
		this.manualDayStatus = null;
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	setManualDayStatus(status: DayStatusColor) {
		this.manualDayStatus = status;
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	handleTextChanged() {
		this.scheduleAutoSave(750);
	}

	toggleTag(tag: string) {
		if (this.selectedTags.includes(tag)) {
			this.selectedTags = this.selectedTags.filter((t) => t !== tag);
		} else {
			this.selectedTags = [...this.selectedTags, tag];
		}
		this.scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	scheduleAutoSave(delayMs = 0) {
		this.isDirty = true;
		this.saveState = 'saving';
		this.syncOptimisticRecentEntry();

		if (browser && this.dateIso) {
			const snapshot = this.buildDailyInputObject();
			void stageDailyEntryInIdb(snapshot).then(() => syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged());
		}

		if (this.autoSaveTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.autoSaveTimer);
		}

		if (delayMs <= 0) {
			this.autoSaveTimer = null;
			void this.flushPendingSave();
		} else {
			this.autoSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				this.autoSaveTimer = null;
				void this.flushPendingSave();
			}, delayMs);
		}
	}

	private syncOptimisticRecentEntry() {
		const currentIso = this.dateIso;
		if (!currentIso) return;
		const updatedItem: AnalyticsRawEntry = {
			dateIso: currentIso,
			dayStatus: this.computedDayStatus,
			reflectionNote: this.reflectionNote,
			tags: [...this.selectedTags]
		};
		const idx = this.recentEntries.findIndex((r) => r.dateIso === currentIso);
		if (idx >= 0) {
			const next = [...this.recentEntries];
			next[idx] = updatedItem;
			this.recentEntries = next;
		} else {
			this.recentEntries = [updatedItem, ...this.recentEntries];
		}
	}

	async flushPendingSave(): Promise<void> {
		if (this.autoSaveTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.autoSaveTimer);
			this.autoSaveTimer = null;
		}
		if (!this.dateIso) return;

		const inputObj = this.buildDailyInputObject();
		const targetDateSnapshot = this.dateIso;
		const seq = ++this.activeSaveSequence;
		this.saveState = 'saving';

		try {
			await stageDailyEntryInIdb(inputObj);
			const syncedOk = await syncEngine.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });

			if (!this.isDestroyed && seq === this.activeSaveSequence && this.dateIso === targetDateSnapshot) {
				this.isDirty = false;
				this.hasExistingEntry = true;
				this.saveState = syncedOk || !navigator.onLine ? 'saved' : 'error';
				this.syncOptimisticRecentEntry();

				if (this.savedIndicatorTimer) clearTimeout(this.savedIndicatorTimer);
				this.savedIndicatorTimer = setTimeout(() => {
					if (!this.isDestroyed && this.saveState === 'saved' && !this.isDirty) {
						this.saveState = 'idle';
					}
				}, 2500);
			}
		} catch {
			if (!this.isDestroyed && seq === this.activeSaveSequence) {
				this.saveState = navigator.onLine ? 'error' : 'saved';
			}
		}
	}

	copyTodayActualsToTomorrowPlan() {
		const next: Record<string, CadenceDraftItem> = { ...this.tomorrowPlansByHabitId };
		for (const habit of this.habits.filter((h) => h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence')) {
			const log = this.logsByHabitId[habit.id];
			if (log) {
				next[habit.id] = {
					habitId: habit.id,
					plannedIntervals: 1,
					plannedMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? 0,
					cadenceWindowMinutes: 60
				};
			}
		}
		this.tomorrowPlansByHabitId = next;
	}

	buildDailyInputObject(): SaveDailyEntryInput {
		return {
			dateIso: this.dateIso,
			dayStatus: this.computedDayStatus,
			reflectionNote: this.reflectionNote,
			journalText: this.journalText.trim() || null,
			tags: this.selectedTags,
			updatedAtIso: new Date().toISOString(),
			logs: Object.values(this.logsByHabitId)
		};
	}

	serializeTomorrowPlanPayload(): string {
		return JSON.stringify({
			targetDateIso: this.tomorrowIso,
			items: Object.values(this.tomorrowPlansByHabitId)
		});
	}
}

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import {
	clearLocalAuthRecord,
	getLocalAuthRecord,
	saveLocalAuthRecord,
	type LocalAuthRecord
} from './idb-sync-store';

function generateRandomSalt(length = 16): Uint8Array {
	const salt = new Uint8Array(length);
	crypto.getRandomValues(salt);
	return salt;
}

function bytesToHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
	return Array.from(bytes)
		.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
		.join('');
}

function hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {
	const bytes = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
	for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) {
		bytes[i / 2] = parseInt(hex.substring(i, i + 2), 16);
	}
	return bytes;
}

async function deriveVerifierHex(password: string, salt: Uint8Array): Promise<string> {
	const enc = new TextEncoder();
	const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
		'raw',
		enc.encode(password),
		{ name: 'PBKDF2' },
		false,
		['deriveBits']
	);

	const derivedBits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
		{
			name: 'PBKDF2',
			salt: salt as BufferSource,
			iterations: 100000,
			hash: 'SHA-256'
		},
		keyMaterial,
		256
	);

	return bytesToHex(new Uint8Array(derivedBits));
}

function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
	if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
	let diff = 0;
	for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
		diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
	}
	return diff === 0;
}

class ClientAuthManager {
	isAuthenticated = $state(false);
	isPasswordConfigured = $state(true);
	isInitialized = $state(false);

	async checkAuth(
		serverAuthenticated?: boolean,
		serverPasswordConfigured?: boolean
	): Promise<{ isAuthenticated: boolean; isPasswordConfigured: boolean }> {
		if (!browser) {
			return {
				isAuthenticated: serverAuthenticated ?? false,
				isPasswordConfigured: serverPasswordConfigured ?? true
			};
		}

		const record = await getLocalAuthRecord();
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

		if (serverAuthenticated === true) {
			this.isAuthenticated = true;
			this.isPasswordConfigured = serverPasswordConfigured ?? true;
			if (record && record.expiresAtIso < nowIso) {
				record.expiresAtIso = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
				await saveLocalAuthRecord(record);
			}
			this.isInitialized = true;
			return {
				isAuthenticated: true,
				isPasswordConfigured: this.isPasswordConfigured
			};
		}

		if (serverAuthenticated === false && navigator.onLine) {
			this.isAuthenticated = false;
			this.isPasswordConfigured = serverPasswordConfigured ?? (record?.isPasswordConfigured ?? true);
			if (record) {
				await clearLocalAuthRecord();
			}
			this.isInitialized = true;
			return {
				isAuthenticated: false,
				isPasswordConfigured: this.isPasswordConfigured
			};
		}

		if (record && record.isAuthenticated && record.expiresAtIso > nowIso) {
			this.isAuthenticated = true;
			this.isPasswordConfigured = record.isPasswordConfigured;
			this.isInitialized = true;
			return {
				isAuthenticated: true,
				isPasswordConfigured: record.isPasswordConfigured
			};
		}

		this.isAuthenticated = false;
		this.isPasswordConfigured = record?.isPasswordConfigured ?? (serverPasswordConfigured ?? true);
		this.isInitialized = true;
		return {
			isAuthenticated: false,
			isPasswordConfigured: this.isPasswordConfigured
		};
	}

	async saveClientCredential(password: string): Promise<void> {
		if (!browser || !password.trim()) return;

		const salt = generateRandomSalt(16);
		const saltHex = bytesToHex(salt);
		const verifierHex = await deriveVerifierHex(password, salt);
		const now = new Date();
		const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

		const record: LocalAuthRecord = {
			key: 'auth:session',
			isAuthenticated: true,
			isPasswordConfigured: true,
			expiresAtIso: expiresAt.toISOString(),
			verifierSaltHex: saltHex,
			passwordVerifierHex: verifierHex,
			updatedAtIso: now.toISOString()
		};

		await saveLocalAuthRecord(record);
		this.isAuthenticated = true;
		this.isPasswordConfigured = true;
	}

	async loginOffline(password: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
		if (!browser) return { success: false, error: 'محیط نامعتبر است' };

		const record = await getLocalAuthRecord();
		if (!record || !record.passwordVerifierHex || !record.verifierSaltHex) {
			return {
				success: false,
				error: 'اطلاعات ورود محلی در این دستگاه یافت نشد. لطفاً یک‌بار در حالت آنلاین وارد شوید.'
			};
		}

		const salt = hexToBytes(record.verifierSaltHex);
		const candidateVerifier = await deriveVerifierHex(password, salt);

		if (!timingSafeEqual(candidateVerifier, record.passwordVerifierHex)) {
			return { success: false, error: 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است' };
		}

		const now = new Date();
		const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

		record.isAuthenticated = true;
		record.expiresAtIso = expiresAt.toISOString();
		record.updatedAtIso = now.toISOString();

		await saveLocalAuthRecord(record);
		this.isAuthenticated = true;

		return { success: true };
	}

	async logout(): Promise<void> {
		if (!browser) return;
		await clearLocalAuthRecord();
		this.isAuthenticated = false;
	}
}

export const clientAuth = new ClientAuthManager();

import { browser } from '$app/env';

class ThemeManager {
	isDark = $state(true);

	constructor() {
		if (browser) {
			const root = document.documentElement;
			const stored = localStorage.getItem('newlife_theme');
			if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark') {
				this.isDark = stored === 'dark';
			} else {
				this.isDark = root.classList.contains('dark');
			}
			this.applyToDom();
		}
	}

	toggle() {
		const nextState = !this.isDark;
		if (!browser) {
			this.isDark = nextState;
			return;
		}

		const applyChange = () => {
			this.isDark = nextState;
			try {
				localStorage.setItem('newlife_theme', this.isDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
			} catch {
				// ignore storage quota errors
			}
			this.applyToDom();
		};

		const docWithTransition = document as Document & {
			startViewTransition?: (callback: () => void) => void;
		};

		if (typeof docWithTransition.startViewTransition === 'function') {
			docWithTransition.startViewTransition(() => {
				applyChange();
			});
		} else {
			applyChange();
		}
	}

	private applyToDom() {
		if (!browser) return;
		const root = document.documentElement;
		root.classList.toggle('dark', this.isDark);
		root.style.colorScheme = this.isDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
	}
}

export const themeState = new ThemeManager();

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import {
	buildFullAnalyticsReport
} from '@core/analytics-engine';
import {
	assembleJalaliMonthArchive,
	computeUpcomingEventsBanner
} from '@core/calendar-engine';
import { serializeHabitSpecSnapshot } from '@core/habit-engine';
import {
	getTodayIso,
	getYesterdayIso,
	shiftIsoDate
} from '@domain/calendar';
import { DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS } from '@domain/constants';
import type {
	AnalyticsRawEntry,
	AnalyticsRawLog,
	AnalyticsTimeRange,
	DayCommandPayload,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitLogItemInput,
	JournalMemoryItem,
	LocalWorkspaceSnapshot,
	SaveDailyEntryInput,
	SyncOutboxMutation
} from '@domain/schemas';

const DB_NAME = 'newlife_local_db';
const DB_VERSION = 1;
const OUTBOX_STORE = 'sync_outbox';
const SNAPSHOT_STORE = 'local_snapshots';

export interface LocalDayCacheRecord {
	key: string;
	dateIso: string;
	updatedAtIso: string;
	payload: SaveDailyEntryInput;
}

export interface SyncMetaRecord {
	key: 'meta:sync';
	clientId: string;
	knownServerVersionIso: string | null;
	lastSyncedAtIso: string | null;
}

export interface LocalAuthRecord {
	key: 'auth:session';
	isAuthenticated: boolean;
	isPasswordConfigured: boolean;
	expiresAtIso: string;
	verifierSaltHex: string;
	passwordVerifierHex: string;
	updatedAtIso: string;
}

export async function getLocalAuthRecord(): Promise<LocalAuthRecord | null> {
	if (!browser) return null;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		return new Promise((resolve) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readonly');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const req = store.get('auth:session');
			req.onsuccess = () => resolve((req.result as LocalAuthRecord) ?? null);
			req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
		});
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}

export async function saveLocalAuthRecord(record: LocalAuthRecord): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readwrite');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			store.put(record);
			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
}

export async function clearLocalAuthRecord(): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readwrite');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			store.delete('auth:session');
			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
}

export interface WorkspaceSnapshotRecord {
	key: 'workspace:full';
	updatedAtIso: string;
	workspace: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot;
}

let dbPromise: Promise<IDBDatabase> | null = null;
let memoryWorkspaceCache: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot | null = null;

function openLocalDatabase(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
	if (!browser) {
		return Promise.reject(new Error('IndexedDB is only available in browser'));
	}
	if (dbPromise) return dbPromise;

	dbPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
		const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);

		request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
			const db = request.result;
			if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(OUTBOX_STORE)) {
				db.createObjectStore(OUTBOX_STORE, { keyPath: 'id' });
			}
			if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(SNAPSHOT_STORE)) {
				db.createObjectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE, { keyPath: 'key' });
			}
		};

		request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
		request.onerror = () => {
			dbPromise = null;
			reject(request.error);
		};
	});

	return dbPromise;
}

function mergeHabitLogsLww(
	existingLogs: HabitLogItemInput[],
	incomingLogs: HabitLogItemInput[],
	fallbackTimestampIso: string
): HabitLogItemInput[] {
	const map = new Map<string, HabitLogItemInput>();
	for (const log of existingLogs) {
		map.set(log.habitId, log);
	}

	for (const nextLog of incomingLogs) {
		const prev = map.get(nextLog.habitId);
		if (!prev) {
			map.set(nextLog.habitId, {
				...nextLog,
				updatedAtIso: nextLog.updatedAtIso ?? fallbackTimestampIso
			});
			continue;
		}

		const prevTime = prev.updatedAtIso ?? '';
		const nextTime = nextLog.updatedAtIso ?? fallbackTimestampIso;
		if (nextTime >= prevTime) {
			map.set(nextLog.habitId, {
				...nextLog,
				updatedAtIso: nextTime
			});
		}
	}

	return Array.from(map.values());
}

export async function stageMutationInIdb(
	mutation: SyncOutboxMutation,
	workspaceMutator?: (ws: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot) => void
): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	const db = await openLocalDatabase();

	return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
		const tx = db.transaction([OUTBOX_STORE, SNAPSHOT_STORE], 'readwrite');
		const outbox = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);
		const snapshots = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);

		outbox.put(mutation);

		if (workspaceMutator) {
			const wsReq = snapshots.get('workspace:full');
			wsReq.onsuccess = () => {
				const existingRecord = wsReq.result as WorkspaceSnapshotRecord | undefined;
				const ws: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot = existingRecord?.workspace ?? {
					versionIso: new Date().toISOString(),
					syncedAtIso: new Date().toISOString(),
					habits: [],
					availableTags: [...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS],
					entries: [],
					logs: [],
					cadencePlans: [],
					books: [],
					media: [],
					notes: [],
					calendarEvents: [],
					lifecycleEvents: []
				};

				workspaceMutator(ws);
				ws.syncedAtIso = new Date().toISOString();
				memoryWorkspaceCache = ws;

				snapshots.put({
					key: 'workspace:full',
					updatedAtIso: ws.syncedAtIso,
					workspace: ws
				} satisfies WorkspaceSnapshotRecord);
			};
		}

		tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
		tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
	});
}

export async function stageDailyEntryInIdb(input: SaveDailyEntryInput): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	const db = await openLocalDatabase();
	const nowIso = input.updatedAtIso || new Date().toISOString();
	const outboxId = `daily:${input.dateIso}`;
	const snapshotKey = `day:${input.dateIso}`;

	const isClearState =
		(!input.reflectionNote || input.reflectionNote.trim() === '') &&
		(!input.journalText || input.journalText.trim() === '') &&
		input.tags.length === 0 &&
		input.logs.every(
			(l) =>
				(l.achievedTier === null || l.achievedTier === undefined) &&
				(l.totalActualMinutes === null || l.totalActualMinutes === 0 || l.totalActualMinutes === undefined)
		);

	return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
		const tx = db.transaction([OUTBOX_STORE, SNAPSHOT_STORE], 'readwrite');
		const outbox = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);
		const snapshots = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);

		if (isClearState) {
			snapshots.delete(snapshotKey);
		} else {
			const daySnapshot: LocalDayCacheRecord = {
				key: snapshotKey,
				dateIso: input.dateIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso,
				payload: input
			};
			snapshots.put(daySnapshot);
		}

		const mutationRecord: SyncOutboxMutation = {
			id: outboxId,
			kind: 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY',
			queuedAtIso: nowIso,
			payload: {
				...input,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			}
		};

		outbox.put(mutationRecord);

		const wsReq = snapshots.get('workspace:full');
		wsReq.onsuccess = () => {
			const existingRecord = wsReq.result as WorkspaceSnapshotRecord | undefined;
			if (!existingRecord?.workspace) return;
			const ws = existingRecord.workspace;
			const habitMap = new Map(ws.habits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));

			if (isClearState) {
				ws.entries = ws.entries.filter((e) => e.dateIso !== input.dateIso);
				ws.logs = ws.logs.filter((l) => l.dateIso !== input.dateIso);
			} else {
				const nextEntry = {
					id: `entry:${input.dateIso}`,
					dateIso: input.dateIso,
					dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
					reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
					journalText: input.journalText ?? null,
					tags: input.tags,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
				const entryIdx = ws.entries.findIndex((e) => e.dateIso === input.dateIso);
				if (entryIdx >= 0) {
					ws.entries[entryIdx] = nextEntry;
				} else {
					ws.entries = [nextEntry, ...ws.entries].sort((a, b) =>
						b.dateIso > a.dateIso ? 1 : b.dateIso < a.dateIso ? -1 : 0
					);
				}

				const otherLogs = ws.logs.filter((l) => l.dateIso !== input.dateIso);
				const dayLogs = input.logs.map((l) => {
					const h = habitMap.get(l.habitId);
					return {
						id: `log:${input.dateIso}:${l.habitId}`,
						dateIso: input.dateIso,
						habitId: l.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot ?? h?.title ?? null,
						achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot ?? null,
						habitSnapshotJson: h ? serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(h) : null,
						completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
						updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso ?? nowIso
					};
				});
				ws.logs = [...dayLogs, ...otherLogs];
			}

			memoryWorkspaceCache = ws;
			snapshots.put({
				key: 'workspace:full',
				updatedAtIso: nowIso,
				workspace: ws
			} satisfies WorkspaceSnapshotRecord);
		};

		tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
		tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
	});
}

export async function getCachedDayFromIdb(
	dateIso: string
): Promise<LocalDayCacheRecord | null> {
	if (!browser) return null;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction([SNAPSHOT_STORE, OUTBOX_STORE], 'readonly');
			const snapshotStore = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const outboxStore = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);

			const snapReq = snapshotStore.get(`day:${dateIso}`);
			const outboxReq = outboxStore.get(`daily:${dateIso}`);

			let snapResult: LocalDayCacheRecord | null = null;
			let outboxResult: SyncOutboxMutation | null = null;

			snapReq.onsuccess = () => {
				snapResult = (snapReq.result as LocalDayCacheRecord) ?? null;
			};
			outboxReq.onsuccess = () => {
				outboxResult = (outboxReq.result as SyncOutboxMutation) ?? null;
			};

			tx.oncomplete = () => {
				if (outboxResult && outboxResult.kind === 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY') {
					if (!snapResult || outboxResult.queuedAtIso >= snapResult.updatedAtIso) {
						resolve({
							key: `day:${dateIso}`,
							dateIso,
							updatedAtIso: outboxResult.queuedAtIso,
							payload: outboxResult.payload
						});
						return;
					}
				}
				resolve(snapResult);
			};

			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}

export async function getAllCachedDaysFromIdb(): Promise<LocalDayCacheRecord[]> {
	if (!browser) return [];
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readonly');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const req = store.getAll();
			req.onsuccess = () => {
				const allRecords = (req.result as Array<{ key?: string }>) ?? [];
				const dayRecords = allRecords.filter(
					(r): r is LocalDayCacheRecord =>
						typeof r.key === 'string' && r.key.startsWith('day:')
				);
				resolve(dayRecords);
			};
			req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
		});
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}

export async function saveRemoteSnapshotsToIdb(params: {
	entries: Array<{
		dateIso: string;
		dayStatus: 'optimal' | 'partial' | 'failed';
		reflectionNote: string;
		journalText: string | null;
		tags: string[];
		updatedAtIso: string;
	}>;
	logs: Array<{
		habitId: string;
		dateIso: string;
		habitTitleSnapshot: string | null;
		achievedTier: number | null;
		achievedRuleSnapshot: string | null;
		completedIntervals: number | null;
		actualMinutesPerInterval: number | null;
		totalActualMinutes: number | null;
		plannedIntervalsSnapshot: number | null;
		plannedMinutesSnapshot: number | null;
		updatedAtIso: string | null;
	}>;
}): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		const logsByDate = new Map<string, HabitLogItemInput[]>();
		for (const l of params.logs) {
			const list = logsByDate.get(l.dateIso) ?? [];
			list.push({
				habitId: l.habitId,
				habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot,
				achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
				achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot,
				completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
				actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
				totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
				plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
				plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
				updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso
			});
			logsByDate.set(l.dateIso, list);
		}

		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction([SNAPSHOT_STORE, OUTBOX_STORE], 'readwrite');
			const snapshots = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const outbox = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);

			for (const entry of params.entries) {
				const outboxCheck = outbox.get(`daily:${entry.dateIso}`);
				outboxCheck.onsuccess = () => {
					if (outboxCheck.result) return;
					const dayLogs = logsByDate.get(entry.dateIso) ?? [];
					const record: LocalDayCacheRecord = {
						key: `day:${entry.dateIso}`,
						dateIso: entry.dateIso,
						updatedAtIso: entry.updatedAtIso,
						payload: {
							dateIso: entry.dateIso,
							dayStatus: entry.dayStatus,
							reflectionNote: entry.reflectionNote,
							journalText: entry.journalText,
							tags: entry.tags,
							updatedAtIso: entry.updatedAtIso,
							logs: dayLogs
						}
					};
					snapshots.put(record);
				};
			}

			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore IndexedDB quota/private mode errors
	}
}

export async function getAllOutboxMutations(): Promise<SyncOutboxMutation[]> {
	if (!browser) return [];
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(OUTBOX_STORE, 'readonly');
			const store = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);
			const req = store.getAll();
			req.onsuccess = () => resolve((req.result as SyncOutboxMutation[]) ?? []);
			req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
		});
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}

export async function removeAppliedOutboxMutations(
	appliedMutations: Array<{ id: string; queuedAtIso: string }>
): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser || appliedMutations.length === 0) return;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(OUTBOX_STORE, 'readwrite');
			const store = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);

			for (const item of appliedMutations) {
				const getReq = store.get(item.id);
				getReq.onsuccess = () => {
					const current = getReq.result as SyncOutboxMutation | undefined;
					if (current && current.queuedAtIso <= item.queuedAtIso) {
						store.delete(item.id);
					}
				};
			}

			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
}

export async function getSyncMetaFromIdb(): Promise<SyncMetaRecord> {
	const fallback: SyncMetaRecord = {
		key: 'meta:sync',
		clientId: browser ? (localStorage.getItem('newlife_client_id') ?? '') : '',
		knownServerVersionIso: null,
		lastSyncedAtIso: null
	};

	if (!browser) return fallback;

	if (!fallback.clientId) {
		fallback.clientId = crypto.randomUUID();
		try {
			localStorage.setItem('newlife_client_id', fallback.clientId);
		} catch {
			// ignore
		}
	}

	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		return new Promise((resolve) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readonly');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const req = store.get('meta:sync');
			req.onsuccess = () => {
				if (req.result) {
					resolve(req.result as SyncMetaRecord);
				} else {
					resolve(fallback);
				}
			};
			req.onerror = () => resolve(fallback);
		});
	} catch {
		return fallback;
	}
}

export async function saveSyncMetaToIdb(meta: SyncMetaRecord): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readwrite');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			store.put(meta);
			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
}

function rebasePendingMutationsOnWorkspace(
	targetWorkspace: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot,
	pendingMutations: SyncOutboxMutation[]
): void {
	for (const m of pendingMutations) {
		if (m.kind === 'UPSERT_BOOK') {
			const idx = targetWorkspace.books.findIndex((b) => b.id === m.payload.id);
			if (idx >= 0) targetWorkspace.books[idx] = m.payload;
			else targetWorkspace.books = [m.payload, ...targetWorkspace.books];
		} else if (m.kind === 'DELETE_BOOK') {
			targetWorkspace.books = targetWorkspace.books.filter((b) => b.id !== m.payload.bookId);
		} else if (m.kind === 'UPSERT_MEDIA') {
			const idx = targetWorkspace.media.findIndex((item) => item.id === m.payload.id);
			if (idx >= 0) targetWorkspace.media[idx] = m.payload;
			else targetWorkspace.media = [m.payload, ...targetWorkspace.media];
		} else if (m.kind === 'DELETE_MEDIA') {
			targetWorkspace.media = targetWorkspace.media.filter((item) => item.id !== m.payload.mediaId);
		} else if (m.kind === 'UPSERT_NOTE') {
			const idx = targetWorkspace.notes.findIndex((n) => n.id === m.payload.id);
			if (idx >= 0) targetWorkspace.notes[idx] = m.payload;
			else targetWorkspace.notes = [m.payload, ...targetWorkspace.notes];
		} else if (m.kind === 'DELETE_NOTE') {
			targetWorkspace.notes = targetWorkspace.notes.filter((n) => n.id !== m.payload.noteId);
		} else if (m.kind === 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT') {
			const idx = targetWorkspace.calendarEvents.findIndex((e) => e.id === m.payload.id);
			if (idx >= 0) targetWorkspace.calendarEvents[idx] = m.payload;
			else targetWorkspace.calendarEvents = [m.payload, ...targetWorkspace.calendarEvents];
		} else if (m.kind === 'DELETE_CALENDAR_EVENT') {
			targetWorkspace.calendarEvents = targetWorkspace.calendarEvents.filter(
				(e) => e.id !== m.payload.eventId
			);
		} else if (m.kind === 'UPSERT_HABIT') {
			const idx = targetWorkspace.habits.findIndex((h) => h.id === m.payload.id);
			if (idx >= 0) targetWorkspace.habits[idx] = m.payload;
			else targetWorkspace.habits = [...targetWorkspace.habits, m.payload];
		} else if (m.kind === 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT') {
			const exists = targetWorkspace.lifecycleEvents.some((e) => e.id === m.payload.id);
			if (!exists) {
				targetWorkspace.lifecycleEvents = [m.payload, ...targetWorkspace.lifecycleEvents];
			}
		}
	}
}

export async function saveFullWorkspaceToIdb(
	workspace: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot
): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();

		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction([SNAPSHOT_STORE, OUTBOX_STORE], 'readwrite');
			const snapshots = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const outbox = tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE);

			const outboxReq = outbox.getAll();

			outboxReq.onsuccess = () => {
				const pendingMutations = (outboxReq.result as SyncOutboxMutation[]) ?? [];
				const mergedWorkspace: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot = {
					...workspace,
					habits: [...workspace.habits],
					books: [...workspace.books],
					media: [...workspace.media],
					notes: [...workspace.notes],
					calendarEvents: [...workspace.calendarEvents],
					lifecycleEvents: [...workspace.lifecycleEvents]
				};

				rebasePendingMutationsOnWorkspace(mergedWorkspace, pendingMutations);
				memoryWorkspaceCache = mergedWorkspace;

				snapshots.put({
					key: 'workspace:full',
					updatedAtIso: mergedWorkspace.syncedAtIso,
					workspace: mergedWorkspace
				} satisfies WorkspaceSnapshotRecord);

				const logsByDate = new Map<string, HabitLogItemInput[]>();
				for (const l of mergedWorkspace.logs) {
					const list = logsByDate.get(l.dateIso) ?? [];
					list.push({
						habitId: l.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot,
						achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot,
						completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
						updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso
					});
					logsByDate.set(l.dateIso, list);
				}

				for (const entry of mergedWorkspace.entries) {
					const hasPendingDaily = pendingMutations.some(
						(m) => m.kind === 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY' && m.payload.dateIso === entry.dateIso
					);
					if (hasPendingDaily) continue;

					const dayLogs = logsByDate.get(entry.dateIso) ?? [];
					snapshots.put({
						key: `day:${entry.dateIso}`,
						dateIso: entry.dateIso,
						updatedAtIso: entry.updatedAtIso,
						payload: {
							dateIso: entry.dateIso,
							dayStatus: entry.dayStatus,
							reflectionNote: entry.reflectionNote,
							journalText: entry.journalText,
							tags: entry.tags,
							updatedAtIso: entry.updatedAtIso,
							logs: dayLogs
						}
					} satisfies LocalDayCacheRecord);
				}
			};

			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore IndexedDB quota errors
	}
}

export async function patchLocalWorkspaceInIdb(
	mutator: (ws: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot) => void
): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	const ws = await getFullWorkspaceFromIdb();
	if (!ws) return;
	mutator(ws);
	ws.syncedAtIso = new Date().toISOString();
	memoryWorkspaceCache = ws;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readwrite');
			tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE).put({
				key: 'workspace:full',
				updatedAtIso: ws.syncedAtIso,
				workspace: ws
			} satisfies WorkspaceSnapshotRecord);
			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
}

export async function getFullWorkspaceFromIdb(): Promise<LocalWorkspaceSnapshot | null> {
	if (!browser) return null;
	if (memoryWorkspaceCache) return memoryWorkspaceCache;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		return new Promise((resolve) => {
			const tx = db.transaction(SNAPSHOT_STORE, 'readonly');
			const store = tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE);
			const req = store.get('workspace:full');
			req.onsuccess = () => {
				const rec = req.result as WorkspaceSnapshotRecord | undefined;
				if (rec?.workspace) {
					memoryWorkspaceCache = rec.workspace;
					resolve(rec.workspace);
				} else {
					resolve(null);
				}
			};
			req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
		});
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}

export async function buildOfflineDayCommandPayload(
	dateIso: string
): Promise<DayCommandPayload | null> {
	const [ws, cachedDay, allCachedDays] = await Promise.all([
		getFullWorkspaceFromIdb(),
		getCachedDayFromIdb(dateIso),
		getAllCachedDaysFromIdb()
	]);

	if (!ws && !cachedDay && allCachedDays.length === 0) return null;

	const tomorrowIso = shiftIsoDate(dateIso, 1);
	const habits: HabitEntity[] = ws?.habits ?? [];
	const availableTags =
		ws?.availableTags && ws.availableTags.length > 0
			? ws.availableTags
			: [...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS];

	const recentMap = new Map<string, AnalyticsRawEntry>();
	if (ws) {
		for (const e of ws.entries) {
			recentMap.set(e.dateIso, {
				dateIso: e.dateIso,
				dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
				reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
				tags: e.tags
			});
		}
	}
	for (const rec of allCachedDays) {
		recentMap.set(rec.dateIso, {
			dateIso: rec.dateIso,
			dayStatus: rec.payload.dayStatus,
			reflectionNote: rec.payload.reflectionNote,
			tags: rec.payload.tags
		});
	}

	const recentEntries = Array.from(recentMap.values())
		.sort((a, b) => (b.dateIso > a.dateIso ? 1 : b.dateIso < a.dateIso ? -1 : 0))
		.slice(0, 60);

	const wsEntry = ws?.entries.find((e) => e.dateIso === dateIso) ?? null;
	const wsLogs = ws?.logs.filter((l) => l.dateIso === dateIso) ?? [];

	const useCachedDay =
		cachedDay &&
		(!wsEntry || cachedDay.updatedAtIso >= wsEntry.updatedAtIso);

	const entry = useCachedDay
		? {
				id: `entry:${dateIso}`,
				dateIso,
				dayStatus: cachedDay.payload.dayStatus,
				reflectionNote: cachedDay.payload.reflectionNote,
				journalText: cachedDay.payload.journalText ?? null,
				tags: cachedDay.payload.tags,
				updatedAtIso: cachedDay.updatedAtIso
			}
		: wsEntry;

	const logs = useCachedDay
		? cachedDay.payload.logs.map((l) => ({
				id: `log:${dateIso}:${l.habitId}`,
				dateIso,
				habitId: l.habitId,
				habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot ?? null,
				achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
				achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot ?? null,
				completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
				actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
				totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
				plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
				plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
				updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso ?? cachedDay.updatedAtIso
			}))
		: wsLogs;

	const dayCadencePlans =
		ws?.cadencePlans.filter((p) => p.dateIso === dateIso) ?? [];
	const tomorrowCadencePlans =
		ws?.cadencePlans.filter((p) => p.dateIso === tomorrowIso) ?? [];

	const { todayAndUpcoming, selectedDayEvents } = computeUpcomingEventsBanner({
		allEvents: ws?.calendarEvents ?? [],
		referenceDateIso: dateIso,
		todayIso: getTodayIso(),
		horizonDays: 7
	});

	return {
		dateIso,
		tomorrowIso,
		habits,
		availableTags,
		entry,
		logs,
		dayCadencePlans,
		tomorrowCadencePlans,
		recentEntries,
		upcomingBannerItems: todayAndUpcoming,
		selectedDayEvents
	};
}

export async function buildOfflineCalendarMonthPayload(
	jy: number,
	jm: number,
	selectedIso?: string
) {
	const ws = await getFullWorkspaceFromIdb();
	if (!ws) return null;

	const todayIso = getTodayIso();
	const { todayAndUpcoming } = computeUpcomingEventsBanner({
		allEvents: ws.calendarEvents,
		referenceDateIso: todayIso,
		todayIso,
		horizonDays: 14
	});

	return assembleJalaliMonthArchive({
		jy,
		jm,
		todayIso,
		selectedIso,
		allHabits: ws.habits,
		entriesInMonth: ws.entries.map((e) => ({
			dateIso: e.dateIso,
			dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
			reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
			journalText: e.journalText,
			tagsJson: JSON.stringify(e.tags)
		})),
		logsInMonth: ws.logs as AnalyticsRawLog[],
		eventsInMonth: ws.calendarEvents,
		booksInMonth: ws.books,
		mediaInMonth: ws.media,
		upcomingEvents: todayAndUpcoming
	});
}

export async function buildOfflineAnalyticsDatasetPayload(
	initialHabitId: string | null,
	initialRange: AnalyticsTimeRange
) {
	const ws = await getFullWorkspaceFromIdb();
	const fallbackEndDateIso = getYesterdayIso();

	if (!ws) {
		return {
			habits: [],
			entries: [],
			logs: [],
			lifecycleEvents: [],
			fallbackEndDateIso,
			initialHabitId,
			initialRange
		};
	}

	return {
		habits: ws.habits,
		entries: ws.entries.map((e) => ({
			dateIso: e.dateIso,
			dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
			reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
			tags: e.tags
		})),
		logs: ws.logs as AnalyticsRawLog[],
		lifecycleEvents: ws.lifecycleEvents,
		fallbackEndDateIso,
		initialHabitId,
		initialRange
	};
}

export async function buildOfflineJournalMemories(): Promise<JournalMemoryItem[] | null> {
	const ws = await getFullWorkspaceFromIdb();
	if (!ws) return null;

	return ws.entries
		.filter((e) => e.journalText && e.journalText.trim().length > 0)
		.map((e) => ({
			id: e.id,
			dateIso: e.dateIso,
			dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
			journalText: e.journalText!.trim(),
			tags: e.tags,
			updatedAtIso: e.updatedAtIso
		}))
		.sort((a, b) => (b.dateIso > a.dateIso ? 1 : b.dateIso < a.dateIso ? -1 : 0));
}

export async function clearLocalClientData(): Promise<void> {
	if (!browser) return;
	memoryWorkspaceCache = null;
	try {
		const db = await openLocalDatabase();
		await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
			const tx = db.transaction([OUTBOX_STORE, SNAPSHOT_STORE], 'readwrite');
			tx.objectStore(OUTBOX_STORE).clear();
			tx.objectStore(SNAPSHOT_STORE).clear();
			tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
			tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
		});
	} catch {
		// ignore IndexedDB errors
	}
}

PATH: src/lib/state/client-db.ts
