import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { invalidateAll } from '$app/navigation';
import {
	getTodayIso,
	isoToJalaliParts,
	resolveCompletedRangeDates
} from '@domain/calendar';
import { DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY } from '@domain/constants';
import type {
	BookEntity,
	BookStatus,
	CalendarEventEntity,
	CreateBookInput,
	CreateCalendarEventInput,
	CreateHabitInput,
	CreateMediaInput,
	HabitEntity,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	MediaEntity,
	MediaStatus,
	MutateHabitStructureInput,
	NoteEntity,
	SaveCadencePlanBatch,
	UpdateBookInput,
	UpdateMediaInput,
	UpsertNoteInput
} from '@domain/schemas';
import { stageMutationInIdb } from './idb-sync-store';
import { syncEngine } from './sync-engine.svelte';

export const clientDb = {
	books: {
		async create(input: CreateBookInput): Promise<BookEntity> {
			const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
			});

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const newBook: BookEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: input.title.trim(),
				author: input.author?.trim() || null,
				status: input.status,
				startedAtIso,
				finishedAtIso,
				rating: input.rating,
				notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:book:${newBook.id}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newBook
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.books = [newBook, ...ws.books.filter((b) => b.id !== newBook.id)];
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
			return newBook;
		},

		async update(input: UpdateBookInput): Promise<void> {
			const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
			});

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:book:${input.bookId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: input.bookId,
						title: input.title.trim(),
						author: input.author?.trim() || null,
						status: input.status,
						startedAtIso,
						finishedAtIso,
						rating: input.rating,
						notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const idx = ws.books.findIndex((b) => b.id === input.bookId);
					if (idx >= 0) {
						ws.books[idx] = {
							...ws.books[idx],
							title: input.title.trim(),
							author: input.author?.trim() || null,
							status: input.status,
							startedAtIso,
							finishedAtIso,
							rating: input.rating,
							notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
							updatedAtIso: nowIso
						};
					}
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async updateStatus(bookId: string, status: BookStatus): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const finishedAtIso = status === 'completed' ? getTodayIso() : null;

			let targetBook: BookEntity | undefined;

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:book:${bookId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: bookId,
						title: '',
						author: null,
						status,
						startedAtIso: null,
						finishedAtIso,
						rating: 5,
						notes: null,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const book = ws.books.find((b) => b.id === bookId);
					if (book) {
						book.status = status;
						book.finishedAtIso = finishedAtIso;
						book.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
						targetBook = book;
					}
				}
			);

			if (targetBook) {
				await stageMutationInIdb({
					id: `mutation:book:${bookId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: targetBook
				});
			}

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async delete(bookId: string): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:book_del:${bookId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'DELETE_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: { bookId }
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.books = ws.books.filter((b) => b.id !== bookId);
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		}
	},

	media: {
		async create(input: CreateMediaInput): Promise<MediaEntity> {
			const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
			});

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const newMedia: MediaEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: input.title.trim(),
				category: input.category,
				status: input.status,
				startedAtIso,
				finishedAtIso,
				rating: input.rating,
				notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:media:${newMedia.id}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_MEDIA',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newMedia
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.media = [newMedia, ...ws.media.filter((m) => m.id !== newMedia.id)];
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
			return newMedia;
		},

		async update(input: UpdateMediaInput): Promise<void> {
			const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed'
			});

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:media:${input.mediaId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_MEDIA',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: input.mediaId,
						title: input.title.trim(),
						category: input.category,
						status: input.status,
						startedAtIso,
						finishedAtIso,
						rating: input.rating,
						notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const idx = ws.media.findIndex((m) => m.id === input.mediaId);
					if (idx >= 0) {
						ws.media[idx] = {
							...ws.media[idx],
							title: input.title.trim(),
							category: input.category,
							status: input.status,
							startedAtIso,
							finishedAtIso,
							rating: input.rating,
							notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
							updatedAtIso: nowIso
						};
					}
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async updateStatus(mediaId: string, status: MediaStatus): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const finishedAtIso = status === 'completed' ? getTodayIso() : null;

			let targetMedia: MediaEntity | undefined;

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:media:${mediaId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_MEDIA',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: mediaId,
						title: '',
						category: 'movie',
						status,
						startedAtIso: null,
						finishedAtIso,
						rating: 5,
						notes: null,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const m = ws.media.find((item) => item.id === mediaId);
					if (m) {
						m.status = status;
						m.finishedAtIso = finishedAtIso;
						m.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
						targetMedia = m;
					}
				}
			);

			if (targetMedia) {
				await stageMutationInIdb({
					id: `mutation:media:${mediaId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_MEDIA',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: targetMedia
				});
			}

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async delete(mediaId: string): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:media_del:${mediaId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'DELETE_MEDIA',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: { mediaId }
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.media = ws.media.filter((m) => m.id !== mediaId);
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		}
	},

	notes: {
		async create(input: UpsertNoteInput): Promise<NoteEntity> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const newNote: NoteEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: input.title.trim(),
				content: input.content.trim(),
				category: input.category.trim() || 'عمومی',
				isPinned: false,
				createdAtIso: nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:note:${newNote.id}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_NOTE',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newNote
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.notes = [newNote, ...ws.notes.filter((n) => n.id !== newNote.id)].sort(
						(a, b) => (b.isPinned ? 1 : 0) - (a.isPinned ? 1 : 0)
					);
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
			return newNote;
		},

		async update(noteId: string, input: UpsertNoteInput): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:note:${noteId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_NOTE',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: noteId,
						title: input.title.trim(),
						content: input.content.trim(),
						category: input.category.trim() || 'عمومی',
						isPinned: false,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const note = ws.notes.find((n) => n.id === noteId);
					if (note) {
						note.title = input.title.trim();
						note.content = input.content.trim();
						note.category = input.category.trim() || 'عمومی';
						note.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
					}
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async togglePin(noteId: string, isPinned: boolean): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:note_pin:${noteId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_NOTE',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: noteId,
						title: '',
						content: '',
						category: 'عمومی',
						isPinned,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const note = ws.notes.find((n) => n.id === noteId);
					if (note) {
						note.isPinned = isPinned;
						note.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
						ws.notes.sort((a, b) => (b.isPinned ? 1 : 0) - (a.isPinned ? 1 : 0));
					}
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async delete(noteId: string): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:note_del:${noteId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'DELETE_NOTE',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: { noteId }
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.notes = ws.notes.filter((n) => n.id !== noteId);
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		}
	},

	calendar: {
		async create(input: CreateCalendarEventInput): Promise<CalendarEventEntity> {
			const parts = isoToJalaliParts(input.dateIso);
			const isAnnual = Boolean(input.isAnnual);
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			const newEvent: CalendarEventEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: input.title.trim(),
				eventType: input.eventType,
				isAnnual,
				dateIso: isAnnual ? null : input.dateIso,
				jalaliMonth: parts.jm,
				jalaliDay: parts.jd,
				isCompleted: false,
				notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:event:${newEvent.id}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newEvent
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.calendarEvents = [newEvent, ...ws.calendarEvents.filter((e) => e.id !== newEvent.id)];
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
			return newEvent;
		},

		async toggleComplete(eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:event:${eventId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: eventId,
						title: '',
						eventType: 'occasion',
						isAnnual: false,
						dateIso: null,
						jalaliMonth: 1,
						jalaliDay: 1,
						isCompleted,
						notes: null,
						createdAtIso: nowIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const ev = ws.calendarEvents.find((e) => e.id === eventId);
					if (ev) {
						ev.isCompleted = isCompleted;
						ev.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
					}
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async delete(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:event_del:${eventId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'DELETE_CALENDAR_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: { eventId }
				},
				(ws) => {
					ws.calendarEvents = ws.calendarEvents.filter((e) => e.id !== eventId);
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		}
	},

	habits: {
		async create(input: CreateHabitInput): Promise<HabitEntity> {
			const todayIso = getTodayIso();
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			let newHabit: HabitEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				parentId: input.parentId ?? null,
				title: input.title.trim(),
				category: input.category.trim() || DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY,
				trackingMode: input.trackingMode,
				timeSlot: input.timeSlot ?? 'day',
				isSituational: input.isSituational ?? false,
				status: 'active',
				scheduleLabel: input.scheduleLabel?.trim() || null,
				notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
				bronzeDesc: input.bronzeDesc?.trim() || null,
				silverDesc: input.silverDesc?.trim() || null,
				goldDesc: input.goldDesc?.trim() || null,
				emergencyMinDesc: input.emergencyMinDesc?.trim() || null,
				defaultIntervals: input.defaultIntervals,
				defaultMinutesPerInterval: input.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
				cadenceWindowMinutes: input.cadenceWindowMinutes,
				sortOrder: 0,
				createdAtIso: todayIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			const lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				habitId: newHabit.id,
				habitTitleSnapshot: newHabit.title,
				eventType: newHabit.parentId ? 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT' : 'CREATED',
				effectiveDateIso: todayIso,
				summary: newHabit.parentId
					? `ایجاد زیرطرح «${newHabit.title}»`
					: `ایجاد طرح «${newHabit.title}»`,
				reason: input.reason?.trim() || 'تعریف طرح جدید',
				previousStateJson: null,
				newStateJson: JSON.stringify(newHabit),
				createdAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:habit:${newHabit.id}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_HABIT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newHabit
				},
				(ws) => {
					const maxOrder = ws.habits.reduce((max, h) => Math.max(max, h.sortOrder), 0);
					newHabit.sortOrder = maxOrder + 1;
					ws.habits = [...ws.habits, newHabit].sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);
					ws.lifecycleEvents = [lifecycleEvent, ...ws.lifecycleEvents];
				}
			);

			await stageMutationInIdb({
				id: `mutation:lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}:${Date.now()}`,
				kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
				queuedAtIso: nowIso,
				payload: lifecycleEvent
			});

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
			return newHabit;
		},

		async update(input: MutateHabitStructureInput): Promise<void> {
			const todayIso = getTodayIso();
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			let updatedHabit: HabitEntity | undefined;
			let lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity | undefined;

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:habit:${input.habitId}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_HABIT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: {
						id: input.habitId,
						parentId: input.parentId,
						title: input.title,
						category: input.category,
						trackingMode: 'tiered',
						timeSlot: input.timeSlot ?? 'day',
						isSituational: input.isSituational,
						status: input.status,
						scheduleLabel: input.scheduleLabel,
						notes: input.notes,
						bronzeDesc: input.bronzeDesc,
						silverDesc: input.silverDesc,
						goldDesc: input.goldDesc,
						emergencyMinDesc: input.emergencyMinDesc,
						defaultIntervals: 1,
						defaultMinutesPerInterval: 10,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: 60,
						sortOrder: 0,
						createdAtIso: todayIso,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					}
				},
				(ws) => {
					const current = ws.habits.find((h) => h.id === input.habitId);
					if (!current) return;

					let eventType: LifecycleEventEntity['eventType'] = 'UPDATED_RULES';
					let summary = `ویرایش شرایط طرح «${input.title}»`;

					if (current.parentId !== input.parentId) {
						if (input.parentId) {
							eventType = 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT';
							const parent = ws.habits.find((h) => h.id === input.parentId);
							summary = `تبدیل «${input.title}» به زیرطرحِ «${parent?.title ?? 'والد'}»`;
						} else {
							eventType = 'PROMOTED_TO_ROOT';
							summary = `تبدیل «${input.title}» به طرح مستقل`;
						}
					} else if (current.status !== input.status) {
						eventType = input.status === 'archived' ? 'DELETED' : 'STATUS_CHANGED';
						summary = `تغییر وضعیت «${input.title}» به ${input.status}`;
					}

					lifecycleEvent = {
						id: crypto.randomUUID(),
						habitId: input.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: input.title,
						eventType,
						effectiveDateIso: todayIso,
						summary,
						reason: input.reason,
						previousStateJson: JSON.stringify(current),
						newStateJson: JSON.stringify(input),
						createdAtIso: nowIso
					};

					current.title = input.title;
					current.category = input.category.trim() || current.category;
					current.parentId = input.parentId;
					current.timeSlot = input.timeSlot ?? current.timeSlot;
					current.isSituational = input.isSituational;
					current.status = input.status;
					current.scheduleLabel = input.scheduleLabel;
					current.notes = input.notes;
					current.bronzeDesc = input.bronzeDesc;
					current.silverDesc = input.silverDesc;
					current.goldDesc = input.goldDesc;
					current.emergencyMinDesc = input.emergencyMinDesc;
					current.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
					updatedHabit = current;

					ws.lifecycleEvents = [lifecycleEvent, ...ws.lifecycleEvents];
				}
			);

			if (lifecycleEvent) {
				await stageMutationInIdb({
					id: `mutation:lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: lifecycleEvent
				});
			}

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		},

		async saveTomorrowPlans(input: SaveCadencePlanBatch): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			await stageMutationInIdb(
				{
					id: `mutation:cadence:${input.targetDateIso}:${Date.now()}`,
					kind: 'SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: input
				},
				(ws) => {
					for (const item of input.items) {
						const planIdx = ws.cadencePlans.findIndex(
							(p) => p.dateIso === input.targetDateIso && p.habitId === item.habitId
						);
						const nextPlan = {
							id: `plan:${input.targetDateIso}:${item.habitId}`,
							dateIso: input.targetDateIso,
							habitId: item.habitId,
							plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
							plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
							cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
							updatedAtIso: nowIso
						};
						if (planIdx >= 0) {
							ws.cadencePlans[planIdx] = nextPlan;
						} else {
							ws.cadencePlans.push(nextPlan);
						}

						const habit = ws.habits.find((h) => h.id === item.habitId);
						if (
							habit &&
							(habit.defaultIntervals !== item.plannedIntervals ||
								habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval !== item.plannedMinutesPerInterval ||
								habit.cadenceWindowMinutes !== item.cadenceWindowMinutes)
						) {
							habit.defaultIntervals = item.plannedIntervals;
							habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval = item.plannedMinutesPerInterval;
							habit.cadenceWindowMinutes = item.cadenceWindowMinutes;
							habit.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
						}
					}
				}
			);

			void syncEngine.notifyLocalMutationStaged();
			if (browser) void invalidateAll();
		}
	}
};

<script lang="ts">
	import type { Snippet } from 'svelte';
	import { fade, scale } from 'svelte/transition';
	import { X } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		title = '',
		subtitle = '',
		maxWidthClass = 'max-w-lg',
		onClose,
		headerIcon,
		headerContent,
		children
	}: {
		title?: string;
		subtitle?: string;
		maxWidthClass?: string;
		onClose: () => void;
		headerIcon?: Snippet;
		headerContent?: Snippet;
		children: Snippet;
	} = $props();

	function handleKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
		if (e.key === 'Escape') {
			e.preventDefault();
			onClose();
		}
	}
</script>

<svelte:window onkeydown={handleKeydown} />

<div
	transition:fade={{ duration: 150 }}
	class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center bg-overlay p-2.5 backdrop-blur-xs sm:items-center sm:p-5"
	role="dialog"
	aria-modal="true"
	tabindex="-1"
	onclick={(e) => {
		if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
	}}
	onkeydown={handleKeydown}
>
	<div
		in:scale={{ start: 0.96, duration: 160 }}
		class="flex max-h-[90dvh] w-full {maxWidthClass} flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface shadow-(--shadow-modal)"
	>
		<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-border px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
			{#if headerContent}
				{@render headerContent()}
			{:else}
				<div class="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
					{#if headerIcon}
						<div
							class="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
						>
							{@render headerIcon()}
						</div>
					{/if}
					<div class="min-w-0">
						<h2 class="truncate text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">{title}</h2>
						{#if subtitle}
							<p class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">{subtitle}</p>
						{/if}
					</div>
				</div>
			{/if}

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={onClose}
				class="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-elevated text-fg-secondary transition hover:text-fg"
				aria-label="بستن"
			>
				<X class="h-4 w-4" />
			</button>
		</div>

		{@render children()}
	</div>
</div>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/ui/CustomSelect.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { fly } from 'svelte/transition';
	import { Check, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-svelte';

	export interface SelectOption {
		value: string | number;
		label: string;
	}

	let {
		id,
		name,
		value = $bindable(),
		options,
		disabled = false,
		triggerClass = '',
		placeholder = 'انتخاب کنید',
		onSelect
	}: {
		id?: string;
		name?: string;
		value: string | number;
		options: ReadonlyArray<SelectOption>;
		disabled?: boolean;
		triggerClass?: string;
		placeholder?: string;
		onSelect?: (newVal: string | number) => void;
	} = $props();

	let isOpen = $state(false);
	let containerEl = $state<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

	let selectedOption = $derived(
		options.find((opt) => String(opt.value) === String(value)) ?? options[0]
	);

	function toggleDropdown() {
		if (disabled) return;
		isOpen = !isOpen;
	}

	function selectOption(opt: SelectOption) {
		value = opt.value;
		isOpen = false;
		onSelect?.(opt.value);
	}

	function handleWindowClick(e: MouseEvent) {
		if (!isOpen || !containerEl) return;
		if (!containerEl.contains(e.target as Node)) {
			isOpen = false;
		}
	}

	function handleKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
		if (disabled) return;
		if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
			e.preventDefault();
			isOpen = false;
			return;
		}
		if (!isOpen && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'ArrowDown')) {
			e.preventDefault();
			isOpen = true;
			return;
		}
		if (isOpen && (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp')) {
			e.preventDefault();
			const currentIndex = options.findIndex((o) => String(o.value) === String(value));
			const targetIndex =
				e.key === 'ArrowDown'
					? (currentIndex + 1) % options.length
					: (currentIndex - 1 + options.length) % options.length;
			const nextVal = options[targetIndex].value;
			value = nextVal;
			onSelect?.(nextVal);
		}
	}
</script>

<svelte:window onclick={isOpen ? handleWindowClick : undefined} />

<div bind:this={containerEl} class="relative w-full">
	{#if name}
		<input type="hidden" {name} {value} />
	{/if}

	<button
		{id}
		type="button"
		{disabled}
		aria-haspopup="listbox"
		aria-expanded={isOpen}
		onclick={toggleDropdown}
		onkeydown={handleKeydown}
		class="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border px-3.5 text-right text-sm font-bold transition focus:outline-none {isOpen
			? 'border-border-strong bg-surface ring-2 ring-accent-border'
			: 'border-border bg-elevated hover:border-border-strong'} {disabled
			? 'opacity-60'
			: ''} {triggerClass}"
	>
		<span class="truncate">{selectedOption ? selectedOption.label : placeholder}</span>
		<ChevronDown
			class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-fg-muted transition-transform duration-200 {isOpen
				? 'rotate-180 text-accent-text'
				: ''}"
		/>
	</button>

	{#if isOpen}
		<div
			role="listbox"
			transition:fly={{ y: -6, duration: 150 }}
			class="absolute right-0 left-0 z-50 mt-2 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-border-strong bg-surface p-1.5 shadow-(--shadow-modal)"
		>
			{#each options as opt (opt.value)}
				{@const isSelected = String(opt.value) === String(value)}
				<button
					type="button"
					role="option"
					aria-selected={isSelected}
					onclick={() => selectOption(opt)}
					class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-right text-xs font-extrabold transition {isSelected
						? 'bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
						: 'text-fg hover:bg-elevated'}"
				>
					<span class="truncate">{opt.label}</span>
					{#if isSelected}
						<Check class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-accent-text" />
					{/if}
				</button>
			{/each}
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

<script lang="ts">

	let {
		report
	}: {
		report: SelectedHabitAnalytics;
	} = $props();

	let isCadence = $derived(report.habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence');
</script>

<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 sm:gap-4">
	<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-card) sm:p-5">

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/analytics/HabitTrendChart.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import {
		CHART_DIMENSIONS,
		buildRollingAreaPath,
		buildRollingPolylinePoints,
		computeMaxCadenceMinutes,
		getChartX as getX,
		getChartYFromMinutes,
		getChartYFromScore as getYFromScore
	} from '@core/chart-math';
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { getOutcomeBadgeMeta } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { HabitDailyPoint, SelectedHabitAnalytics } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { GitCommitHorizontal, Info, Sparkles } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		report
	}: {
		report: SelectedHabitAnalytics;
	} = $props();

	let manualPointSelection = $state<{ habitId: string; index: number } | null>(null);

	let activePointIndex = $derived.by<number | null>(() => {
		const series = report.dailySeries;
		if (series.length === 0) return null;

		if (
			manualPointSelection &&
			manualPointSelection.habitId === report.habit.id &&
			manualPointSelection.index >= 0 &&
			manualPointSelection.index < series.length
		) {
			return manualPointSelection.index;
		}

		for (let i = series.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
			if (series[i].outcome !== 'unlogged') {
				return i;
			}
		}
		return series.length - 1;
	});

	function selectPoint(index: number) {
		manualPointSelection = { habitId: report.habit.id, index };
	}

	let isCadence = $derived(report.habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence');

	const { SVG_WIDTH, SVG_HEIGHT, PAD_TOP, PAD_LEFT, PAD_RIGHT, PLOT_W, PLOT_H } =
		CHART_DIMENSIONS;

	let maxCadenceMinutes = $derived(
		isCadence
			? computeMaxCadenceMinutes(
					report.dailySeries,
					report.habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
				)
			: 100
	);

	function getYFromMinutes(minutes: number): number {
		return getChartYFromMinutes(minutes, maxCadenceMinutes);
	}

	let rollingPolylinePoints = $derived(
		buildRollingPolylinePoints(report.dailySeries, isCadence, maxCadenceMinutes)
	);

	let rollingAreaPath = $derived(
		buildRollingAreaPath(report.dailySeries, isCadence, maxCadenceMinutes)
	);

	let inspectedPoint = $derived<HabitDailyPoint | null>(
		activePointIndex !== null ? (report.dailySeries[activePointIndex] ?? null) : null
	);

	function getBarColorVar(outcome: HabitDailyPoint['outcome']): string {
		if (outcome === 'gold') return 'var(--tier-gold)';
		if (outcome === 'silver') return 'var(--tier-silver)';
		if (outcome === 'bronze') return 'var(--tier-bronze)';
		if (outcome === 'failed') return 'var(--state-failed)';
		if (outcome === 'skipped') return 'var(--text-muted)';
		return 'var(--border-hairline)';
	}
</script>

<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
	<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
		<div>
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
				<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">
					نمودار روند زمانی و تکامل «{report.habit.title}»
				</h2>
				{#if report.parentTitle}
					<span
						class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
					>
						زیرمجموعه {report.parentTitle}
					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>
			<p class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
				{#if isCadence}
					ستون‌ها نشان‌دهنده دقایق انجام‌شده در هر روز و خط نقطه‌چین نشان‌دهنده هدف تعیین‌شده است
				{:else}
					ستون‌ها سطح کسب‌شده در هر روز و خط ممتد منحنی میانگین متحرک ۷ روزه (روند کلی) را نشان می‌دهد
				{/if}
			</p>
		</div>

		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-[11px] font-extrabold">
			<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-gold-text">
				<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-gold"></span>
				<span>{isCadence ? 'تکمیل هدف' : 'طلا'}</span>
			</span>
			{#if !isCadence && report.maxSupportedTier >= 2}
				<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-silver-text">
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-silver"></span>
					<span>نقره</span>
				</span>
			{/if}
			{#if isCadence || report.maxSupportedTier > 1}
				<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-bronze-text">
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-bronze"></span>
					<span>{isCadence ? 'کمتر از هدف' : 'برنز'}</span>
				</span>
			{/if}
			<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-failed">
				<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-failed"></span>
				<span>نشد</span>
			</span>
			<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-accent-text">
				<span class="h-0.5 w-4 rounded-full bg-accent"></span>
				<span>{isCadence ? 'خط هدف' : 'میانگین روند'}</span>
			</span>
		</div>
	</div>

	<div class="mt-5 overflow-x-auto pb-2" dir="ltr">
		<svg
			viewBox="0 0 {SVG_WIDTH} {SVG_HEIGHT}"
			class="h-60 min-w-[600px] w-full select-none overflow-visible"
			role="img"
			aria-label="نمودار روند طرح در طول زمان"
		>
			<defs>
				<linearGradient id="trendAreaGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
					<stop offset="0%" stop-color="var(--surface-accent)" stop-opacity="0.24" />
					<stop offset="100%" stop-color="var(--surface-accent)" stop-opacity="0.0" />
				</linearGradient>
			</defs>

			{#if isCadence}
				{#each [0, 0.5, 1] as ratio (ratio)}
					{@const val = Math.round(maxCadenceMinutes * ratio)}
					{@const y = getYFromMinutes(val)}
					<line
						x1={PAD_LEFT}
						y1={y}
						x2={SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT}
						y2={y}
						stroke="var(--border-hairline)"
						stroke-dasharray={ratio === 0 ? 'none' : '4 4'}
						stroke-width="1"
					/>
					<text
						x={SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT + 8}
						y={y + 4}
						fill="var(--text-muted)"
						font-size="10"
						font-weight="700"
					>
						{toPersianDigits(val)}′
					</text>
				{/each}
			{:else}
				{#each [{ score: 100, label: 'طلا' }, { score: 68, label: 'نقره' }, { score: 38, label: 'برنز' }, { score: 0, label: 'صفر' }] as grid (grid.score)}
					{@const y = getYFromScore(grid.score)}
					<line
						x1={PAD_LEFT}
						y1={y}
						x2={SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT}
						y2={y}
						stroke="var(--border-hairline)"
						stroke-dasharray={grid.score === 0 ? 'none' : '4 4'}
						stroke-width="1"
					/>
					<text
						x={SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT + 8}
						y={y + 4}
						fill="var(--text-muted)"
						font-size="10"
						font-weight="700"
					>
						{grid.label}
					</text>
				{/each}
			{/if}

			{#if rollingAreaPath}
				<path d={rollingAreaPath} fill="url(#trendAreaGrad)" />
			{/if}

			{#each report.dailySeries as pt, idx (pt.dateIso)}
				{@const total = report.dailySeries.length}
				{@const cx = getX(idx, total)}
				{@const barW = Math.max(4, Math.min(20, (PLOT_W / Math.max(1, total)) * 0.62))}
				{@const baseY = PAD_TOP + PLOT_H}
				{@const targetY = isCadence
					? pt.actualMinutes !== null && pt.actualMinutes > 0
						? getYFromMinutes(pt.actualMinutes)
						: pt.outcome === 'failed'
							? baseY - 6
							: baseY
					: pt.score !== null && pt.score > 0
						? getYFromScore(pt.score)
						: pt.outcome === 'failed'
							? baseY - 7
							: pt.outcome === 'skipped'
								? baseY - 12
								: baseY}
				{@const barH = Math.max(0, baseY - targetY)}
				{@const isSelected = activePointIndex === idx}

				<g
					class="cursor-pointer transition-opacity"
					onclick={() => selectPoint(idx)}
					onmouseenter={() => selectPoint(idx)}
					role="button"
					tabindex="0"
					aria-label="{pt.humanLabel}"
					onkeydown={(e) => {
						if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
							e.preventDefault();
							selectPoint(idx);
						}
					}}
				>
					<rect
						x={cx - Math.max(barW, 12) / 2}
						y={PAD_TOP}
						width={Math.max(barW, 12)}
						height={PLOT_H}
						fill="transparent"
					/>

					{#if isSelected}
						<line
							x1={cx}
							y1={PAD_TOP}
							x2={cx}
							y2={baseY}
							stroke="var(--surface-accent)"
							stroke-width="1.5"
							stroke-dasharray="2 2"
						/>
					{/if}

					{#if barH > 0}
						<rect
							x={cx - barW / 2}
							y={targetY}
							width={barW}
							height={barH}
							rx={Math.min(5, barW / 2)}
							fill={getBarColorVar(pt.outcome)}
							opacity={isSelected ? '1' : '0.82'}
						/>
					{:else if pt.outcome === 'unlogged'}
						<circle cx={cx} cy={baseY - 2} r="2" fill="var(--border-hairline)" />
					{/if}

					{#if pt.lifecycleEventsOnDay.length > 0}
						<circle
							cx={cx}
							cy={PAD_TOP - 10}
							r="6"
							fill="var(--surface-accent)"
							stroke="var(--bg-surface)"
							stroke-width="2"
						/>
					{/if}

					{#if idx === 0 || idx === total - 1 || idx === Math.floor(total / 2) || (total <= 14 && idx % 2 === 0)}
						<text
							x={cx}
							y={SVG_HEIGHT - 8}
							text-anchor="middle"
							fill={isSelected ? 'var(--text-primary)' : 'var(--text-muted)'}
							font-size="10"
							font-weight={isSelected ? '800' : '600'}
						>
							{toPersianDigits(pt.jalaliSlash.slice(5))}
						</text>
					{/if}
				</g>
			{/each}

			{#if rollingPolylinePoints}
				<polyline
					fill="none"
					stroke="var(--surface-accent)"
					stroke-width="2.5"
					stroke-linecap="round"
					stroke-linejoin="round"
					stroke-dasharray={isCadence ? '5 4' : 'none'}
					points={rollingPolylinePoints}
				/>
			{/if}
		</svg>
	</div>

	{#if inspectedPoint}
		{@const badge = getOutcomeBadgeMeta(inspectedPoint.outcome, report.maxSupportedTier)}
		<div
			class="mt-3 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
		>
			<div class="space-y-1">
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
					<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{inspectedPoint.humanLabel}</span>
					<span
						class="rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold {badge.badgeClass}"
					>
						{badge.label}
					</span>
					{#if !isCadence && inspectedPoint.rollingAvgScore !== null}
						<span class="text-xs font-bold text-accent-text">
							(میانگین روند ۷ روزه: %{toPersianDigits(inspectedPoint.rollingAvgScore)})
						</span>
					{/if}
				</div>

				{#if inspectedPoint.ruleSnapshot}
					<p class="text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary">
						<Info class="ml-1 inline h-3.5 w-3.5 text-fg-muted" />
						<span>قانون و وضعیت ثبت‌شده در این روز: </span>
						<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">{inspectedPoint.ruleSnapshot}</strong>
					</p>
				{:else}
					<p class="text-xs text-fg-muted">در این تاریخ عملکردی برای این طرح ثبت نشده است.</p>
				{/if}

				{#if inspectedPoint.lifecycleEventsOnDay.length > 0}
					<div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
						{#each inspectedPoint.lifecycleEventsOnDay as ev (ev.id)}
							<span
								class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text"
							>
								<GitCommitHorizontal class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
								<span>رویداد تغییر در این روز: {ev.summary}</span>
							</span>
						{/each}
					</div>
				{/if}
			</div>

			{#if inspectedPoint.dayTags.length > 0}
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
					<span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-muted">تگ‌های روز:</span>
					{#each inspectedPoint.dayTags as tag (tag)}
						<span
							class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
						>
							#{tag}
						</span>
					{/each}
				</div>
			{:else if inspectedPoint.outcome === 'gold'}
				<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-gold-text">
					<Sparkles class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>عملکرد ایده‌آل</span>
				</div>
			{/if}
		</div>
	{/if}
</section>

							</div>

									<span

<script lang="ts">
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import type { HabitDailyPoint, SelectedHabitAnalytics } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { Calendar, Hash, Layers, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		report
	}: {
		report: SelectedHabitAnalytics;
	} = $props();

	let totalLogged = $derived(Math.max(1, report.loggedDaysCount));
	let goldPct = $derived(Math.round((report.goldCount / totalLogged) * 100));
	let silverPct = $derived(Math.round((report.silverCount / totalLogged) * 100));
	let bronzePct = $derived(Math.round((report.bronzeCount / totalLogged) * 100));
	let failedPct = $derived(Math.round((report.failedCount / totalLogged) * 100));

	function getCellClasses(outcome: HabitDailyPoint['outcome']): string {
		if (outcome === 'gold') return 'border-gold-border bg-gold text-on-solid';
		if (outcome === 'silver') return 'border-silver-border bg-silver text-on-solid';
		if (outcome === 'bronze') return 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze text-on-solid';
		if (outcome === 'failed') return 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed';
		if (outcome === 'skipped') return 'border-border bg-muted text-fg-secondary';
		return 'border-border/50 bg-elevated/40 text-fg-muted';
	}
</script>

<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
	<div
		class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6 lg:col-span-7"
	>
		<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
				<Layers class="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-accent-text" />
				<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">کالبدشکافی سطوح و ماتریس روزها</h3>
			</div>
			<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-muted">
				{toPersianDigits(report.loggedDaysCount)} روز ثبت‌شده
			</span>
		</div>

		{#if report.loggedDaysCount > 0}
			<div class="space-y-2">
				<div class="flex h-4 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated p-0.5">
					{#if report.goldCount > 0}
						<div
							class="h-full bg-gold transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
							style="width: {goldPct}%"
							title="طلا: {toPersianDigits(report.goldCount)} روز (%{toPersianDigits(goldPct)})"
						></div>
					{/if}
					{#if report.silverCount > 0}
						<div
							class="h-full bg-silver transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
							style="width: {silverPct}%"
							title="نقره: {toPersianDigits(report.silverCount)} روز (%{toPersianDigits(silverPct)})"
						></div>
					{/if}
					{#if report.bronzeCount > 0}
						<div
							class="h-full bg-bronze transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
							style="width: {bronzePct}%"
							title="برنز: {toPersianDigits(report.bronzeCount)} روز (%{toPersianDigits(bronzePct)})"
						></div>
					{/if}
					{#if report.failedCount > 0}
						<div
							class="h-full bg-failed transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
							style="width: {failedPct}%"
							title="عدم انجام: {toPersianDigits(report.failedCount)} روز (%{toPersianDigits(failedPct)})"
						></div>
					{/if}
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary px-1">
					<span class="text-gold-text">طلا: %{toPersianDigits(goldPct)}</span>
					{#if report.silverCount > 0}
						<span class="text-silver-text">نقره: %{toPersianDigits(silverPct)}</span>
					{/if}
					<span class="text-bronze-text">برنز: %{toPersianDigits(bronzePct)}</span>
					<span class="text-failed">نشد: %{toPersianDigits(failedPct)}</span>
				</div>
			</div>
		{/if}

		<div class="space-y-2 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
				ماتریس حرارتی روزهای بازه (از راست به چپ):
			</div>
			<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
				{#each report.dailySeries as pt (pt.dateIso)}
					<div
						class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border text-[11px] font-black transition hover:scale-110 {getCellClasses(
							pt.outcome
						)}"
						title="{pt.humanLabel} — {pt.ruleSnapshot || 'ثبت‌نشده'}"
					>
						{toPersianDigits(pt.jalaliSlash.slice(8))}
					</div>
				{/each}
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>

	<div
		class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6 lg:col-span-5"
	>
		<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
				<Calendar class="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-accent-text" />
				<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">الگوی روزهای هفته و عوامل موثر</h3>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="space-y-2.5">
			{#each report.weekdayStats as w (w.weekdayIndex)}
				<div class="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
					<span class="w-16 shrink-0 font-bold text-fg-secondary">{w.weekdayLabel}</span>
					<div class="h-2.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
						<div
							class="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300 {w.avgScore >= 80
								? 'bg-optimal'
								: w.avgScore >= 50
									? 'bg-accent'
									: w.avgScore > 0
										? 'bg-bronze'
										: 'bg-transparent'}"
							style="width: {w.avgScore}%"
						></div>
					</div>
					<span class="w-12 shrink-0 text-left font-mono font-extrabold text-fg">
						{w.loggedDays > 0 ? `%${toPersianDigits(w.avgScore)}` : '—'}
					</span>
				</div>
			{/each}
		</div>

		{#if report.bestWeekday || report.weakestWeekday}
			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 border-t border-border pt-3.5 text-xs">
				{#if report.bestWeekday}
					<div class="rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft p-3">
						<div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-optimal">نقطه قوت هفتگی</div>
						<div class="mt-0.5 font-black text-fg">{report.bestWeekday.weekdayLabel}‌ها</div>
						<div class="mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-optimal">
							میانگین کیفیت: %{toPersianDigits(report.bestWeekday.avgScore)}
						</div>
					</div>
				{/if}
				{#if report.weakestWeekday && report.weakestWeekday.weekdayIndex !== report.bestWeekday?.weekdayIndex}
					<div class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft p-3">
						<div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-failed">نقطه آسیب‌پذیر هفتگی</div>
						<div class="mt-0.5 font-black text-fg">{report.weakestWeekday.weekdayLabel}‌ها</div>
						<div class="mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-failed">
							میانگین کیفیت: %{toPersianDigits(report.weakestWeekday.avgScore)}
						</div>
					</div>
				{/if}
			</div>
		{/if}

		{#if report.tagCorrelations.length > 0}
			<div class="space-y-2 border-t border-border pt-3.5">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
					<Hash class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text" />
					<span>همبستگی تگ‌های روزانه با عملکرد این طرح:</span>
				</div>
				<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
					{#each report.tagCorrelations.slice(0, 6) as item (item.tag)}
						<span
							class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold {item.impactDelta >=
							0
								? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal'
								: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'}"
						>
							{#if item.impactDelta >= 0}
								<TrendingUp class="h-3 w-3" />
							{:else}
								<TrendingDown class="h-3 w-3" />
							{/if}
							<span>#{item.tag}</span>
							<span dir="ltr" class="font-mono">
								({item.impactDelta > 0 ? '+' : ''}{toPersianDigits(item.impactDelta)}%)
							</span>
						</span>
					{/each}
				</div>
			</div>
		{/if}
	</div>
</div>

<script lang="ts">
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import type { HabitComparisonRow } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { BarChart3, Flame, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		rows,
		selectedHabitId,
		onSelectHabit
	}: {
		rows: HabitComparisonRow[];
		selectedHabitId: string | undefined;
		onSelectHabit: (habitId: string) => void;
	} = $props();

	let sortedRows = $derived(
		[...rows].sort((a, b) => b.avgQualityScore - a.avgQualityScore)
	);
</script>

<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
	<div class="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
		<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
			<div
				class="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
			>
				<BarChart3 class="h-5 w-5" />
			</div>
			<div>
				<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">مقایسه جامع همه طرح‌ها در این بازه</h3>
				<p class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					روی هر طرح کلیک کنید تا نمودار روند و جزئیات تکاملی همان طرح در بالا باز شود
				</p>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>

	<div class="space-y-2.5">
		{#each sortedRows as row (row.habit.id)}
			{@const isSelected = row.habit.id === selectedHabitId}
			{@const total = Math.max(1, row.loggedDays)}
			{@const gPct = Math.round((row.goldCount / total) * 100)}
			{@const sPct = Math.round((row.silverCount / total) * 100)}
			{@const bPct = Math.round((row.bronzeCount / total) * 100)}
			{@const fPct = Math.round((row.failedCount / total) * 100)}

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => onSelectHabit(row.habit.id)}
				class="flex w-full flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-3.5 text-right transition sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-4 {isSelected
					? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft/50 ring-1 ring-accent-border'
					: 'border-border bg-elevated hover:border-border-strong'}"
			>
				<div class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
						<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{row.habit.title}</span>
						{#if row.parentTitle}
							<span
								class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted"
							>
								زیرطرحِ {row.parentTitle}
							</span>
						{:else if row.isComposite}
							<span
								class="rounded-lg border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-gold-text"
							>
								مجموعه‌طرح
							</span>
						{/if}
						{#if row.habit.status === 'archived'}
							<span
								class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted"
							>
								بایگانی
							</span>
						{/if}
					</div>

					<div class="flex h-2 w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-surface">
						{#if row.goldCount > 0}
							<div class="h-full bg-gold" style="width: {gPct}%"></div>
						{/if}
						{#if row.silverCount > 0}
							<div class="h-full bg-silver" style="width: {sPct}%"></div>
						{/if}
						{#if row.bronzeCount > 0}
							<div class="h-full bg-bronze" style="width: {bPct}%"></div>
						{/if}
						{#if row.failedCount > 0}
							<div class="h-full bg-failed" style="width: {fPct}%"></div>
						{/if}
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs sm:justify-end">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-fg-secondary" title="رکورد فعلی">
						<Flame class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-bronze-text" />
						<span>{toPersianDigits(row.currentStreak)} روز</span>
					</div>

					<div
						class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-2.5 py-1 font-mono font-black text-fg"
					>
						استمرار: %{toPersianDigits(row.consistencyRate)}
					</div>

					<div
						class="w-16 text-center font-mono font-black {row.avgQualityScore >= 80
							? 'text-gold-text'
							: row.avgQualityScore >= 50
								? 'text-accent-text'
								: 'text-fg-secondary'}"
					>
						امتیاز {toPersianDigits(row.avgQualityScore)}
					</div>

					{#if row.trendDelta !== 0}
						<span
							class="inline-flex w-14 items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-lg py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-black {row.trendDelta >
							0
								? 'bg-optimal-soft text-optimal'
								: 'bg-failed-soft text-failed'}"
						>
							{#if row.trendDelta > 0}
								<TrendingUp class="h-3 w-3" />
								<span>+{toPersianDigits(row.trendDelta)}</span>
							{:else}
								<TrendingDown class="h-3 w-3" />
								<span>{toPersianDigits(row.trendDelta)}</span>
							{/if}
						</span>
					{/if}
				</div>
			</button>
		{/each}
	</div>
</section>

PATH: src/lib/components/notes/NoteUpsertModal.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { detectNoteSmartSignals, type NoteEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import BaseModal from '$lib/components/ui/BaseModal.svelte';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import { Tag } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		editingNote = null,
		existingCategories = [],
		onClose
	}: {
		editingNote?: NoteEntity | null;
		existingCategories: string[];
		onClose: () => void;
	} = $props();

	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let title = $state(editingNote?.title ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let content = $state(editingNote?.content ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let category = $state(editingNote?.category || 'عمومی');
	let isSubmitting = $state(false);

	let liveSignals = $derived(detectNoteSmartSignals(`${title} ${content}`));

	async function handleSubmit(e: SubmitEvent) {
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!content.trim() || isSubmitting) return;
		isSubmitting = true;

		try {
			if (editingNote) {
				await clientDb.notes.update(editingNote.id, {
					noteId: editingNote.id,
					title: title.trim(),
					content: content.trim(),
					category: category.trim() || 'عمومی'
				});
			} else {
				await clientDb.notes.create({
					title: title.trim(),
					content: content.trim(),
					category: category.trim() || 'عمومی'
				});
			}
			onClose();
		} finally {
			isSubmitting = false;
		}
	}
</script>

<BaseModal
	title={editingNote ? 'ویرایش یادداشت' : 'افزودن یادداشت جدید'}
	{onClose}
>
	<form onsubmit={handleSubmit} class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
				<div class="flex-1">
					<label for="note-title" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						عنوان (اختیاری)
					</label>
					<input
						id="note-title"
						name="title"
						bind:value={title}
						placeholder="مثلاً: سایت ابزار هوش مصنوعی / شماره پیگیری..."
						class="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<div class="w-full sm:w-36">
					<label
						for="note-cat"
						class="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary"
					>
						<Tag class="h-3 w-3 text-fg-muted" />
						<span>دسته‌بندی</span>
					</label>
					<input
						id="note-cat"
						name="category"
						list="note-categories-list"
						bind:value={category}
						placeholder="عمومی"
						class="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
					<datalist id="note-categories-list">
						{#each existingCategories as cat (cat)}
							<option value={cat}></option>
						{/each}
					</datalist>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="note-content" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					متن یادداشت، لینک یا شماره
				</label>
				<textarea
					id="note-content"
					name="content"
					required
					rows="5"
					bind:value={content}
					placeholder="هر لینک، شماره، دستور یا نکته‌ای که می‌خواهید ذخیره شود را اینجا بنویسید..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-sm leading-7 text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:bg-surface focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-1">
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={onClose}
					class="h-10 rounded-xl px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					disabled={isSubmitting}
					class="h-10 rounded-xl bg-accent px-6 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : editingNote ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت یادداشت'}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
</BaseModal>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/notes/NoteItemCard.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { detectNoteSmartSignals, type NoteEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import {
		Check,
		Copy,
		ExternalLink,
		Pencil,
		Pin,
		Trash2
	} from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		note,
		onEdit
	}: {
		note: NoteEntity;
		onEdit: (note: NoteEntity) => void;
	} = $props();

	let copied = $state(false);
	let copyTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
	let signals = $derived(detectNoteSmartSignals(`${note.title} ${note.content}`));

	$effect(() => {
		return () => {
			if (copyTimer) clearTimeout(copyTimer);
		};
	});

	async function copyContent() {
		try {
			await navigator.clipboard.writeText(
				note.title ? `${note.title}\n${note.content}` : note.content
			);
			copied = true;
			if (copyTimer) clearTimeout(copyTimer);
			copyTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				copied = false;
				copyTimer = null;
			}, 1600);
		} catch {
			// ignore clipboard permission errors
		}
	}
</script>

<article
	class="group flex flex-col justify-between rounded-3xl border p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:border-border-strong {note.isPinned
		? 'border-accent-border bg-surface ring-1 ring-accent-border/40'
		: 'border-border bg-surface'}"
>
	<div>
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
			<span
				class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary"
			>
				{note.category}
			</span>

			<div class="flex items-center gap-1">
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => clientDb.notes.togglePin(note.id, !note.isPinned)}
					class="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition active:scale-95 {note.isPinned
						? 'bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
						: 'text-fg-muted hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg'}"
					title={note.isPinned ? 'برداشتن سنجاق' : 'سنجاق به بالای لیست'}
				>
					<Pin class="h-3.5 w-3.5 {note.isPinned ? 'fill-current' : ''}" />
				</button>

				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={copyContent}
					class="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
					title="کپی متن"
				>
					{#if copied}
						<Check class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-optimal" />
					{:else}
						<Copy class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
					{/if}
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		{#if note.title}
			<h3 class="mt-3 text-base font-black text-fg">{note.title}</h3>
		{/if}

		<p
			dir="auto"
			class="mt-2.5 line-clamp-6 whitespace-pre-line break-words text-xs leading-6 font-medium text-fg-secondary select-text"
		>
			{note.content}
		</p>
	</div>

	<div
		class="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border pt-3 text-[11px] text-fg-muted"
	>
		<span dir="ltr" class="font-mono font-bold">
			{toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(note.updatedAtIso.slice(0, 10)))}
		</span>

		<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
			{#if signals.firstUrl}
				<a
					href={signals.firstUrl}
					target="_blank"
					rel="noopener noreferrer"
					class="flex h-7 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-accent-text transition hover:opacity-90"
					title="باز کردن لینک"
				>
					<span>باز کردن</span>
					<ExternalLink class="h-3 w-3" />
				</a>
			{/if}

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => onEdit(note)}
				class="flex h-7 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg"
			>
				<Pencil class="h-3 w-3" />
				<span>ویرایش</span>
			</button>

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => clientDb.notes.delete(note.id)}
				class="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
				title="حذف یادداشت"
			>
				<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
			</button>
		</div>
	</div>
</article>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/architect/HabitUpsertModal.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import type { HabitEntity, HabitStatus, HabitTimeSlot } from '@domain/schemas';
	import BaseModal from '$lib/components/ui/BaseModal.svelte';
	import CustomSelect from '$lib/components/ui/CustomSelect.svelte';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';

	let {
		mode,
		editingHabit = null,
		hasSubHabits = false,
		initialParentId = '',
		rootHabits,
		onClose
	}: {
		mode: 'create' | 'edit';
		editingHabit?: HabitEntity | null;
		hasSubHabits?: boolean;
		initialParentId?: string;
		rootHabits: HabitEntity[];
		onClose: () => void;
	} = $props();

	type FormStructureKind = 'tiered' | 'composite' | 'interval_cadence';

	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let structureKind = $state<FormStructureKind>(
		mode === 'edit' && editingHabit
			? editingHabit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
				? 'interval_cadence'
				: hasSubHabits || (!editingHabit.bronzeDesc && !editingHabit.parentId)
					? 'composite'
					: 'tiered'
			: 'tiered'
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedParentId = $state(
		mode === 'edit' && editingHabit ? (editingHabit.parentId ?? '') : initialParentId
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedStatus = $state<HabitStatus>(
		mode === 'edit' && editingHabit ? editingHabit.status : 'active'
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedTimeSlot = $state<HabitTimeSlot>(
		mode === 'edit' && editingHabit ? (editingHabit.timeSlot ?? 'day') : 'day'
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let isSituational = $state(
		mode === 'edit' && editingHabit ? Boolean(editingHabit.isSituational) : false
	);

	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let title = $state(editingHabit?.title ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let scheduleLabel = $state(editingHabit?.scheduleLabel ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let bronzeDesc = $state(editingHabit?.bronzeDesc ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let silverDesc = $state(editingHabit?.silverDesc ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let goldDesc = $state(editingHabit?.goldDesc ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let emergencyMinDesc = $state(editingHabit?.emergencyMinDesc ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let notes = $state(editingHabit?.notes ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let reason = $state(mode === 'create' ? 'تعریف طرح جدید' : '');
	let isSubmitting = $state(false);

	let modalTitle = $derived(
		mode === 'edit' && editingHabit
			? `ویرایش «${editingHabit.title}»`
			: selectedParentId
				? 'افزودن زیرطرح جدید'
				: 'افزودن طرح جدید'
	);

	let eligibleParents = $derived(
		rootHabits.filter(
			(h) => h.trackingMode === 'tiered' && (mode === 'create' || h.id !== editingHabit?.id)
		)
	);

	let structureKindOptions = $derived.by(() => {
		const base = [
			{ value: 'tiered', label: 'طرح سطح‌بندی شده (برنز / نقره / طلا)' },
			{ value: 'composite', label: 'مجموعه‌طرح (شامل چند زیرطرح)' }
		];
		if (mode === 'create' || editingHabit?.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
			base.push({
				value: 'interval_cadence',
				label: 'طرح تکرارشونده (هدف‌گذاری از شب قبل)'
			});
		}
		return base;
	});

	let parentOptions = $derived([
		{ value: '', label: 'طرح اصلی و مستقل' },
		...eligibleParents.map((p) => ({ value: p.id, label: `زیرمجموعهٔ «${p.title}»` }))
	]);

	const statusOptions = [
		{ value: 'active', label: 'فعال' },
		{ value: 'paused', label: 'متوقف موقت' },
		{ value: 'archived', label: 'حذف / بایگانی' }
	] as const;

	const timeSlotOptions = [
		{ value: 'morning', label: 'صبحگاهی' },
		{ value: 'day', label: 'در طول روز' },
		{ value: 'night', label: 'شبانه' }
	] as const;

	async function handleSubmit(e: SubmitEvent) {
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!title.trim() || isSubmitting) return;
		isSubmitting = true;

		try {
			if (mode === 'edit' && editingHabit) {
				await clientDb.habits.update({
					habitId: editingHabit.id,
					title: title.trim(),
					parentId: selectedParentId || null,
					category: editingHabit.category,
					timeSlot: selectedTimeSlot,
					isSituational,
					status: selectedStatus,
					scheduleLabel: scheduleLabel.trim() || null,
					notes: notes.trim() || null,
					bronzeDesc: bronzeDesc.trim() || null,
					silverDesc: silverDesc.trim() || null,
					goldDesc: goldDesc.trim() || null,
					emergencyMinDesc: emergencyMinDesc.trim() || null,
					reason: reason.trim() || 'ویرایش قوانین طرح'
				});
			} else {
				await clientDb.habits.create({
					title: title.trim(),
					parentId: selectedParentId || null,
					category: 'عمومی',
					trackingMode: structureKind === 'interval_cadence' ? 'interval_cadence' : 'tiered',
					timeSlot: selectedTimeSlot,
					isSituational,
					scheduleLabel: scheduleLabel.trim() || undefined,
					notes: notes.trim() || undefined,
					bronzeDesc: bronzeDesc.trim() || undefined,
					silverDesc: silverDesc.trim() || undefined,
					goldDesc: goldDesc.trim() || undefined,
					emergencyMinDesc: emergencyMinDesc.trim() || undefined,
					defaultIntervals: 1,
					defaultMinutesPerInterval: 10,
					cadenceWindowMinutes: 60,
					reason: reason.trim() || 'تعریف طرح جدید'
				});
			}
			onClose();
		} finally {
			isSubmitting = false;
		}
	}
</script>

<BaseModal title={modalTitle} maxWidthClass="max-w-xl" {onClose}>
	<form onsubmit={handleSubmit} class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:space-y-5 sm:p-8">
		<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
			<div>
				<label for="h-title" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">عنوان طرح</label>
				<input
					id="h-title"
					required
					bind:value={title}
					placeholder="مثلاً: مطالعه شبانه"
					class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-base font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="h-sched" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">
					زمان انجام (اختیاری)
				</label>
				<input
					id="h-sched"
					bind:value={scheduleLabel}
					placeholder="مثلاً: بعد از نماز صبح"
					class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-base text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>
		</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
				{#if !selectedParentId}
					<div>
						<label for="h-kind" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">نوع طرح</label>
						<CustomSelect
							id="h-kind"
							bind:value={structureKind}
							disabled={hasSubHabits}
							options={structureKindOptions}
							triggerClass="h-12 text-fg"
						/>
					</div>
				{/if}

				{#if structureKind === 'tiered'}
					<div>
						<label for="h-parent" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">
							جایگاه در ساختار
						</label>
						<CustomSelect
							id="h-parent"
							bind:value={selectedParentId}
							options={parentOptions}
							triggerClass="h-12 text-fg"
						/>
					</div>
				{/if}

				{#if structureKind !== 'interval_cadence' && !selectedParentId}
					<div>
						<label for="h-timeslot" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">
							بازهٔ زمانی در روز
						</label>
						<CustomSelect
							id="h-timeslot"
							bind:value={selectedTimeSlot}
							options={timeSlotOptions}
							triggerClass="h-12 text-fg"
						/>
					</div>
				{/if}

				{#if mode === 'edit' && editingHabit}
					<div>
						<label for="h-status" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">وضعیت طرح</label>
						<CustomSelect
							id="h-status"
							bind:value={selectedStatus}
							options={statusOptions}
							triggerClass="h-12 text-fg"
						/>
					</div>
				{/if}
			</div>

			{#if structureKind === 'tiered'}
				<div class="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4">
					<div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
						شرایط سطوح (سطح برنزی همان حداقلِ انجام طرح است؛ نقره‌ای و طلایی اختیاری‌اند)
					</div>

					<div>
						<label for="h-bronze" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-bronze-text">
							برنز (سطح ۱ - حداقل)
						</label>
						<input
							id="h-bronze"
							required
							bind:value={bronzeDesc}
							placeholder="مثلاً: ۵ دقیقه مطالعه"
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
						/>
					</div>

					<div>
						<label for="h-silver" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-silver-text">
							نقره (سطح ۲ - اختیاری)
						</label>
						<input
							id="h-silver"
							bind:value={silverDesc}
							placeholder="مثلاً: ۱۰ دقیقه مطالعه"
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
						/>
					</div>

					<div>
						<label for="h-gold" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-gold-text">
							طلا (سطح ۳ - اختیاری)
						</label>
						<input
							id="h-gold"
							bind:value={goldDesc}
							placeholder="مثلاً: ۲۰ دقیقه مطالعه"
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
						/>
					</div>

					<label
						class="mt-3 flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 transition hover:border-border-strong"
					>
						<input
							type="checkbox"
							bind:checked={isSituational}
							class="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 rounded-lg accent-accent"
						/>
						<div class="space-y-1.5">
							<div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">
								طرح رویدادی / موقعیتی (گزینهٔ «پیش نیامد» داشته باشد)
							</div>
							<div class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-fg-secondary">
								مخصوص طرح‌هایی که هر روز اتفاق نمی‌افتند (مثلاً مواجهه با خوراکی خاص یا موقعیت خاص)
							</div>
						</div>
					</label>
				</div>
			{:else if structureKind === 'composite'}
				<div
					class="rounded-2xl border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft p-4 text-xs font-bold leading-relaxed text-gold-text"
				>
					این مورد یک «مجموعه‌طرح» است و خودش به تنهایی سطح برنز/نقره/طلا ندارد. پس از ایجاد، زیرطرح‌های آن را اضافه کنید تا امتیاز آن از ترکیب زیرطرح‌ها محاسبه شود.
				</div>
			{:else}
				<div class="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-4">
					<div class="text-xs font-bold text-accent-text">
						مقدار هدف این طرح تکرارشونده هر شب برای روز بعد تعیین می‌شود.
					</div>
					<div>
						<label for="h-emerg" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg">
							حداقل اضطراری (در روزهای بحرانی)
						</label>
						<input
							id="h-emerg"
							bind:value={emergencyMinDesc}
							placeholder="مثلاً: ۱۰ دقیقه برای کار"
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
						/>
					</div>
				</div>
			{/if}

			<div>
				<label for="h-notes" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">
					تبصره یا توضیح کوتاه (اختیاری)
				</label>
				<input
					id="h-notes"
					bind:value={notes}
					placeholder="مثلاً: اگر مهمان داشتیم استثناست..."
					class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-sm text-fg"
				/>
			</div>

			{#if mode === 'edit'}
				<div class="rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-4">
					<label for="h-reason-edit" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-extrabold text-accent-text">
						دلیل این تغییر چیست؟ (برای ثبت در تاریخچه)
					</label>
					<input
						id="h-reason-edit"
						required
						bind:value={reason}
						placeholder="مثلاً: تغییر شرایط مطالعه..."
						class="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			{/if}

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 border-t border-border pt-5">
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={onClose}
					class="h-12 rounded-2xl px-5 text-sm font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					disabled={isSubmitting}
					class="h-12 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-8 text-sm font-extrabold text-accent-fg disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{isSubmitting ? 'در حال ذخیره...' : mode === 'create' ? 'ایجاد طرح' : 'ذخیره تغییرات'}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
</BaseModal>

PATH: src/lib/components/tracker/JalaliDateScrubber.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import { goto } from '$app/navigation';
	import {
		buildRecentCalendarDays,
		formatJalaliHuman,
		getTodayIso,
		getYesterdayIso,
		shiftIsoDate,
		toPersianDigits,
		type CalendarScrubberDay
	} from '@domain/calendar';
	import { getDayStatusMeta } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { DayStatusColor } from '@domain/schemas';
	import type { SaveSyncState } from '$lib/state/daily-tracker.svelte';
	import {
		AlertCircle,
		Check,
		ChevronLeft,
		ChevronRight
	} from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		currentDateIso,
		recentEntries = [],
		liveDayStatus,
		isLiveDirty = false,
		hasExistingEntry = false,
		saveState = 'idle',
		onRetrySave,
		completionStats
	}: {
		currentDateIso: string;
		recentEntries?: Array<{
			dateIso: string;
			dayStatus: DayStatusColor;
			reflectionNote: string;
			tags: string[];
		}>;
		liveDayStatus: DayStatusColor;
		isLiveDirty?: boolean;
		hasExistingEntry?: boolean;
		saveState?: SaveSyncState;
		onRetrySave?: () => void;
		completionStats: { completed: number; goldCount: number; total: number; percent: number };
	} = $props();

	let human = $derived(formatJalaliHuman(currentDateIso));
	let todayIso = $derived(getTodayIso());
	let yesterdayIso = $derived(getYesterdayIso());
	let prevDayIso = $derived(shiftIsoDate(currentDateIso, -1));
	let nextDayIso = $derived(shiftIsoDate(currentDateIso, 1));

	let savedStatusByIso = $derived(new Map(recentEntries.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e.dayStatus])));

	let calendarDays = $derived(
		buildRecentCalendarDays<DayStatusColor>({
			currentDateIso,
			todayIso,
			savedStatusByIso,
			liveStatus: liveDayStatus,
			useLiveStatusForSelected: isLiveDirty || hasExistingEntry,
			defaultStatus: 'failed',
			daysCount: 14
		})
	);

	async function navigateToIso(iso: string) {
		await goto(`/?date=${iso}`);
	}

	function handleKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
		const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName;
		if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
		if (e.key === '[') {
			e.preventDefault();
			navigateToIso(shiftIsoDate(currentDateIso, -1));
		} else if (e.key === ']') {
			e.preventDefault();
			navigateToIso(shiftIsoDate(currentDateIso, 1));
		}
	}

	function getDayCellClasses(day: CalendarScrubberDay<DayStatusColor>): string {
		const selectedGlow = day.isSelected
			? 'border-2 -translate-y-0.5 shadow-(--shadow-card)'
			: 'border hover:border-border-strong';

		if (day.visualState === 'optimal') {
			return `${selectedGlow} ${
				day.isSelected
					? 'border-optimal bg-optimal-soft'
					: 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft'
			}`;
		}
		if (day.visualState === 'partial') {
			return `${selectedGlow} ${
				day.isSelected
					? 'border-partial bg-partial-soft'
					: 'border-partial-border bg-partial-soft'
			}`;
		}
		return `${selectedGlow} ${
			day.isSelected
				? 'border-failed bg-failed-soft'
				: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft'
		}`;
	}
</script>

<svelte:window onkeydown={handleKeydown} />

<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
	<div
		class="flex flex-col gap-3.5 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-4"
	>
		<div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1.5 sm:w-auto sm:justify-start sm:gap-2">
			<a
				href="/?date={prevDayIso}"
				data-sveltekit-noscroll
				class="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95 sm:h-11 sm:w-11"
				aria-label="روز قبل"
			>
				<ChevronRight class="h-5 w-5" />
			</a>

			<div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center sm:min-w-80 sm:flex-initial">
				<div
					class="flex w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1.5 text-center sm:gap-2.5 sm:px-3"
				>
					<h1 class="truncate text-base font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">
						{human.fullLabel}
					</h1>
					{#if human.relativeBadge}
						<span
							class="shrink-0 rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-accent-text sm:px-2.5 sm:text-xs"
						>
							{human.relativeBadge}
						</span>
					{/if}
				</div>
			</div>

			<a
				href="/?date={nextDayIso}"
				data-sveltekit-noscroll
				class="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95 sm:h-11 sm:w-11"
				aria-label="روز بعد"
			>
				<ChevronLeft class="h-5 w-5" />
			</a>
		</div>

		<div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:w-auto sm:justify-end sm:gap-3">
			<div
				class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 sm:gap-2.5 sm:px-3.5"
			>
				<div class="text-xs font-black text-fg">
					{toPersianDigits(completionStats.completed)} از {toPersianDigits(
						completionStats.total
					)}
				</div>

				{#if completionStats.total > 0 && completionStats.completed === completionStats.total}
					<span
						class="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-optimal-soft text-optimal"
						title="تمام طرح‌های امروز تکمیل شد"
					>
						<Check class="h-3 w-3 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
				{:else if saveState === 'error'}
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() => onRetrySave?.()}
						class="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-failed-soft text-failed transition hover:scale-105"
						title="خطا در ذخیره‌سازی — کلیک برای تلاش مجدد"
					>
						<AlertCircle class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
					</button>
				{/if}
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
				<a
					href="/?date={yesterdayIso}"
					data-sveltekit-noscroll
					class="rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition {currentDateIso ===
					yesterdayIso
						? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
						: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
				>
					دیروز
				</a>
				<a
					href="/?date={todayIso}"
					data-sveltekit-noscroll
					class="rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition {currentDateIso ===
					todayIso
						? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
						: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
				>
					امروز
				</a>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>

	<div class="mt-4 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 sm:mt-5 sm:grid-cols-14 sm:gap-2">
		{#each calendarDays as day (day.iso)}
			<a
				href="/?date={day.iso}"
				data-sveltekit-noscroll
				class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between rounded-2xl px-0.5 py-2 transition-all duration-150 sm:px-1 sm:py-2.5 {getDayCellClasses(
					day
				)}"
				title="{day.weekdayShort} {toPersianDigits(day.jd)} {day.monthName}"
			>
				<span
					class="text-[11px] font-bold {day.isSelected ? 'text-fg' : 'text-fg-secondary'}"
				>
					{day.weekdayShort}
				</span>

				<span class="my-1 text-base font-black text-fg">
					{toPersianDigits(day.jd)}
				</span>

				<span
					class="h-2 w-2 rounded-full {getDayStatusMeta(day.visualState).dotClass}"
				></span>
			</a>
		{/each}
	</div>
</section>
