================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/tracker/TieredHabitCard.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import {
		getMaxSupportedTier,
		resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
	} from '@core/habit-engine';
	import { COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL, TIER_SCORES } from '@domain/constants';
	import type {
		HabitEntity,
		HabitLogItemInput,
		HabitTreeNode
	} from '@domain/schemas';
	import { Check, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Minus, X } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		node,
		logsByHabitId,
		compositeNotation,
		onSelectTier,
		onSelectGroupTier
	}: {
		node: HabitTreeNode;
		logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>;
		compositeNotation?: string;
		onSelectTier: (habitId: string, tier: number) => void;
		onSelectGroupTier?: (parentId: string, tier: number) => void;
	} = $props();

	let isExpanded = $state(false);
	let hasChildren = $derived(node.children.length > 0);
	let maxTier = $derived(getMaxSupportedTier(node));
	let currentLog = $derived(logsByHabitId[node.id]);
	let currentTier = $derived(currentLog?.achievedTier ?? null);
	let displayTitle = $derived(currentLog?.habitTitleSnapshot || node.title);
	let hasSituationalGroup = $derived(
		Boolean(node.isSituational) || node.children.some((c) => Boolean(c.isSituational))
	);

	let uniformChildrenTier = $derived.by<number | null>(() => {
		if (!hasChildren) return null;
		const tiers = node.children.map((c) => logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier ?? null);
		const first = tiers[0];
		if (first === null || first === undefined) return null;
		return tiers.every((t) => t === first) ? first : null;
	});

	let hasMixedChildren = $derived.by<boolean>(() => {
		if (!hasChildren) return false;
		const tiers = node.children.map((c) => logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier ?? null);
		const hasAnyLogged = tiers.some((t) => t !== null && t !== undefined);
		return hasAnyLogged && uniformChildrenTier === null;
	});

	let maxChildSupportedTier = $derived.by<1 | 2 | 3>(() => {
		if (!hasChildren) return maxTier;
		return node.children.reduce<1 | 2 | 3>((acc, child) => {
			const m = getMaxSupportedTier(child);
			return m > acc ? m : acc;
		}, 1);
	});

	let isNodeLogged = $derived.by(() => {
		if (hasChildren) {
			return node.children.every((c) => {
				const t = logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier;
				return t !== null && t !== undefined;
			});
		}
		return currentTier !== null && currentTier !== undefined;
	});

	let activeRuleSubtitle = $derived.by(() => {
		if (hasChildren) {
			return node.scheduleLabel || '';
		}
		if (currentTier !== null && currentTier !== undefined) {
			return (
				currentLog?.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(node, currentTier) ||
				''
			);
		}
		return node.bronzeDesc || node.scheduleLabel || '';
	});

	let effectiveCardTier = $derived(hasChildren ? uniformChildrenTier : currentTier);

	let cardSurfaceClasses = $derived.by(() => {
		const spacing = 'py-3.5 sm:py-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft)';

		if (hasChildren && compositeNotation === COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL) {
			return `${spacing} border-gold-border bg-gold-soft`;
		}
		if (effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED)
			return `${spacing} border-border bg-elevated`;
		if (effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD)
			return `${spacing} border-gold-border bg-gold-soft`;
		if (effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER)
			return `${spacing} border-silver-border bg-silver-soft`;
		if (effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE)
			return `${spacing} border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft`;
		if (effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED)
			return `${spacing} border-failed-border bg-failed-soft`;
		return 'py-3.5 sm:py-4 border-border-strong bg-surface shadow-(--shadow-soft)';
	});

	function getOptionStyle(selected: number | null, target: number): string {
		if (selected !== target) {
			return 'text-fg-secondary hover:bg-surface hover:text-fg';
		}
		if (target === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED)
			return 'bg-muted text-fg font-extrabold border border-border-strong shadow-xs';
		if (target === TIER_SCORES.FAILED)
			return 'bg-failed text-on-solid font-extrabold shadow-xs';
		if (target === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE)
			return 'bg-bronze text-on-solid font-extrabold shadow-xs';
		if (target === TIER_SCORES.SILVER)
			return 'bg-silver text-on-solid font-extrabold shadow-xs';
		return 'bg-gold text-on-solid font-black shadow-xs';
	}
</script>

<div class="rounded-3xl border px-4 transition-all duration-200 sm:px-6 {cardSurfaceClasses}">
	<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3.5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
		<div class="min-w-0 space-y-1">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
				{#if !isNodeLogged}
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-accent"></span>
				{:else if effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED}
					<span
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-failed-border bg-surface text-failed"
					>
						<X class="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
				{:else if effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED}
					<span
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface text-fg-secondary"
					>
						<Minus class="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
				{:else}
					<span
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border bg-surface {effectiveCardTier ===
						TIER_SCORES.GOLD
							? 'border-gold-border text-gold-text'
							: effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER
								? 'border-silver-border text-silver-text'
								: 'border-bronze-border text-bronze-text'}"
					>
						<Check class="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
				{/if}

				<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">
					{displayTitle}
				</h3>

				{#if node.status === 'archived'}
					<span
						class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted"
					>
						بایگانی‌شده
					</span>
				{/if}

				{#if hasChildren && compositeNotation}
					<span
						dir="ltr"
						class="rounded-xl border border-gold-border bg-surface px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs font-black text-gold-text"
					>
						{compositeNotation}
					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>
			{#if activeRuleSubtitle}
				<p class="text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary">{activeRuleSubtitle}</p>
			{/if}
		</div>

		{#if hasChildren && onSelectGroupTier}
			<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
				{@render tierButtons({
					selectedTier: uniformChildrenTier,
					maxSupported: maxChildSupportedTier,
					showSituational: hasSituationalGroup,
					singleTierBronzeLabel: false,
					compact: false,
					bronzeTitle: '',
					silverTitle: '',
					goldTitle: '',
					onPick: (t) => onSelectGroupTier(node.id, t)
				})}

				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => (isExpanded = !isExpanded)}
					class="flex h-11 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong sm:px-3.5"
				>
					<span>تک‌به‌تک</span>
					{#if isExpanded || hasMixedChildren}
						<ChevronUp class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-secondary" />
					{:else}
						<ChevronDown class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-secondary" />
					{/if}
				</button>
			</div>
		{:else}
			{@render tierButtons({
				selectedTier: currentTier,
				maxSupported: maxTier,
				showSituational: Boolean(node.isSituational),
				singleTierBronzeLabel: maxTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE,
				compact: false,
				bronzeTitle: node.bronzeDesc ?? '',
				silverTitle: node.silverDesc ?? '',
				goldTitle: node.goldDesc ?? '',
				onPick: (t) => onSelectTier(node.id, t)
			})}
		{/if}
	</div>

	{#if hasChildren && (isExpanded || hasMixedChildren)}
		<div class="mt-4 space-y-2.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
			{#each node.children as sub (sub.id)}
				{@render subHabitRow(sub)}
			{/each}
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

{#snippet tierButtons(params: {
	selectedTier: number | null;
	maxSupported: 1 | 2 | 3;
	showSituational: boolean;
	singleTierBronzeLabel: boolean;
	compact: boolean;
	bronzeTitle: string;
	silverTitle: string;
	goldTitle: string;
	onPick: (tier: number) => void;
})}
	<div
		class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 border border-border {params.compact
			? 'w-full rounded-xl bg-surface p-1 sm:w-auto'
			: 'min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-2xl bg-elevated p-1 sm:w-auto sm:flex-initial sm:gap-1.5 sm:p-1.5'}"
	>
		{#if params.showSituational}
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={(e) => {
					e.stopPropagation();
					params.onPick(TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED);
				}}
				class="flex-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition sm:flex-initial {params.compact
					? 'h-9 rounded-lg px-2 sm:px-2.5'
					: 'h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11 sm:px-3'} {getOptionStyle(
					params.selectedTier,
					TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED
				)}"
				title={params.selectedTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED
					? 'کلیک مجدد برای لغو انتخاب'
					: 'عدم وجود شرایط در امروز'}
			>
				پیش نیامد
			</button>
		{/if}

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={(e) => {
				e.stopPropagation();
				params.onPick(TIER_SCORES.FAILED);
			}}
			class="flex-1 text-xs transition sm:flex-initial {params.compact
				? 'h-9 rounded-lg px-2.5 sm:px-3'
				: 'h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11 sm:min-w-14 sm:px-3.5'} {getOptionStyle(
				params.selectedTier,
				TIER_SCORES.FAILED
			)}"
			title={params.selectedTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED ? 'کلیک مجدد برای لغو انتخاب' : 'ثبت انجام نشدن'}
		>
			نشد
		</button>

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={(e) => {
				e.stopPropagation();
				params.onPick(TIER_SCORES.BRONZE);
			}}
			class="flex-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition sm:flex-initial {params.compact
				? 'h-9 rounded-lg px-2.5 sm:px-3'
				: 'h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11 sm:min-w-16 sm:px-3.5'} {getOptionStyle(
				params.selectedTier,
				TIER_SCORES.BRONZE
			)}"
			title={params.selectedTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE
				? 'کلیک مجدد برای لغو انتخاب'
				: params.bronzeTitle}
		>
			{params.singleTierBronzeLabel ? 'انجام شد' : 'برنز'}
		</button>

		{#if params.maxSupported >= TIER_SCORES.SILVER}
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={(e) => {
					e.stopPropagation();
					params.onPick(TIER_SCORES.SILVER);
				}}
				class="flex-1 text-xs transition sm:flex-initial {params.compact
					? 'h-9 rounded-lg px-2.5 sm:px-3'
					: 'h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11 sm:min-w-16 sm:px-3.5'} {getOptionStyle(
					params.selectedTier,
					TIER_SCORES.SILVER
				)}"
				title={params.selectedTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER
					? 'کلیک مجدد برای لغو انتخاب'
					: params.silverTitle}
			>
				نقره
			</button>
		{/if}

		{#if params.maxSupported === TIER_SCORES.GOLD}
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={(e) => {
					e.stopPropagation();
					params.onPick(TIER_SCORES.GOLD);
				}}
				class="flex-1 text-xs transition sm:flex-initial {params.compact
					? 'h-9 rounded-lg px-2.5 sm:px-3'
					: 'h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11 sm:min-w-16 sm:px-3.5'} {getOptionStyle(
					params.selectedTier,
					TIER_SCORES.GOLD
				)}"
				title={params.selectedTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD
					? 'کلیک مجدد برای لغو انتخاب'
					: params.goldTitle}
			>
				طلا
			</button>
		{/if}
	</div>
{/snippet}

{#snippet subHabitRow(sub: HabitEntity)}
	{@const subLog = logsByHabitId[sub.id]}
	{@const subTier = subLog?.achievedTier ?? null}
	{@const subMax = getMaxSupportedTier(sub)}
	<div
		class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
	>
		<div>
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
				<span class="text-sm font-extrabold text-fg">
					{subLog?.habitTitleSnapshot || sub.title}
				</span>
				{#if sub.status === 'archived'}
					<span
						class="rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted"
					>
						بایگانی‌شده
					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>
			<div class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-fg-secondary">
				{subTier !== null && subTier !== undefined
					? subLog?.achievedRuleSnapshot || resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(sub, subTier) || sub.bronzeDesc
					: sub.bronzeDesc}
			</div>
		</div>

		{@render tierButtons({
			selectedTier: subTier,
			maxSupported: subMax,
			showSituational: Boolean(sub.isSituational || node.isSituational),
			singleTierBronzeLabel: false,
			compact: true,
			bronzeTitle: sub.bronzeDesc ?? '',
			silverTitle: sub.silverDesc ?? '',
			goldTitle: sub.goldDesc ?? '',
			onPick: (t) => onSelectTier(sub.id, t)
		})}
	</div>
{/snippet}

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/tracker/TrackerActionToolbar.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import { slide } from 'svelte/transition';
	import { formatJalaliHuman, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import type { HabitEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import type { DailyTrackerController } from '$lib/state/daily-tracker.svelte';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import {
		CalendarClock,
		Copy,
		Eraser,
		MessageSquarePlus,
		Sparkles
	} from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		tracker,
		cadenceHabits
	}: {
		tracker: DailyTrackerController;
		cadenceHabits: HabitEntity[];
	} = $props();

	let showReflectionDrawer = $state(false);
	let showTomorrowDrawer = $state(false);
	let isSavingTomorrow = $state(false);

	let tomorrowHuman = $derived(formatJalaliHuman(tracker.tomorrowIso));
	let activeNotesBadgeCount = $derived(
		tracker.selectedTags.length + (tracker.reflectionNote.trim().length > 0 ? 1 : 0)
	);
</script>

<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-6">
	<div
		class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-3"
	>
		<div class="flex w-full items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => tracker.markAllOptimalFiveSecond()}
				class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-4 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:flex-initial sm:px-5 sm:text-sm"
			>
				<Sparkles class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
				<span>همه عالی انجام شد</span>
			</button>

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => tracker.clearDay()}
				class="flex h-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-failed-border hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed sm:px-3.5"
				title="پاک کردن کامل ثبت‌های این روز و بازگشت به حالت ثبت‌نشده"
			>
				<Eraser class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
				<span>پاک کردن روز</span>
			</button>
		</div>

		<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => (showReflectionDrawer = !showReflectionDrawer)}
				class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border px-3 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:gap-2 sm:px-4 {showReflectionDrawer ||
				activeNotesBadgeCount > 0
					? 'border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
					: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
			>
				<MessageSquarePlus class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
				<span class="truncate">علت‌ها و یادداشت</span>
				{#if activeNotesBadgeCount > 0}
					<span
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-accent text-[11px] font-black text-accent-fg"
					>
						{toPersianDigits(activeNotesBadgeCount)}
					</span>
				{/if}
			</button>

			{#if cadenceHabits.length > 0}
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => (showTomorrowDrawer = !showTomorrowDrawer)}
					class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border px-3 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:gap-2 sm:px-4 {showTomorrowDrawer
						? 'border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
						: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
				>
					<CalendarClock class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
					<span class="truncate">تنظیم ریتم فردا</span>
				</button>
			{/if}
		</div>
	</div>

	{#if showReflectionDrawer}
		<div transition:slide={{ duration: 220 }} class="mt-4 space-y-4 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="space-y-2">
				<div class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تگ‌های سریع علت کاهش یا افزایش:</div>
				<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
					{#each tracker.availableTags as tag (tag)}
						{@const active = tracker.selectedTags.includes(tag)}
						<button
							type="button"
							onclick={() => tracker.toggleTag(tag)}
							class="h-9 rounded-xl border px-3 text-xs font-bold transition {active
								? 'border-accent-border bg-accent text-accent-fg'
								: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
						>
							#{tag}
						</button>
					{/each}
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<textarea
					rows="2"
					bind:value={tracker.reflectionNote}
					oninput={() => tracker.handleTextChanged()}
					placeholder="یادداشت کوتاه درباره اتفاقات یا دلایل امروز..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-sm text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>
		</div>
	{/if}

	{#if showTomorrowDrawer && cadenceHabits.length > 0}
		<div transition:slide={{ duration: 220 }} class="mt-4 space-y-4 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<div>
					<h3 class="text-sm font-black text-fg">
						تعیین هدف طرح‌های تکرارشونده برای فردا ({tomorrowHuman.fullLabel})
					</h3>
				</div>

				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => tracker.copyTodayActualsToTomorrowPlan()}
					class="flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg hover:border-border-strong"
				>
					<Copy class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text" />
					<span>کپی از امروز</span>
				</button>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
				{#each cadenceHabits as habit (habit.id)}
					{@const draft = tracker.tomorrowPlansByHabitId[habit.id]}
					{#if draft}
						<div
							class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3"
						>
							<span class="text-sm font-extrabold text-fg">{habit.title}</span>
							<label class="flex items-center gap-2">
								<input
									type="number"
									min="0"
									step="1"
									bind:value={draft.plannedMinutesPerInterval}
									class="h-10 w-20 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3 text-center font-mono text-base font-black text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
								/>
								<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">دقیقه</span>
							</label>
						</div>
					{/if}
				{/each}
			</div>

			<div class="flex justify-end">
				<button
					type="button"
					disabled={isSavingTomorrow}
					onclick={async () => {
						isSavingTomorrow = true;
						try {
							const payload = JSON.parse(tracker.serializeTomorrowPlanPayload());
							await clientDb.habits.saveTomorrowPlans(payload);
							showTomorrowDrawer = false;
						} finally {
							isSavingTomorrow = false;
						}
					}}
					class="h-10 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg transition active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{isSavingTomorrow ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ذخیره برنامه فردا'}
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

PATH: src/lib/components/tracker/DailyJournalSection.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import { BookHeart } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		journalText = $bindable(),
		onTextChange
	}: {
		journalText: string;
		onTextChange: () => void;
	} = $props();
</script>

<section
	class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition focus-within:border-border-strong sm:px-6"
>
	<div class="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
		<label for="daily-journal-input" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
			<BookHeart class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
			<span>خاطره و روایت امروز</span>
		</label>
		<a
			href="/memories"
			class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:text-accent-text"

		</a>
	</div>

	<textarea
		id="daily-journal-input"
		rows="2"
		bind:value={journalText}
		oninput={onTextChange}
		placeholder="چند خط درباره حس‌وحال، اتفاقات مهم یا خاطره این روز بنویسید..."
		class="w-full [field-sizing:content] min-h-19 resize-none overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-7 text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:bg-surface focus:outline-none"
	></textarea>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/tracker/UpcomingEventsBanner.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import type { CalendarEventEntity, UpcomingEventBannerItem } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import { BellRing, CalendarDays, Check, Clock } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		upcomingItems = [],
		selectedDayEvents = [],
		currentDateIso
	}: {
		upcomingItems: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
		selectedDayEvents: Array<
			Pick<CalendarEventEntity, 'id' | 'title' | 'isAnnual' | 'isCompleted'>
		>;
		currentDateIso: string;
	} = $props();

	let hasAnythingToShow = $derived(
		upcomingItems.length > 0 || selectedDayEvents.length > 0
	);

	function getRelativeText(days: number): string {
		if (days === -1) return 'دیروز (انجام‌نشده)';
		if (days === 0) return 'امروز';
		if (days === 1) return 'فردا';
		if (days === 2) return 'پس‌فردا';
		return `${toPersianDigits(days)} روز دیگر`;
	}
</script>

{#if hasAnythingToShow}
	<section
		class="rounded-3xl border border-accent-border bg-surface p-3.5 shadow-(--shadow-card) sm:px-5 sm:py-4"
	>
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
				<div
					class="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
				>
					<BellRing class="h-4 w-4" />
				</div>

				{#each selectedDayEvents as dayEv (dayEv.id)}
					<div
						class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text"
					>
						<CalendarDays class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
						<span>در این روز: {dayEv.title}</span>
						{#if !dayEv.isAnnual}
							<button
								type="button"
								onclick={() => clientDb.calendar.toggleComplete(dayEv.id, !dayEv.isCompleted)}
								class="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-current transition active:scale-95 {dayEv.isCompleted
									? 'bg-optimal text-on-solid'
									: 'bg-surface'}"
								title={dayEv.isCompleted ? 'انجام شد' : 'علامت به عنوان انجام‌شده'}
							>
								{#if dayEv.isCompleted}
									<Check class="h-3 w-3" />
								{/if}
							</button>
						{/if}
					</div>
				{/each}

				{#each upcomingItems.filter(
					(u) => !selectedDayEvents.some((s) => s.id === u.id)
				) as item (item.id + item.occurrenceDateIso)}
					<a
						href="/calendar?date={item.occurrenceDateIso}"
						class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong"
					>
						<span class="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-accent"></span>
						<span class="font-extrabold text-accent-text">
							{getRelativeText(item.daysRemaining)}:
						</span>
						<span>{item.title}</span>
					</a>
				{/each}
			</div>

			<a
				href="/calendar?date={currentDateIso}"
				class="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg"
			>
				<Clock class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text" />
				<span>مشاهده در تقویم</span>
			</a>
		</div>
	</section>
{/if}

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/tracker/CadenceHabitCard.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes } from '@core/habit-engine';
	import type { HabitEntity, HabitLogItemInput } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { CADENCE_DEFAULTS } from '@domain/constants';
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { Flame, Timer } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		habit,
		log,
		onUpdateActual
	}: {
		habit: HabitEntity;
		log: HabitLogItemInput | undefined;
		onUpdateActual: (
			habitId: string,
			intervals: number | null,
			minutesPerInterval: number
		) => void;
	} = $props();

	let plannedMinutes = $derived(
		log?.plannedMinutesSnapshot && log.plannedMinutesSnapshot > 0
			? log.plannedMinutesSnapshot
			: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval > 0
				? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
				: 10
	);
	let actualMinutes = $derived(log?.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? 0);
	let effectiveTotalMinutes = $derived(resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log));

	let progressPercent = $derived(
		plannedMinutes > 0
			? Math.min(100, Math.round((effectiveTotalMinutes / plannedMinutes) * 100))
			: 0
	);

	let quickPresets = $derived.by(() => {
		const set = new Set<number>([0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 30]);
		if (plannedMinutes > 0) set.add(plannedMinutes);
		return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => a - b);
	});
</script>

<div
	class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition-all duration-200 sm:px-6 sm:py-4 {effectiveTotalMinutes >=
	plannedMinutes
		? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft'
		: effectiveTotalMinutes > 0
			? 'border-accent-border bg-accent-soft'
			: 'border-border bg-surface'}"
>
	<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3.5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
		<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
			<div
				class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border bg-surface {effectiveTotalMinutes >=
				plannedMinutes
					? 'border-optimal-border text-optimal'
					: 'border-accent-border text-accent-text'}"
			>
				<Timer class="h-5 w-5" />
			</div>

			<div class="space-y-0.5">
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
					<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">{habit.title}</h3>
					<span
						class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg"
					>
						{toPersianDigits(effectiveTotalMinutes)}
						<span class="text-fg-muted">/ {toPersianDigits(plannedMinutes)} دقیقه</span>
					</span>
				</div>
				{#if habit.scheduleLabel}
					<p class="text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary">{habit.scheduleLabel}</p>
				{/if}
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1 lg:w-auto">
			{#each quickPresets as preset (preset)}
				{@const isSelected = actualMinutes === preset}
				{@const isGoal = preset === plannedMinutes && preset > 0}
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() =>
						onUpdateActual(
							habit.id,
							null,
							isSelected && preset !== 0 ? 0 : preset
						)}
					class="relative h-9.5 min-w-10 flex-1 rounded-xl px-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition active:scale-95 sm:h-10 sm:min-w-12 sm:flex-initial sm:px-3 {isSelected
						? preset === 0
							? 'bg-failed text-on-solid shadow-xs'
							: isGoal
								? 'bg-optimal text-on-solid shadow-xs'
								: 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
						: isGoal
							? 'border border-dashed border-accent-border text-accent-text hover:bg-surface'
							: 'text-fg-secondary hover:bg-surface hover:text-fg'}"
					title={isSelected && preset !== 0
						? 'کلیک مجدد برای صفر کردن'
						: isGoal
							? `هدف تعیین‌شده (${toPersianDigits(preset)} دقیقه)`
							: ''}
				>
					{preset === 0 ? 'صفر' : toPersianDigits(preset)}
				</button>
			{/each}

			{#if habit.emergencyMinDesc}
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() =>
						onUpdateActual(habit.id, null, CADENCE_DEFAULTS.EMERGENCY_MINUTES)}
					class="flex h-9.5 items-center gap-1 rounded-xl px-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:h-10 {actualMinutes ===
					CADENCE_DEFAULTS.EMERGENCY_MINUTES
						? 'bg-bronze text-on-solid shadow-xs'
						: 'text-bronze-text hover:bg-surface'}"
					title={habit.emergencyMinDesc}
				>
					<Flame class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
					<span>{toPersianDigits(CADENCE_DEFAULTS.EMERGENCY_MINUTES)}</span>
				</button>
			{/if}
		</div>
	</div>

	<div class="mt-3.5 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
		<div
			class="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300 {effectiveTotalMinutes >= plannedMinutes
				? 'bg-optimal'
				: effectiveTotalMinutes > 0
					? 'bg-accent'
					: 'bg-transparent'}"
			style="width: {progressPercent}%"
		></div>
	</div>
</div>

<script lang="ts">
	import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS, MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { MediaEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import { CheckCircle2, Pencil, Star, Trash2 } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		item,
		onEdit
	}: {
		item: MediaEntity;
		onEdit: (item: MediaEntity) => void;
	} = $props();

	let categoryMeta = $derived(
		MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS.find((c) => c.value === item.category) ?? MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS[0]
	);

	let statusMeta = $derived(
		MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS.find((s) => s.value === item.status) ?? MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS[0]
	);
</script>

<div
	class="group flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface px-4 py-3 shadow-(--shadow-card) transition hover:border-border-strong sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-5 sm:py-3.5"
>
	<div class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
			<span class="rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold {categoryMeta.badgeClasses}">
				{categoryMeta.label}
			</span>

			<h3 class="truncate text-sm font-black text-fg sm:text-base">{item.title}</h3>

			<span class="rounded-lg px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold {statusMeta.badgeClasses}">
				{statusMeta.badgeLabel}
			</span>

			{#if item.rating}
				<div class="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-gold" title="{item.rating} ستاره">
					{#each Array.from({ length: item.rating }) as _, idx (idx)}
						<Star class="h-3 w-3 fill-current" />
					{/each}
				</div>
			{/if}
		</div>

		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-fg-secondary">
			{#if item.finishedAtIso}
				<span class="text-fg-muted">
					تماشا:
					<strong class="font-mono font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(item.finishedAtIso))}
					</strong>
				</span>
			{:else if item.startedAtIso}
				<span class="text-fg-muted">
					شروع:
					<strong class="font-mono font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(item.startedAtIso))}
					</strong>
				</span>
			{/if}
			{#if item.notes}
				<span class="truncate text-[11px] text-fg-muted" title={item.notes}>
					«{item.notes}»
				</span>
			{/if}
		</div>
	</div>

	<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-end gap-2 border-t border-border pt-2 sm:border-t-0 sm:pt-0">
		{#if item.status !== 'completed'}
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => clientDb.media.updateStatus(item.id, 'completed')}
				class="flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-optimal-soft px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-optimal transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
			>
				<CheckCircle2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
				<span>دیده شد</span>
			</button>
		{/if}

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={() => onEdit(item)}
			class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
			title="ویرایش اطلاعات اثر"
		>
			<Pencil class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
		</button>

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={() => clientDb.media.delete(item.id)}
			class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
			title="حذف اثر"
		>
			<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
		</button>
	</div>
</div>

<script lang="ts">
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS } from '@domain/constants';
	import type {
		MediaCategory,
		MediaSortBy,
		MediaStatusFilter
	} from '@domain/schemas';

	import { Clapperboard, Plus } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		totalCount,
		unseenCount,
		categoryFilter = $bindable(),
		statusFilter = $bindable(),
		sortBy = $bindable(),
		onOpenCreate
	}: {
		totalCount: number;
		unseenCount: number;
		categoryFilter: MediaCategory | 'ALL';
		statusFilter: MediaStatusFilter;
		sortBy: MediaSortBy;
		onOpenCreate?: () => void;
	} = $props();

	const sortOptions: ReadonlyArray<{ value: MediaSortBy; label: string }> = [
		{ value: 'rating_desc', label: 'مرتب‌سازی: بیشترین ستاره ★' },
		{ value: 'newest', label: 'مرتب‌سازی: جدیدترین‌ها' },
		{ value: 'oldest', label: 'مرتب‌سازی: قدیمی‌ترین‌ها' },
		{ value: 'title_asc', label: 'مرتب‌سازی: حروف الفبا' }
	];
</script>

<div
	class="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-6"
>
	<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
		<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
			<div
				class="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
			>
				<Clapperboard class="h-5 w-5" />

				<h1 class="text-lg font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-xl">فیلم، سریال و انیمه</h1>

				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => (statusFilter = 'ALL')}
					class="flex-1 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3 {statusFilter === 'ALL'
						? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
						: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"

					class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3 {statusFilter ===
					'UNSEEN'

	</div>

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={() => (categoryFilter = 'ALL')}
			class="rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition {categoryFilter === 'ALL'
				? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
				: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"

			همه دسته‌ها
		</button>
		{#each MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS as cat (cat.value)}
			<button

				onclick={() => (categoryFilter = cat.value)}
				class="rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition {categoryFilter ===
				cat.value
					? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
					: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"

			</button>
		{/each}
	</div>
</div>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/media/MediaUpsertModal.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import {
		BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS,
		MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS,
		MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS
	} from '@domain/constants';
	import type { MediaCategory, MediaEntity, MediaStatus } from '@domain/schemas';
	import BaseModal from '$lib/components/ui/BaseModal.svelte';
	import CustomSelect from '$lib/components/ui/CustomSelect.svelte';
	import { persianDigits } from '$lib/actions/persian-input';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import { Clapperboard, History } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		editingMedia = null,
		existingSuggestions = [],
		onClose
	}: {
		editingMedia?: MediaEntity | null;
		existingSuggestions?: Array<{ title: string; category: MediaCategory }>;
		onClose: () => void;
	} = $props();

	const defaultJalaliToday = toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(getTodayIso()));
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	const initialStatus: MediaStatus = editingMedia?.status ?? 'completed';

	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let title = $state(editingMedia?.title ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedCategory = $state<MediaCategory>(editingMedia?.category ?? 'movie');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedStatus = $state<MediaStatus>(initialStatus);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedRating = $state<number>(editingMedia?.rating ?? 5);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let startedJalali = $state(
		editingMedia?.startedAtIso
			? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(editingMedia.startedAtIso))
			: ''
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let finishedJalali = $state(
		editingMedia?.finishedAtIso
			? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(editingMedia.finishedAtIso))
			: initialStatus === 'completed'
				? defaultJalaliToday
				: ''
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let notes = $state(editingMedia?.notes ?? '');
	let isSubmitting = $state(false);

	let matchedSuggestion = $derived(
		existingSuggestions.find(
			(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === title.trim().toLowerCase()
		)
	);

	function handleTitleChange(e: Event) {
		const val = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value;
		title = val;
		const match = existingSuggestions.find(
			(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === val.trim().toLowerCase()
		);
		if (match && !editingMedia) {
			selectedCategory = match.category;
		}
	}

	async function handleSubmit(e: SubmitEvent) {
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!title.trim() || isSubmitting) return;
		isSubmitting = true;

		try {
			if (editingMedia) {
				await clientDb.media.update({
					mediaId: editingMedia.id,
					title: title.trim(),
					category: selectedCategory,
					status: selectedStatus,
					startedJalali: startedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					finishedJalali: finishedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					rating: selectedRating,
					notes: notes.trim() || undefined
				});
			} else {
				await clientDb.media.create({
					title: title.trim(),
					category: selectedCategory,
					status: selectedStatus,
					startedJalali: startedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					finishedJalali: finishedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					rating: selectedRating,
					notes: notes.trim() || undefined
				});
			}
			onClose();
		} finally {
			isSubmitting = false;
		}
	}
</script>

<BaseModal title={editingMedia ? 'ویرایش اطلاعات اثر' : 'ثبت اثر جدید'} {onClose}>
	{#snippet headerIcon()}
		<Clapperboard class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
	{/snippet}

	<form onsubmit={handleSubmit} class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6">
		<div>
			<span class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نوع اثر</span>
			<input type="hidden" name="category" value={selectedCategory} />
			<div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
				{#each MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS as catOpt (catOpt.value)}
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() => (selectedCategory = catOpt.value)}
						class="h-9 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition {selectedCategory ===
						catOpt.value
							? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
							: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
					>
						{catOpt.label}
					</button>
				{/each}
			</div>
		</div>

		<div>
			<div class="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between">
				<label for="md-title" class="block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نام اثر</label>
				{#if matchedSuggestion && !editingMedia}
					<span
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-accent-text"
					>
						<History class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>قبلاً در آرشیو ثبت شده (تماشای مجدد)</span>
					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>

			<input
				id="md-title"
				name="title"
				list="existing-media-datalist"
				required
				bind:value={title}
				oninput={handleTitleChange}
				placeholder="مثلاً: Interstellar یا Attack on Titan..."
				class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
			/>

			<datalist id="existing-media-datalist">
				{#each existingSuggestions as item (item.title)}
					<option value={item.title}></option>
				{/each}
			</datalist>
		</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<div>
					<span class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">وضعیت</span>
					<input type="hidden" name="status" value={selectedStatus} />
					<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
						{#each MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS as statusOpt (statusOpt.value)}
							<button
								type="button"
								onclick={() => (selectedStatus = statusOpt.value)}
								class="h-9 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition {selectedStatus ===
								statusOpt.value
									? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
									: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
							>
								{statusOpt.label}
							</button>
						{/each}
					</div>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="md-rating" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						امتیاز
					</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="md-rating"
						name="rating"
						bind:value={selectedRating}
						options={BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS}
						triggerClass="text-gold"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
				<div>
					<label for="md-start" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						تاریخ شروع
					</label>
					<input
						id="md-start"
						name="startedJalali"
						dir="ltr"
						use:persianDigits
						bind:value={startedJalali}
						placeholder={defaultJalaliToday}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="md-end" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						تاریخ تماشا
					</label>
					<input
						id="md-end"
						name="finishedJalali"
						dir="ltr"
						use:persianDigits
						bind:value={finishedJalali}
						placeholder={defaultJalaliToday}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="md-notes" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					یادداشت کوتاه (برداشت، نوبت تماشا، اپیزود یا نکته داستان...)
				</label>
				<textarea
					id="md-notes"
					name="notes"
					rows="3"
					bind:value={notes}
					placeholder="نظر یا نکته کوتاه درباره این اثر..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={onClose}
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl px-5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					disabled={isSubmitting}
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-7 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : editingMedia ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت در آرشیو'}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
</BaseModal>

PATH: src/lib/components/calendar/CalendarMonthGrid.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import {
		JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS,
		JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT,
		toPersianDigits
	} from '@domain/calendar';
	import { getDayStatusMeta } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { CalendarDayAggregate } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { BookHeart, BookOpen, CalendarClock, Clapperboard } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		leadingEmptyCells,
		days,
		selectedIso,
		onSelectDay
	}: {
		leadingEmptyCells: number;
		days: CalendarDayAggregate[];
		selectedIso: string;
		onSelectDay: (iso: string) => void;
	} = $props();

	function getDayCellClasses(day: CalendarDayAggregate, isSelected: boolean): string {
		const ring = isSelected
			? 'border-2 border-accent ring-2 ring-accent-border/60 shadow-(--shadow-card) scale-[1.02] z-10'
			: day.isToday
				? 'border-2 border-accent-border'
				: 'border border-border hover:border-border-strong';

		if (day.entry?.dayStatus === 'optimal') {
			return `${ring} bg-optimal-soft`;
		}
		if (day.entry?.dayStatus === 'partial') {
			return `${ring} bg-partial-soft`;
		}
		if (day.entry?.dayStatus === 'failed') {
			return `${ring} bg-failed-soft`;
		}
		return `${ring} bg-elevated/85 hover:bg-elevated`;
	}
</script>

<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-5">
	<div class="mb-2.5 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 text-center sm:gap-2">
		{#each JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS as weekday, idx (weekday)}
			<div class="py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary sm:text-xs">
				<span class="hidden sm:inline">{weekday}</span>
				<span class="sm:hidden">{JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT[idx]}</span>
			</div>
		{/each}
	</div>

	<div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
		{#each Array.from({ length: leadingEmptyCells }) as _, idx (idx)}
			<div
				class="aspect-square rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/40 bg-elevated/20"
			></div>
		{/each}

		{#each days as day (day.iso)}
			{@const isSelected = day.iso === selectedIso}
			{@const hasEvent = day.events.length > 0}
			{@const hasBook = day.booksActivity.length > 0}
			{@const hasMedia = day.mediaActivity.length > 0}
			{@const hasJournal = Boolean(day.entry?.journalText)}

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => onSelectDay(day.iso)}
				class="group relative flex aspect-square flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl p-1.5 text-right transition-all duration-150 sm:p-2 {getDayCellClasses(
					day,
					isSelected
				)}"
			>
				<div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1">
					<span
						class=" leading-none font-black tracking-tight {day.isToday
							? 'rounded-lg bg-accent px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-accent-fg sm:text-sm'
							: 'text-sm text-fg sm:text-base'}"
					>
						{toPersianDigits(day.jd)}
					</span>

					{#if day.entry}
						<span
							class="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full {getDayStatusMeta(day.entry.dayStatus).dotClass}"
						></span>
					{/if}
				</div>

				<div class="mt-auto flex w-full items-center justify-start gap-1">
					{#if hasEvent}
						<span
							class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-2xs"
							title={day.events.map((e) => e.title).join('، ')}
						>
							<CalendarClock class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
						</span>
					{/if}
					{#if hasBook}
						<span
							class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-optimal text-on-solid shadow-2xs"
							title="کتاب"
						>
							<BookOpen class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
						</span>
					{/if}
					{#if hasMedia}
						<span
							class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-bronze text-on-solid shadow-2xs"
							title="فیلم / انیمه"
						>
							<Clapperboard class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
						</span>
					{/if}
					{#if hasJournal}
						<span
							class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-silver text-on-solid shadow-2xs"
							title="خاطره ثبت‌شده"
						>
							<BookHeart class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
						</span>
					{/if}
				</div>
			</button>
		{/each}
	</div>
</div>

PATH: src/lib/components/calendar/CalendarDayInspector.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import { slide } from 'svelte/transition';
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { getDayStatusMeta, getTierBadgeMeta, TIER_SCORES } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { CalendarDayAggregate } from '@domain/schemas';
	import BaseModal from '$lib/components/ui/BaseModal.svelte';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import {
		ArrowLeft,
		BookOpen,
		CalendarPlus,
		Check,
		ChevronDown,
		ChevronUp,
		Clapperboard,
		Clock,
		Eye,
		Plus,
		Star,
		Trash2
	} from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		day
	}: {
		day: CalendarDayAggregate;
	} = $props();

	let isAnnualChecked = $state(false);
	let showAddEventForm = $state(false);
	let eventTitleInput = $state('');
	let eventNotesInput = $state('');
	let isSubmittingEvent = $state(false);
	let isModalOpen = $state(false);
	let expandedHabitId = $state<string | null>(null);

	async function handleAddEvent(e: SubmitEvent) {
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!eventTitleInput.trim() || isSubmittingEvent) return;
		isSubmittingEvent = true;

		try {
			await clientDb.calendar.create({
				title: eventTitleInput.trim(),
				eventType: 'occasion',
				isAnnual: isAnnualChecked,
				dateIso: day.iso,
				notes: eventNotesInput.trim() || undefined
			});
			eventTitleInput = '';
			eventNotesInput = '';
			isAnnualChecked = false;
			showAddEventForm = false;
		} finally {
			isSubmittingEvent = false;
		}
	}

	function openModalWithHabit(habitId?: string) {
		expandedHabitId = habitId ?? null;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function closeModal() {
		isModalOpen = false;
		expandedHabitId = null;
	}

	function toggleHabitExpanded(habitId: string) {
		expandedHabitId = expandedHabitId === habitId ? null : habitId;
	}
</script>

<aside class="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
	<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-4">
		<div>
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
				{#if day.entry}
					<span
						class="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 rounded-full {getDayStatusMeta(day.entry.dayStatus).dotClass}"
					></span>
				{/if}
				<h2 class="text-lg font-black text-fg sm:text-xl">{day.fullHumanLabel}</h2>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => (showAddEventForm = !showAddEventForm)}
				class="flex h-10 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-3.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت رویداد در این روز</span>
			</button>

			<a
				href="/?date={day.iso}"
				class="flex h-10 items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong"
				title="باز کردن در صفحه ثبت روزانه"
			>
				<span>ثبت روزانه</span>
				<ArrowLeft class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
			</a>
		</div>
	</div>

	{#if showAddEventForm}
		<form
			onsubmit={handleAddEvent}
			transition:slide={{ duration: 180 }}
			class="space-y-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-4"
		>
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
				<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-black text-accent-text">
					<CalendarPlus class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>رویداد جدید برای {day.fullHumanLabel}</span>
				</span>
			</div>

			<div>
				<input
					bind:value={eventTitleInput}
					required
					placeholder="مثلاً: تولد امیر / تحویل پروژه پایانی..."
					class="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div>
				<input
					bind:value={eventNotesInput}
					placeholder="توضیح کوتاه (اختیاری)..."
					class="h-9 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
				<label class="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-fg">
					<input
						type="checkbox"
						bind:checked={isAnnualChecked}
						class="h-4 w-4 rounded accent-accent"
					/>
					<span>تکرار هرساله در همین تاریخ شمسی</span>
				</label>

				<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() => (showAddEventForm = false)}
						class="h-9 rounded-xl px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-surface"
					>
						لغو
					</button>
					<button
						type="submit"
						disabled={isSubmittingEvent}
						class="h-9 rounded-xl bg-accent px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg disabled:opacity-60"
					>
						{isSubmittingEvent ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ذخیره رویداد'}
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</form>
	{/if}

	<div class="space-y-3">
		<h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">رویدادها و مناسبت‌های این روز</h3>
		{#if day.events.length === 0}
			<div class="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border bg-elevated p-4 text-center text-xs text-fg-muted">
				رویداد یا مناسبتی برای این تاریخ ثبت نشده است.
			</div>
		{:else}
			<div class="space-y-2">
				{#each day.events as ev (ev.id)}
					<div
						class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-3 text-accent-text"
					>
						<div class="min-w-0 space-y-0.5">
							<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
								<span
									class="text-sm font-black text-fg {ev.isCompleted
										? 'line-through opacity-75'
										: ''}"
								>
									{ev.title}
								</span>
								{#if ev.isAnnual}
									<span class="rounded-lg border border-current px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold">
										هرساله
									</span>
								{/if}
							</div>
							{#if ev.notes}
								<p class="text-xs opacity-90">{ev.notes}</p>
							{/if}
						</div>

						<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
							{#if !ev.isAnnual}
								<button
									type="button"
									onclick={() => clientDb.calendar.toggleComplete(ev.id, !ev.isCompleted)}
									class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-current bg-surface text-fg transition hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"
									title={ev.isCompleted ? 'انجام شد' : 'علامت انجام شدن'}
								>
									<Check
										class="h-4 w-4 {ev.isCompleted ? 'text-optimal' : 'text-fg-muted'}"
									/>
								</button>
							{/if}

							<button
								type="button"
								onclick={() => clientDb.calendar.delete(ev.id)}
								class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-surface text-fg-muted transition hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
								title="حذف رویداد"
							>
								<Trash2 class="h-4 w-4" />
							</button>
						</div>
					</div>
				{/each}
			</div>
		{/if}
	</div>

	{#if day.booksActivity.length > 0 || day.mediaActivity.length > 0}
		<div class="space-y-2.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">کتاب‌ها و آثار تماشا شده در این روز</h3>
			<div class="space-y-2">
				{#each day.booksActivity as item (item.book.id + item.activityKind)}
					<div
						class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs"
					>
						<div class="flex items-center gap-2 font-extrabold text-fg">
							<BookOpen class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-optimal" />
							<span>
								{item.activityKind === 'finished' ? 'اتمام کتاب:' : 'شروع کتاب:'}
								{item.book.title}
							</span>
						</div>
						{#if item.book.rating}
							<span class="flex items-center gap-0.5 font-black text-gold-text">
								<Star class="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-current" />
								{toPersianDigits(item.book.rating)}
							</span>
						{/if}
					</div>
				{/each}

				{#each day.mediaActivity as item (item.media.id + item.activityKind)}
					<div
						class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs"
					>
						<div class="flex items-center gap-2 font-extrabold text-fg">
							<Clapperboard class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-bronze-text" />
							<span>
								{item.activityKind === 'finished' ? 'تماشای کامل:' : 'شروع تماشا:'}
								{item.media.title}
							</span>
						</div>
						{#if item.media.rating}
							<span class="flex items-center gap-0.5 font-black text-gold-text">
								<Star class="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-current" />
								{toPersianDigits(item.media.rating)}
							</span>
						{/if}
					</div>
				{/each}
			</div>
		</div>
	{/if}

	<div class="space-y-2.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
				<h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">عملکرد طرح‌ها در این روز</h3>
				{#if day.entry}
					{@const statusMeta = getDayStatusMeta(day.entry.dayStatus)}
					<span
						class="rounded-xl px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black {statusMeta.softBadgeClasses}"
					>
						{statusMeta.shortBadgeLabel}
					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>

			{#if day.habitPerformances.length > 0}
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => openModalWithHabit()}
					class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-accent-border hover:text-accent-text"
				>
					<Eye class="h-3 w-3" />
					<span>دیدن جزئیات</span>
				</button>
			{/if}
		</div>

		{#if day.habitPerformances.length === 0}
			<div
				class="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border bg-elevated p-4 text-center text-xs text-fg-muted"
			>
				عملکردی برای طرح‌ها در این تاریخ ثبت نشده است.
			</div>
		{:else}
			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
				{#each day.habitPerformances as perf (perf.habitId)}
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() => openModalWithHabit(perf.habitId)}
						class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-right text-xs transition hover:border-border-strong hover:bg-muted/60 active:scale-98"
						title="کلیک برای مشاهده جزئیات این طرح"
					>
						<span class="truncate font-bold text-fg">{perf.title}</span>

						{#if perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'}
							{@const badge = getTierBadgeMeta(perf.achievedTier)}
							<span
								class="shrink-0 rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-black {badge.classes}"
							>
								{badge.label}
							</span>
						{:else}
							<span
								class="shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-black text-accent-text"
							>
								{toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0)} دقیقه
							</span>
						{/if}
					</button>
				{/each}
			</div>
		{/if}
	</div>
</aside>

{#if isModalOpen}
	<BaseModal
		title="جزئیات عملکرد طرح‌ها"
		subtitle={day.fullHumanLabel}
		maxWidthClass="max-w-xl"
		onClose={closeModal}
	>
		<div class="space-y-3 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6 max-h-[75vh]">
			{#each day.habitPerformances as perf (perf.habitId)}
				{@const isExpanded = expandedHabitId === perf.habitId}
				{@const spec = perf.specSnapshot}
				<div
					class="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated transition hover:border-border-strong"
				>
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() => toggleHabitExpanded(perf.habitId)}
						class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3.5 text-right transition hover:bg-muted/40"
					>
						<div class="min-w-0 space-y-1">
							<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
								<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{perf.title}</span>
								{#if perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'}
									{@const badge = getTierBadgeMeta(perf.achievedTier)}
									<span class="rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black {badge.classes}">
										{badge.label}
									</span>
								{:else}
									<span class="rounded-lg border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-accent-text">
										{toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0)} دقیقه
									</span>
								{/if}
								{#if perf.isArchived}
									<span class="rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
										بایگانی‌شده
									</span>
								{/if}
							</div>

							<p class="text-xs font-medium text-fg-secondary">
								{#if perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'}
									{perf.achievedRule || 'بدون توضیح ثبت‌شده'}
								{:else}
									انجام‌شده: {toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0)} دقیقه از هدفِ {toPersianDigits(
										perf.plannedMinutes ?? 0
									)} دقیقه
								{/if}
							</p>
						</div>

						<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary shrink-0">
							<span class="text-[11px]">{isExpanded ? 'بستن' : 'قوانین'}</span>
							{#if isExpanded}
								<ChevronUp class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
							{:else}
								<ChevronDown class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-muted" />
							{/if}
						</div>
					</button>

					{#if isExpanded}
						<div transition:slide={{ duration: 160 }} class="space-y-3 border-t border-border bg-surface p-4 text-xs">
							{#if spec.scheduleLabel}
								<div class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3.5 py-2">
									<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-fg-secondary">
										<Clock class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-fg-muted" />
										<span>زمان انجام:</span>
									</span>
									<span class="font-extrabold text-fg">{spec.scheduleLabel}</span>
								</div>
							{/if}

							{#if perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'}
								<div class="space-y-1.5">
									<div class="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">شرایط سطوح در این روز:</div>
									{#if spec.bronzeDesc}
										<div class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 {perf.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE ? 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft' : 'border-border bg-elevated/60'}">
											<span class="font-extrabold text-bronze-text">برنز:</span>
											<span class="font-bold text-fg">{spec.bronzeDesc}</span>
										</div>
									{/if}
									{#if spec.silverDesc && spec.silverDesc !== '~'}
										<div class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 {perf.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER ? 'border-silver-border bg-silver-soft' : 'border-border bg-elevated/60'}">
											<span class="font-extrabold text-silver-text">نقره:</span>
											<span class="font-bold text-fg">{spec.silverDesc}</span>
										</div>
									{/if}
									{#if spec.goldDesc && spec.goldDesc !== '~'}
										<div class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 {perf.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD ? 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft' : 'border-border bg-elevated/60'}">
											<span class="font-extrabold text-gold-text">طلا:</span>
											<span class="font-bold text-fg">{spec.goldDesc}</span>
										</div>
									{/if}
								</div>
							{:else}
								<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
									<div class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated p-2.5 text-center">
										<div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">انجام‌شده</div>
										<div class="mt-0.5 font-mono text-sm font-black text-accent-text">
											{toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0)} دقیقه
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated p-2.5 text-center">
										<div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">هدف روز</div>
										<div class="mt-0.5 font-mono text-sm font-black text-fg">
											{toPersianDigits(perf.plannedMinutes ?? 0)} دقیقه
										</div>
									</div>
								</div>
								{#if spec.emergencyMinDesc}
									<div class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2">
										<span class="font-bold text-fg-secondary">حداقل اضطراری:</span>
										<span class="font-bold text-fg">{spec.emergencyMinDesc}</span>
									</div>
								{/if}
							{/if}

							{#if spec.notes}
								<div class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-fg-secondary">
									<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">تبصره: </strong>
									<span>{spec.notes}</span>
								</div>
							{/if}
						</div>
					{/if}
				</div>
			{/each}
		</div>
	</BaseModal>
{/if}

PATH: src/lib/components/books/BookItemCard.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { BookEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import { CheckCircle2, Pencil, Star, Trash2 } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		book,
		onEdit
	}: {
		book: BookEntity;
		onEdit: (book: BookEntity) => void;
	} = $props();

	let statusMeta = $derived(
		BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS.find((s) => s.value === book.status) ?? BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS[0]
	);
</script>

<div
	class="group flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface px-4 py-3 shadow-(--shadow-card) transition hover:border-border-strong sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-5 sm:py-3.5"
>
	<div class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
			<h3 class="truncate text-sm font-black text-fg sm:text-base">{book.title}</h3>

			<span class="rounded-lg px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold {statusMeta.badgeClasses}">
				{statusMeta.badgeLabel}
			</span>

			{#if book.rating}
				<div class="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-gold" title="{book.rating} ستاره">
					{#each Array.from({ length: book.rating }) as _, idx (idx)}
						<Star class="h-3 w-3 fill-current" />
					{/each}
				</div>
			{/if}
		</div>

		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-fg-secondary">
			{#if book.author}
				<span class="font-medium text-fg-muted">نویسنده: <strong class="font-semibold text-fg-secondary">{book.author}</strong></span>
			{/if}
			{#if book.finishedAtIso}
				<span class="text-fg-muted">
					اتمام:
					<strong class="font-mono font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(book.finishedAtIso))}
					</strong>
				</span>
			{/if}
			{#if book.notes}
				<span class="truncate text-[11px] text-fg-muted" title={book.notes}>
					«{book.notes}»
				</span>
			{/if}
		</div>
	</div>

	<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-end gap-2 border-t border-border pt-2 sm:border-t-0 sm:pt-0">
		{#if book.status !== 'completed'}
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => clientDb.books.updateStatus(book.id, 'completed')}
				class="flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-optimal-soft px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-optimal transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
			>
				<CheckCircle2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
				<span>تکمیل شد</span>
			</button>
		{/if}

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={() => onEdit(book)}
			class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
			title="ویرایش اطلاعات کتاب"
		>
			<Pencil class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
		</button>

		<button
			type="button"
			onclick={() => clientDb.books.delete(book.id)}
			class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
			title="حذف کتاب"
		>
			<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
		</button>
	</div>
</div>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/components/books/BookUpsertModal.svelte
================================================================

<script lang="ts">
	import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS, BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS } from '@domain/constants';
	import type { BookEntity, BookStatus } from '@domain/schemas';
	import BaseModal from '$lib/components/ui/BaseModal.svelte';
	import CustomSelect from '$lib/components/ui/CustomSelect.svelte';
	import { persianDigits } from '$lib/actions/persian-input';
	import { clientDb } from '$lib/state/client-db';
	import { BookOpen, History } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		editingBook = null,
		existingSuggestions = [],
		onClose
	}: {
		editingBook?: BookEntity | null;
		existingSuggestions?: Array<{ title: string; author: string | null }>;
		onClose: () => void;
	} = $props();

	const defaultJalaliToday = toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(getTodayIso()));

	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let title = $state(editingBook?.title ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let author = $state(editingBook?.author ?? '');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedStatus = $state<BookStatus>(editingBook?.status ?? 'completed');
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let selectedRating = $state<number>(editingBook?.rating ?? 5);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let startedJalali = $state(
		editingBook?.startedAtIso
			? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(editingBook.startedAtIso))
			: ''
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let finishedJalali = $state(
		editingBook?.finishedAtIso
			? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(editingBook.finishedAtIso))
			: defaultJalaliToday
	);
	// svelte-ignore state_referenced_locally
	let notes = $state(editingBook?.notes ?? '');
	let isSubmitting = $state(false);

	let matchedSuggestion = $derived(
		existingSuggestions.find(
			(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === title.trim().toLowerCase()
		)
	);

	function handleTitleChange(e: Event) {
		const val = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value;
		title = val;
		const match = existingSuggestions.find(
			(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === val.trim().toLowerCase()
		);
		if (match && match.author && !editingBook) {
			author = match.author;
		}
	}

	async function handleSubmit(e: SubmitEvent) {
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!title.trim() || isSubmitting) return;
		isSubmitting = true;

		try {
			if (editingBook) {
				await clientDb.books.update({
					bookId: editingBook.id,
					title: title.trim(),
					author: author.trim() || undefined,
					status: selectedStatus,
					startedJalali: startedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					finishedJalali: finishedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					rating: selectedRating,
					notes: notes.trim() || undefined
				});
			} else {
				await clientDb.books.create({
					title: title.trim(),
					author: author.trim() || undefined,
					status: selectedStatus,
					startedJalali: startedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					finishedJalali: finishedJalali.trim() || undefined,
					rating: selectedRating,
					notes: notes.trim() || undefined
				});
			}
			onClose();
		} finally {
			isSubmitting = false;
		}
	}
</script>

<BaseModal title={editingBook ? 'ویرایش اطلاعات کتاب' : 'افزودن کتاب جدید'} {onClose}>
	{#snippet headerIcon()}
		<BookOpen class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
	{/snippet}

	<form onsubmit={handleSubmit} class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6">
		<div>
			<div class="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between">
				<label for="bk-title" class="block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نام کتاب</label>
				{#if matchedSuggestion && !editingBook}
					<span
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-accent-text"
					>
						<History class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>قبلاً در کتابخانه ثبت شده (خواندن مجدد)</span>
					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>

			<input
				id="bk-title"
				name="title"
				list="existing-books-datalist"
				required
				bind:value={title}
				oninput={handleTitleChange}
				placeholder="مثلاً: عادت‌های اتمی یا صحیفه سجادیه..."
				class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
			/>

			<datalist id="existing-books-datalist">
				{#each existingSuggestions as s (s.title)}
					<option value={s.title}>{s.author ? `اثر: ${s.author}` : ''}</option>
				{/each}
			</datalist>
		</div>

		<div>
			<label for="bk-author" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
				نویسنده / مترجم
			</label>
			<input
				id="bk-author"
				name="author"
				bind:value={author}
				placeholder="مثلاً: جیمز کلیر"
				class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
			/>
		</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
				<div>
					<label for="bk-status" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">وضعیت</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="bk-status"
						name="status"
						bind:value={selectedStatus}
						options={BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS}
						triggerClass="text-fg"
					/>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="bk-rating" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						امتیاز
					</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="bk-rating"
						name="rating"
						bind:value={selectedRating}
						options={BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS}
						triggerClass="text-gold"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
				<div>
					<label for="bk-start" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						تاریخ شروع
					</label>
					<input
						id="bk-start"
						name="startedJalali"
						dir="ltr"
						use:persianDigits
						bind:value={startedJalali}
						placeholder={defaultJalaliToday}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="bk-end" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						تاریخ اتمام
					</label>
					<input
						id="bk-end"
						name="finishedJalali"
						dir="ltr"
						use:persianDigits
						bind:value={finishedJalali}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="bk-notes" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					نکات کلیدی یا خلاصه برداشت (نوبت مطالعه، خلاصه، نقل‌قول و...)
				</label>
				<textarea
					id="bk-notes"
					name="notes"
					rows="3"
					bind:value={notes}
					placeholder="مثلاً: نوبت دوم مطالعه در سال ۱۴۰۵ / نکات مهم فصل سوم..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={onClose}
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl px-5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					disabled={isSubmitting}
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-7 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : editingBook ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت در کتابخانه'}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
</BaseModal>

<script lang="ts">
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { BookOpen, Plus } from 'lucide-svelte';

	let {
		completedCount,
		readingCount,
		onOpenCreate
	}: {
		completedCount: number;
		readingCount: number;
		onOpenCreate?: () => void;
	} = $props();
</script>

<div
	class="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4.5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-6 sm:p-6 sm:px-8"
>
	<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
		<div
			class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text sm:h-12 sm:w-12"
		>
			<BookOpen class="h-5 w-5 sm:h-6 sm:w-6" />
		</div>
		<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">کتابخانه</h1>
	</div>

	<div
		class="flex w-full flex-col gap-3 sm:w-auto sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:justify-end sm:gap-3.5"
	>
		<div class="grid w-full grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:w-auto sm:flex sm:items-center sm:gap-3.5">
			<div
				class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft px-3.5 py-2.5 text-center sm:min-w-32 sm:px-5 sm:py-3"
			>
				<div class="font-mono text-xl font-black text-optimal sm:text-2xl">{toPersianDigits(completedCount)}</div>
				<div class="mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-bold text-optimal">خوانده‌شده</div>
			</div>
			<div
				class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft px-3.5 py-2.5 text-center sm:min-w-32 sm:px-5 sm:py-3"
			>
				<div class="font-mono text-xl font-black text-gold-text sm:text-2xl">{toPersianDigits(readingCount)}</div>
				<div class="mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-bold text-gold-text">در حال مطالعه</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		{#if onOpenCreate}
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={onOpenCreate}
				class="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:h-12 sm:w-auto sm:text-sm"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>افزودن کتاب جدید</span>
			</button>
		{/if}
	</div>
</div>

================================================================
PATH: src/lib/actions/persian-input.ts
================================================================

import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';

/**
 * Svelte Action to convert typed or pasted Latin/Arabic digits
 * into Persian digits in real-time, preserving cursor position.
 */
export function persianDigits(node: HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement) {
	function handleInput() {
		const start = node.selectionStart;
		const end = node.selectionEnd;
		const original = node.value;
		const converted = toPersianDigits(original);

		if (original !== converted) {
			node.value = converted;
			if (start !== null && end !== null) {
				node.setSelectionRange(start, end);
			}
			node.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
		}
	}

	node.addEventListener('input', handleInput);

	return {
		destroy() {
			node.removeEventListener('input', handleInput);
		}
	};
}
