<script lang="ts">
	import { enhance } from '$app/forms';
	import { goto } from '$app/navigation';
	import { clientAuth } from '$lib/state/client-auth.svelte';
	import { Eye, EyeOff, KeyRound, Lock, ShieldCheck, WifiOff } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let password = $state('');
	let confirmPassword = $state('');
	let showPassword = $state(false);
	let isSubmitting = $state(false);
	let localError = $state<string | null>(null);

	let displayError = $derived(localError || form?.error || null);

	async function handleLoginSubmit(e: SubmitEvent) {
		if (!navigator.onLine) {
			e.preventDefault();
			if (!password.trim() || isSubmitting) return;
			isSubmitting = true;
			localError = null;

			try {
				const res = await clientAuth.loginOffline(password);
				if (res.success) {
					await goto(data.redirectTo || '/');
				} else {
					localError = res.error || 'رمز عبور نادرست است';
				}
			} finally {
				isSubmitting = false;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

<div class="flex min-h-[78dvh] items-center justify-center py-8">
	<div
		class="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-modal) sm:p-8"
	>
		<div class="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
			<div
				class="mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs"
			>
				{#if data.isPasswordConfigured}
					<Lock class="h-7 w-7" />

					<ShieldCheck class="h-7 w-7" />
				{/if}
			</div>

					{/if}
				</h1>
				{#if typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine}

					</span>
				{/if}
			</div>

				{#if data.isPasswordConfigured}
					با وارد کردن رمز عبور، نشست شما به صورت امن تا یک سال روی این دستگاه فعال می‌ماند.
				{:else}

					localError = null;

					return async ({ result, update }) => {
						isSubmitting = false;
						if (result.type === 'redirect' || result.type === 'success') {
							await clientAuth.saveClientCredential(passToSave);
						}
						await update();
					};
				}}
				class="space-y-4"
			>
				<div>
					<label for="login-pass" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">
						رمز عبور
					</label>
					<div class="relative">
						<input
							id="login-pass"
							name="password"
							type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
							required
							dir="ltr"
							bind:value={password}
							placeholder="••••••••"
							class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-4 pl-11 text-center font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:bg-surface focus:outline-none"
						/>
						<button
							type="button"
							onclick={() => (showPassword = !showPassword)}
							class="absolute top-1/2 left-3 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-fg-muted transition hover:text-fg"
							aria-label="نمایش یا مخفی‌سازی رمز"
						>
							{#if showPassword}
								<EyeOff class="h-4 w-4" />
							{:else}
								<Eye class="h-4 w-4" />
							{/if}

				>
					<KeyRound class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>{isSubmitting ? 'در حال بررسی...' : 'ورود و ذخیره نشست'}</span>
				</button>
			</form>
		{:else}
			<form
				method="POST"
				action="?/setupPassword&redirectTo={encodeURIComponent(data.redirectTo)}"
				use:enhance={() => {
					isSubmitting = true;
					localError = null;
					const passToSave = password;
					return async ({ result, update }) => {
						isSubmitting = false;
						if (result.type === 'redirect' || result.type === 'success') {
							await clientAuth.saveClientCredential(passToSave);
						}
						await update();
					};
				}}
				class="space-y-4"
			>
				<div>
					<label for="setup-pass" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">
						رمز عبور جدید
					</label>
					<div class="relative">
						<input
							id="setup-pass"
							name="password"
							type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
							required
							minlength="4"
							dir="ltr"
							bind:value={password}
							placeholder="حداقل ۴ کاراکتر"
							class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-4 pl-11 text-center font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:bg-surface focus:outline-none"
						/>
						<button

							class="absolute top-1/2 left-3 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-fg-muted transition hover:text-fg"
							aria-label="نمایش یا مخفی‌سازی رمز"

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/login/+page.server.ts
================================================================

import { fail, redirect } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { dev } from '$app/env';
import {
	ChangePasswordInputSchema,
	LoginInputSchema,
	SetupPasswordInputSchema
} from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
	changeMasterPassword,
	createAuthSession,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	isPasswordConfigured,
	revokeAuthSession,
	setupInitialPassword,
	verifyPassword
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/auth.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

const LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
const LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
const MAX_RATE_LIMIT_TRACKED_IPS = 500;

interface RateLimitRecord {
	count: number;
	firstAttemptMs: number;
}

const failedLoginAttemptsByIp = new Map<string, RateLimitRecord>();

function pruneExpiredRateLimits(now: number): void {
	for (const [ip, record] of failedLoginAttemptsByIp.entries()) {
		if (now - record.firstAttemptMs > LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS) {
			failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(ip);
		}
	}
	if (failedLoginAttemptsByIp.size > MAX_RATE_LIMIT_TRACKED_IPS) {
		const excessCount = failedLoginAttemptsByIp.size - MAX_RATE_LIMIT_TRACKED_IPS;
		const oldestIps = Array.from(failedLoginAttemptsByIp.keys()).slice(0, excessCount);
		for (const ip of oldestIps) {
			failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(ip);
		}
	}
}

function checkLoginRateLimit(ip: string): { allowed: boolean; retryAfterMinutes: number } {
	const now = Date.now();
	pruneExpiredRateLimits(now);

	const record = failedLoginAttemptsByIp.get(ip);
	if (!record) return { allowed: true, retryAfterMinutes: 0 };

	if (now - record.firstAttemptMs > LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS) {
		failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(ip);
		return { allowed: true, retryAfterMinutes: 0 };
	}

	if (record.count >= LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
		const remainingMs = LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS - (now - record.firstAttemptMs);
		return {
			allowed: false,
			retryAfterMinutes: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(remainingMs / 60000))
		};
	}

	return { allowed: true, retryAfterMinutes: 0 };
}

function recordFailedLoginAttempt(ip: string): void {
	const now = Date.now();
	pruneExpiredRateLimits(now);

	const record = failedLoginAttemptsByIp.get(ip);
	if (!record || now - record.firstAttemptMs > LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS) {
		failedLoginAttemptsByIp.set(ip, { count: 1, firstAttemptMs: now });
	} else {
		record.count += 1;
	}
}

function clearFailedLoginAttempts(ip: string): void {
	failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(ip);
}

function resolveClientIp(request: Request, getClientAddress: () => string): string {
	const cfIp = request.headers.get('cf-connecting-ip')?.trim();
	if (cfIp) return cfIp;

	const realIp = request.headers.get('x-real-ip')?.trim();
	if (realIp) return realIp;

	const forwardedFor = request.headers.get('x-forwarded-for');
	if (forwardedFor) {
		const firstIp = forwardedFor.split(',')[0]?.trim();
		if (firstIp) return firstIp;
	}

	try {
		return getClientAddress();
	} catch {
		return 'unknown';
	}
}

function resolveSafeRedirect(url: URL): string {
	const target = url.searchParams.get('redirectTo') ?? '/';
	if (target.startsWith('/') && !target.startsWith('//') && !target.startsWith('/login')) {
		return target;
	}
	return '/';
}

export const load: PageServerLoad = async ({ locals, url }) => {
	return {
		isPasswordConfigured: locals.isPasswordConfigured,
		redirectTo: resolveSafeRedirect(url)
	};
};

function isHttpsRequest(request: Request, url: URL): boolean {
	if (dev) return false;
	return (
		url.protocol === 'https:' ||
		request.headers.get('x-forwarded-proto') === 'https'
	);
}

export const actions: Actions = {
	setupPassword: async ({ request, cookies, url }) => {
		if (isPasswordConfigured()) {
			return fail(400, { error: 'رمز عبور قبلاً تنظیم شده است' });
		}

		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = SetupPasswordInputSchema.safeParse({
			password: String(fd.get('password') ?? ''),
			confirmPassword: String(fd.get('confirmPassword') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'ورودی نامعتبر است'
			});
		}

		await setupInitialPassword(parsed.data.password);

		const session = createAuthSession();

		cookies.set(
			SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
			session.token,
			getSessionCookieOptions(isHttpsRequest(request, url))
		);

		redirect(303, resolveSafeRedirect(url));
	},

	login: async ({ request, cookies, url, getClientAddress }) => {
		const clientIp = resolveClientIp(request, getClientAddress);

		const rateStatus = checkLoginRateLimit(clientIp);
		if (!rateStatus.allowed) {
			return fail(429, {
				error: `تلاش‌های ناموفق بیش از حد مجاز است. لطفاً ${rateStatus.retryAfterMinutes} دقیقه دیگر امتحان کنید.`
			});
		}

		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = LoginInputSchema.safeParse({
			password: String(fd.get('password') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'رمز عبور الزامی است' });
		}

		const isValid = await verifyPassword(parsed.data.password);
		if (!isValid) {
			recordFailedLoginAttempt(clientIp);
			return fail(401, { error: 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است' });
		}

		clearFailedLoginAttempts(clientIp);

		const session = createAuthSession();

		cookies.set(
			SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
			session.token,
			getSessionCookieOptions(isHttpsRequest(request, url))
		);

		redirect(303, resolveSafeRedirect(url));
	},

	changePassword: async ({ request, locals }) => {
		if (!locals.isAuthenticated) {
			return fail(401, { error: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شوید' });
		}

		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = ChangePasswordInputSchema.safeParse({
			currentPassword: String(fd.get('currentPassword') ?? ''),
			newPassword: String(fd.get('newPassword') ?? ''),
			confirmNewPassword: String(fd.get('confirmNewPassword') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				passwordError: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در تغییر رمز عبور'
			});
		}

		const ok = await changeMasterPassword(
			parsed.data.currentPassword,
			parsed.data.newPassword
		);

		if (!ok) {
			return fail(400, { passwordError: 'رمز عبور فعلی نادرست است' });
		}

		return { passwordChanged: true };
	},

	logout: async ({ cookies, locals }) => {
		if (locals.sessionToken) {
			revokeAuthSession(locals.sessionToken);
		}
		cookies.delete(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, { path: '/' });
		redirect(303, '/login');
	}
};

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/+layout.ts
================================================================

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { redirect } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { clientAuth } from '$lib/state/client-auth.svelte';
import type { LayoutLoad } from './$types';

export const ssr = false;
export const prerender = false;

export const load: LayoutLoad = async ({ data, url }) => {
	if (!browser) {
		return {
			isAuthenticated: data?.isAuthenticated ?? false,
			isPasswordConfigured: data?.isPasswordConfigured ?? true
		};
	}

	const localAuth = await clientAuth.checkAuth(
		data?.isAuthenticated,
		data?.isPasswordConfigured
	);

	const isLoginPage = url.pathname.startsWith('/login');

	if (!localAuth.isAuthenticated && !isLoginPage) {
		const redirectTarget = url.pathname + url.search;
		const query =
			redirectTarget && redirectTarget !== '/'
				? `?redirectTo=${encodeURIComponent(redirectTarget)}`
				: '';
		redirect(303, `/login${query}`);
	}

	if (localAuth.isAuthenticated && isLoginPage) {
		redirect(303, '/');
	}

	return {
		isAuthenticated: localAuth.isAuthenticated,
		isPasswordConfigured: localAuth.isPasswordConfigured
	};
};

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/+page.svelte

<script lang="ts">
	import { page } from '$app/state';
	import { buildHabitTree } from '@core/habit-engine';
	import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import type { HabitTimeSlot, HabitTreeNode } from '@domain/schemas';
	import { DailyTrackerController } from '$lib/state/daily-tracker.svelte';
	import JalaliDateScrubber from '$lib/components/tracker/JalaliDateScrubber.svelte';
	import UpcomingEventsBanner from '$lib/components/tracker/UpcomingEventsBanner.svelte';
	import TrackerActionToolbar from '$lib/components/tracker/TrackerActionToolbar.svelte';
	import TieredHabitCard from '$lib/components/tracker/TieredHabitCard.svelte';
	import CadenceHabitCard from '$lib/components/tracker/CadenceHabitCard.svelte';
	import DailyJournalSection from '$lib/components/tracker/DailyJournalSection.svelte';
	import { CheckCircle2, Moon, Sun, Sunrise } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	const tracker = new DailyTrackerController(() => data);

	$effect(() => {
		const urlDate = page.url.searchParams.get('date');
		const targetIso =
			urlDate && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(urlDate) ? urlDate : data.dateIso;

		tracker.hydrate(data);
		if (targetIso !== data.dateIso) {
			void tracker.switchDateOfflineIfNeeded(targetIso, data.dateIso);
		}

		return () => {
			tracker.destroy();
		};
	});

	let activeSelectedDayEvents = $derived(
		tracker.dateIso === data.dateIso
			? (data.selectedDayEvents ?? [])
			: (data.upcomingBannerItems ?? []).filter(
					(u) => u.occurrenceDateIso === tracker.dateIso
				)
	);

	let tree = $derived(
		buildHabitTree(tracker.habits, {
			dateIso: tracker.dateIso,
			hasExistingEntry: tracker.hasExistingEntry,
			loggedHabitIds: tracker.loggedHabitIds
		})
	);

	const timeSlotOrder: ReadonlyArray<{
		slot: HabitTimeSlot;
		icon: typeof Sunrise;
	}> = [
		{ slot: 'morning', icon: Sunrise },
		{ slot: 'day', icon: Sun },
		{ slot: 'night', icon: Moon }
	];

	let groupedTieredSections = $derived.by(() => {
		const groups: Array<{
			slot: HabitTimeSlot;
			icon: typeof Sunrise;
			nodes: HabitTreeNode[];
			completedCount: number;
		}> = [];

		for (const meta of timeSlotOrder) {
			const nodes = tree.tieredRoots.filter((n) => n.timeSlot === meta.slot);
			if (nodes.length > 0) {
				const completedCount = nodes.filter((n) => tracker.isNodeCompleted(n.id)).length;
				groups.push({
					slot: meta.slot,
					icon: meta.icon,
					nodes,
					completedCount
				});
			}
		}
		return groups;
	});
</script>

<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
	<JalaliDateScrubber
		currentDateIso={tracker.dateIso}
		recentEntries={tracker.recentEntries}
		liveDayStatus={tracker.computedDayStatus}
		isLiveDirty={tracker.isDirty}
		hasExistingEntry={tracker.hasExistingEntry}
		saveState={tracker.saveState}
		onRetrySave={() => tracker.flushPendingSave()}
		completionStats={tracker.completionStats}
	/>

	<UpcomingEventsBanner
		upcomingItems={data.upcomingBannerItems ?? []}
		selectedDayEvents={activeSelectedDayEvents}
		currentDateIso={tracker.dateIso}
	/>

	{#if tracker.habits.length === 0}
		<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center">
			<h2 class="text-xl font-black text-fg">دیتابیس خالی است</h2>
			<p class="mt-2 text-sm text-fg-secondary">
				برای بارگذاری طرح‌های اولیه، دستور زیر را در ترمینال اجرا کنید:
			</p>
			<pre
				dir="ltr"
				class="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-elevated p-4 font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fg">pnpm db:seed</pre>
		</div>
	{:else}
		<TrackerActionToolbar
			{tracker}
			cadenceHabits={tree.cadenceHabits}
		/>

		{#if form?.planSaved}
			<div
				class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft px-5 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-optimal"
			>
				<CheckCircle2 class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>
					برنامه فردا ({toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(form.targetDateIso))}) ثبت شد.
				</span>
			</div>
		{/if}

		{#if tree.cadenceHabits.length > 0}
			<div class="space-y-4">
				{#each tree.cadenceHabits as habit (habit.id)}
					<CadenceHabitCard
						{habit}
						log={tracker.logsByHabitId[habit.id]}
						onUpdateActual={(id, intervals, mins) =>
							tracker.updateCadenceActual(id, intervals, mins)}
					/>
				{/each}
			</div>
		{/if}

		<div class="space-y-5">
			{#each groupedTieredSections as section (section.slot)}
				{@const SlotIcon = section.icon}
				<div class="space-y-2.5">
					{#if groupedTieredSections.length > 1}
						<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2">
							<div
								class="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary"
							>
								<SlotIcon class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
							</div>
							<div class="h-px flex-1 bg-border"></div>
							<span class="font-mono text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-muted">
								{toPersianDigits(section.completedCount)}/{toPersianDigits(
									section.nodes.length
								)}
							</span>
						</div>
					{/if}

					<div class="space-y-2.5">
						{#each section.nodes as node (node.id)}
							<TieredHabitCard
								{node}
								logsByHabitId={tracker.logsByHabitId}
								compositeNotation={node.children.length > 0
									? tracker.getCompositeNotation(node.id)
									: undefined}
								onSelectTier={(id, tier) => tracker.setTierScore(id, tier)}
								onSelectGroupTier={(parentId, tier) =>
									tracker.setParentGroupAllTier(parentId, tier)}
							/>
						{/each}
					</div>
				</div>
			{/each}
		</div>

		<DailyJournalSection
			bind:journalText={tracker.journalText}
			onTextChange={() => tracker.handleTextChanged()}
		/>
	{/if}
</div>

PATH: src/routes/+page.ts

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { getYesterdayIso, shiftIsoDate } from '@domain/calendar';
import { DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS } from '@domain/constants';
import type { DayCommandPayload } from '@domain/schemas';
import { buildOfflineDayCommandPayload } from '$lib/state/idb-sync-store';
import type { PageLoad } from './$types';

function createFallbackPayload(dateIso: string): DayCommandPayload {
	return {
		dateIso,
		tomorrowIso: shiftIsoDate(dateIso, 1),
		habits: [],
		availableTags: [...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS],
		entry: null,
		logs: [],
		dayCadencePlans: [],
		tomorrowCadencePlans: [],
		recentEntries: [],
		upcomingBannerItems: [],
		selectedDayEvents: []
	};
}

export const load: PageLoad = async ({ url }) => {
	const requestedDate = url.searchParams.get('date');
	const targetDateIso =
		requestedDate && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(requestedDate)
			? requestedDate
			: getYesterdayIso();

	if (!browser) {
		return createFallbackPayload(targetDateIso);
	}

	const offlinePayload = await buildOfflineDayCommandPayload(targetDateIso);
	if (offlinePayload) {
		return offlinePayload;
	}

	return createFallbackPayload(targetDateIso);
};

<script lang="ts">
	import { buildFullAnalyticsReport } from '@core/analytics-engine';
	import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import type { AnalyticsTimeRange } from '@domain/schemas';
	import CustomSelect from '$lib/components/ui/CustomSelect.svelte';
	import AnalyticsSummaryCards from '$lib/components/analytics/AnalyticsSummaryCards.svelte';
	import HabitTrendChart from '$lib/components/analytics/HabitTrendChart.svelte';
	import HabitHeatmapMatrix from '$lib/components/analytics/HabitHeatmapMatrix.svelte';
	import HabitEvolutionTimeline from '$lib/components/analytics/HabitEvolutionTimeline.svelte';
	import HabitsComparisonTable from '$lib/components/analytics/HabitsComparisonTable.svelte';
	import { BarChart3 } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data }: PageProps = $props();

	let rangeOverride = $state<AnalyticsTimeRange | null>(null);
	let habitIdOverride = $state<string | null>(null);

	let selectedRange = $derived<AnalyticsTimeRange>(rangeOverride ?? data.initialRange);

	let selectedHabitId = $derived.by<string>(() => {
		if (habitIdOverride && data.habits.some((h) => h.id === habitIdOverride)) {
			return habitIdOverride;
		}
		if (data.initialHabitId && data.habits.some((h) => h.id === data.initialHabitId)) {
			return data.initialHabitId;
		}
		const firstActive = data.habits.find((h) => h.status !== 'archived') ?? data.habits[0];
		return firstActive?.id ?? '';
	});

	const rangeOptions: ReadonlyArray<{ value: AnalyticsTimeRange; label: string }> = [
		{ value: '7d', label: '۷ روز اخیر' },
		{ value: '30d', label: '۳۰ روز اخیر' },
		{ value: '90d', label: '۹۰ روز اخیر' },
		{ value: 'all', label: 'کل زمان' }
	];

	let report = $derived(
		buildFullAnalyticsReport({
			habits: data.habits,
			entries: data.entries,
			logs: data.logs,
			lifecycleEvents: data.lifecycleEvents,
			selectedHabitId,
			range: selectedRange,
			fallbackEndDateIso: data.fallbackEndDateIso
		})
	);

	let habitSelectOptions = $derived(
		report.selectableHabits.map((item) => ({
			value: item.habit.id,
			label: item.displayLabel
		}))
	);

	function selectHabitAndScroll(habitId: string) {
		habitIdOverride = habitId;
		window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
	}
</script>

<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
				<div
					class="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
				>
					<BarChart3 class="h-6 w-6" />
				</div>
				<div>
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
						<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">
							آمار و مهندسی روند طرح‌ها
						</h1>
						<span
							dir="ltr"
							class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary"
						>
							{toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(report.startDateIso))} → {toPersianDigits(
								isoToJalaliSlash(report.endDateIso)
							)}
						</span>
					</div>
					<p class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						تحلیل عمیق استمرار، کیفیت سطوح و تاثیر تغییرات قوانین هر طرح در طول زمان
					</p>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
				<div
					class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1"
				>
					{#each rangeOptions as opt (opt.value)}
						<button
							type="button"
							onclick={() => (rangeOverride = opt.value)}
							class="rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition {selectedRange ===
							opt.value
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
						>
							{opt.label}
						</button>
					{/each}
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		{#if habitSelectOptions.length > 0}
			<div
				class="mt-5 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 border-t border-border pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
			>
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
					<span class="text-xs font-black text-fg">انتخاب طرح برای کاوش عمیق:</span>
				</div>

				<div class="w-full sm:w-80">
					<CustomSelect
						value={selectedHabitId}
						options={habitSelectOptions}
						onSelect={(val) => (habitIdOverride = String(val))}
						triggerClass="h-11 text-fg"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>
		{/if}
	</section>

	{#if data.habits.length === 0}
		<div
			class="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز طرحی در سیستم تعریف نشده است</h2>
			<a
				href="/architect"
				class="mt-4 inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg"
			>
				تعریف اولین طرح
			</a>
		</div>
	{:else if report.selectedHabitReport}
		{@const activeReport = report.selectedHabitReport}

		<AnalyticsSummaryCards report={activeReport} />

		<HabitTrendChart report={activeReport} />

		<HabitHeatmapMatrix report={activeReport} />

		<HabitEvolutionTimeline
			habitTitle={activeReport.habit.title}
			evolutionTimeline={activeReport.evolutionTimeline}
		/>

		<HabitsComparisonTable
			rows={report.comparisonRows}
			{selectedHabitId}
			onSelectHabit={selectHabitAndScroll}
		/>
	{/if}
</div>

PATH: src/routes/analytics/+page.ts

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { getYesterdayIso } from '@domain/calendar';
import { AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema, type AnalyticsTimeRange } from '@domain/schemas';
import { buildOfflineAnalyticsDatasetPayload } from '$lib/state/idb-sync-store';
import type { PageLoad } from './$types';

export const load: PageLoad = async ({ url }) => {
	const rawHabitParam = url.searchParams.get('habit');
	const rawRangeParam = url.searchParams.get('range');
	const parsedRange = AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema.safeParse(rawRangeParam);
	const initialRange: AnalyticsTimeRange = parsedRange.success ? parsedRange.data : '30d';
	const initialHabitId = rawHabitParam || null;

	const fallback = {
		habits: [],
		entries: [],
		logs: [],
		lifecycleEvents: [],
		fallbackEndDateIso: getYesterdayIso(),
		initialHabitId,
		initialRange
	};

	if (!browser) return fallback;

	const offlineDataset = await buildOfflineAnalyticsDatasetPayload(
		initialHabitId,
		initialRange
	);

	if (offlineDataset) {
		return offlineDataset;
	}

	return fallback;
};

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/+page.server.ts

import { fail } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { getYesterdayIso, shiftIsoDate } from '@domain/calendar';
import { SaveCadencePlanBatchSchema } from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	getDayCommandPayload,
	saveCadencePlansForDate
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/daily-tracker.repo';
import { toggleCalendarEventCompletion } from '$lib/server/db/repositories/calendar.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	saveTomorrowCadence: async ({ request }) => {
		const formData = await request.formData();
		const rawPayload = formData.get('planPayload');
		if (typeof rawPayload !== 'string') {
			return fail(400, { error: 'داده برنامه فردا نامعتبر است' });
		}

		let parsedJson: unknown;
		try {
			parsedJson = JSON.parse(rawPayload);
		} catch {
			return fail(400, { error: 'فرمت داده برنامه فردا نامعتبر است' });
		}

		const validated = SaveCadencePlanBatchSchema.safeParse(parsedJson);
		if (!validated.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: 'ساختار برنامه بازه‌های فردا معتبر نیست' });
		}

		saveCadencePlansForDate(validated.data);
		return { planSaved: true, targetDateIso: validated.data.targetDateIso };
	},

	toggleEventComplete: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const eventId = String(fd.get('eventId') ?? '');
		const isCompleted = fd.get('isCompleted') === 'true';
		if (!eventId) return fail(400, { error: 'شناسه رویداد نامعتبر است' });
		toggleCalendarEventCompletion(eventId, isCompleted);
		return { eventToggled: true };
	}
};

<script lang="ts">
	import { formatJalaliHuman, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { getDayStatusMeta } from '@domain/constants';
	import { ArrowDownWideNarrow, ArrowUpNarrowWide, BookHeart, Calendar, Search } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data }: PageProps = $props();

	let sortOrder = $state<'desc' | 'asc'>('desc');
	let searchQuery = $state('');

	let displayedMemories = $derived.by(() => {
		const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
		const filtered = q
			? data.memories.filter((m) => m.journalText.toLowerCase().includes(q))
			: data.memories;

		return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {
			if (a.dateIso === b.dateIso) return 0;
			if (sortOrder === 'desc') return b.dateIso > a.dateIso ? 1 : -1;
			return a.dateIso > b.dateIso ? 1 : -1;
		});
	});
</script>

<div class="space-y-8">
	<div
		class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-7"
	>
		<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
			<div
				class="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
			>
				<BookHeart class="h-6 w-6" />
			</div>
			<div>
				<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">دفتر خاطرات</h1>
				<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					{toPersianDigits(data.memories.length)} خاطره ثبت‌شده
				</span>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="flex w-full flex-col gap-2.5 sm:w-auto sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:gap-3">
			{#if data.memories.length > 1}
				<div class="relative w-full sm:w-auto">
					<Search
						class="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 right-3.5 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-fg-muted"
					/>
					<input
						type="search"
						bind:value={searchQuery}
						placeholder="جستجو در خاطرات..."
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-9 pl-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none sm:w-56"
					/>
				</div>
			{/if}

			<div class="flex w-full items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1 sm:w-auto">
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => (sortOrder = 'desc')}
					class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3.5 {sortOrder ===
					'desc'
						? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
						: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
				>
					<ArrowDownWideNarrow class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
					<span>جدید به قدیم</span>
				</button>
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => (sortOrder = 'asc')}
					class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3.5 {sortOrder ===
					'asc'
						? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
						: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
				>
					<ArrowUpNarrowWide class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
					<span>قدیم به جدید</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>

	{#if data.memories.length === 0}
		<div
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div
				class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted"
			>
				<BookHeart class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز خاطره‌ای ثبت نشده است</h2>
			<p class="mt-1.5 text-sm text-fg-muted">
				در صفحهٔ «ثبت روزانه» می‌توانید پایین لیست طرح‌ها خاطره هر روز را بنویسید.
			</p>
			<a
				href="/"
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90"
			>
				ثبت اولین خاطره
			</a>
		</div>
	{:else if displayedMemories.length === 0}
		<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center text-sm text-fg-muted">
			موردی مطابق با عبارت جستجو شده یافت نشد.
		</div>
	{:else}
		<div class="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5">
			{#each displayedMemories as item (item.id)}
				{@const human = formatJalaliHuman(item.dateIso)}
				<article
					class="group rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:border-border-strong sm:p-8"
				>
					<header class="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-4">
						<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
							<span
								class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full {getDayStatusMeta(item.dayStatus).dotClass}"
								title="وضعیت روز"
							></span>
							<h2 class="text-lg font-black text-fg">{human.fullLabel}</h2>
						</div>

						<a
							href="/?date={item.dateIso}"
							class="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg"
							title="مشاهده یا ویرایش این روز"
						>
							<Calendar class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-fg-muted" />
							<span dir="ltr">{toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(item.dateIso))}</span>
						</a>
					</header>

					<p
						class="whitespace-pre-line text-base leading-8 font-normal text-fg select-text"
					>
						{item.journalText}
					</p>

					{#if item.tags.length > 0}
						<footer class="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-2">
							{#each item.tags as tag (tag)}
								<span
									class="rounded-xl bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
								>
									#{tag}
								</span>
							{/each}
						</footer>
					{/if}
				</article>
			{/each}
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { buildOfflineJournalMemories } from '$lib/state/idb-sync-store';
import type { PageLoad } from './$types';

export const load: PageLoad = async () => {
	if (!browser) return { memories: [] };

	const memories = await buildOfflineJournalMemories();
	return {
		memories: memories ?? []
	};
};

<script lang="ts">
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import {
		detectNoteSmartSignals,
		type NoteEntity,
		type NoteSmartFilter
	} from '@domain/schemas';
	import NoteItemCard from '$lib/components/notes/NoteItemCard.svelte';
	import NoteUpsertModal from '$lib/components/notes/NoteUpsertModal.svelte';
	import { Link2, NotebookPen, Plus, Search } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let searchQuery = $state('');
	let smartFilter = $state<NoteSmartFilter>('ALL');
	let selectedCategory = $state<string>('ALL');
	let isModalOpen = $state(false);
	let editingNote = $state<NoteEntity | null>(null);

	let categories = $derived.by(() => {
		const set = new Set<string>(['عمومی']);
		for (const n of data.notes) {
			if (n.category.trim()) set.add(n.category.trim());
		}
		return Array.from(set);
	});

	let filteredNotes = $derived.by(() => {
		const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

		return data.notes.filter((note) => {
			if (selectedCategory !== 'ALL' && note.category !== selectedCategory) {
				return false;
			}

			const combinedText = `${note.title} ${note.content}`;
			const signals = detectNoteSmartSignals(combinedText);

			if (smartFilter === 'HAS_LINK' && !signals.hasLink) return false;
			if (smartFilter === 'HAS_NUMBER' && !signals.hasNumber) return false;

			if (q) {
				const haystack = `${note.title} ${note.content} ${note.category}`.toLowerCase();
				if (!haystack.includes(q)) return false;
			}

			return true;
		});
	});

	function openCreateModal() {
		editingNote = null;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function openEditModal(note: NoteEntity) {
		editingNote = note;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function closeModal() {
		isModalOpen = false;
		editingNote = null;
	}
</script>

<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
				<div
					class="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
				>
					<NotebookPen class="h-6 w-6" />
				</div>
				<div>
					<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">یادداشت‌ها</h1>
					<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{toPersianDigits(data.notes.length)} یادداشت، لینک و شماره ذخیره‌شده
					</span>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2.5 sm:w-auto">
				{#if data.notes.length > 0}
					<div class="relative flex-1 sm:w-60 sm:flex-initial">
						<Search
							class="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 right-3.5 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-fg-muted"
						/>
						<input
							type="search"
							bind:value={searchQuery}
							placeholder="جستجو در یادداشت‌ها..."
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-9 pl-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
						/>
					</div>
				{/if}

				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={openCreateModal}
					class="flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:text-sm"
				>
					<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>یادداشت جدید</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		{#if data.notes.length > 0}
			<div
				class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-border pt-4"
			>
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() => (smartFilter = 'ALL')}
						class="rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition {smartFilter ===
						'ALL'
							? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
							: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
					>
						همه
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						onclick={() =>
							(smartFilter = smartFilter === 'HAS_LINK' ? 'ALL' : 'HAS_LINK')}
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition {smartFilter ===
						'HAS_LINK'
							? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
							: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
					>
						<Link2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						<span>حاوی لینک</span>
					</button>
				</div>

				{#if categories.length > 1}
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
						<button
							type="button"
							onclick={() => (selectedCategory = 'ALL')}
							class="rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition {selectedCategory ===
							'ALL'
								? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg'
								: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
						>
							همه دسته‌ها
						</button>
						{#each categories as cat (cat)}
							<button
								type="button"
								onclick={() => (selectedCategory = cat)}
								class="rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition {selectedCategory ===
								cat
									? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg'
									: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'}"
							>
								{cat}
							</button>
						{/each}
					</div>
				{/if}
			</div>
		{/if}
	</section>

	{#if form?.error}
		<div
			class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-failed"
		>
			{form.error}
		</div>
	{/if}

	{#if data.notes.length === 0}
		<div
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div
				class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted"
			>
				<NotebookPen class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز یادداشتی ثبت نشده است</h2>
			<p class="mt-1.5 max-w-md text-xs leading-6 text-fg-muted">
				سایت‌های کاربردی، شماره‌های مهم یا یادداشت‌های روزمره را اینجا ذخیره کنید؛ سیستم به صورت خودکار لینک‌ها و شماره‌های ۱۰ رقمی را تگ‌گذاری می‌کند.
			</p>
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={openCreateModal}
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت اولین یادداشت</span>
			</button>
		</div>
	{:else if filteredNotes.length === 0}
		<div
			class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-fg-muted"
		>
			موردی با این فیلتر یا عبارت جستجو یافت نشد.
		</div>
	{:else}
		<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
			{#each filteredNotes as note (note.id)}
				<NoteItemCard {note} onEdit={openEditModal} />
			{/each}
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

{#if isModalOpen}
	{#key editingNote?.id ?? 'new'}
		<NoteUpsertModal
			{editingNote}
			existingCategories={categories}
			onClose={closeModal}
		/>
	{/key}
{/if}

PATH: src/routes/notes/+page.server.ts

import { fail } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { UpsertNoteInputSchema } from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	createNoteRecord,
	deleteNoteRecord,
	getAllNotes,
	toggleNotePinRecord,
	updateNoteRecord
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/notes.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	createNote: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = UpsertNoteInputSchema.safeParse({
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			content: String(fd.get('content') ?? ''),
			category: String(fd.get('category') ?? 'عمومی')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ثبت یادداشت' });
		}

		createNoteRecord(parsed.data);
		return { created: true };
	},

	updateNote: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const noteId = String(fd.get('noteId') ?? '');
		const parsed = UpsertNoteInputSchema.safeParse({
			noteId,
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			content: String(fd.get('content') ?? ''),
			category: String(fd.get('category') ?? 'عمومی')
		});

		if (!noteId || !parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				error: parsed.error?.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ویرایش یادداشت'
			});
		}

		updateNoteRecord(noteId, parsed.data);
		return { updated: true };
	},

	togglePin: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const noteId = String(fd.get('noteId') ?? '');
		const isPinned = fd.get('isPinned') === 'true';
		if (noteId) toggleNotePinRecord(noteId, isPinned);
		return { pinned: true };
	},

	deleteNote: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const noteId = String(fd.get('noteId') ?? '');
		if (noteId) deleteNoteRecord(noteId);
		return { deleted: true };
	}
};

<script lang="ts">
	import type { HabitEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import ArchitectHabitCard from '$lib/components/architect/ArchitectHabitCard.svelte';
	import ArchivedHabitsSection from '$lib/components/architect/ArchivedHabitsSection.svelte';
	import HabitUpsertModal from '$lib/components/architect/HabitUpsertModal.svelte';
	import { BarChart3, Plus } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let activeModal = $state<'create' | 'edit' | null>(null);
	let editingHabit = $state<HabitEntity | null>(null);
	let createParentId = $state<string>('');

	let rootHabits = $derived(
		data.habits.filter((h) => h.parentId === null && h.status !== 'archived')
	);

	let archivedHabits = $derived(data.habits.filter((h) => h.status === 'archived'));

	function getChildren(parentId: string): HabitEntity[] {
		return data.habits.filter((h) => h.parentId === parentId && h.status !== 'archived');
	}

	function openCreateModal(parentId = '') {
		createParentId = parentId;
		editingHabit = null;
		activeModal = 'create';
	}

	function openEditModal(habit: HabitEntity) {
		editingHabit = habit;
		activeModal = 'edit';
	}

	function closeModal() {
		activeModal = null;
		editingHabit = null;
	}
</script>

<div class="space-y-8">
	<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
		<h1 class="text-2xl font-black text-fg sm:text-3xl">ساختار طرح‌ها</h1>

		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
			<a
				href="/timeline"
				class="flex h-11 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg sm:h-12"
			>
				<span>تاریخچه وقایع</span>
			</a>

			<a
				href="/analytics"
				class="flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-4.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:h-12 sm:text-sm"
			>
				<BarChart3 class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
				<span>مشاهده آمار و روندها</span>
			</a>

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => openCreateModal('')}
				class="flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:h-12 sm:text-sm"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
				<span>افزودن طرح جدید</span>
			</button>
		</div>
	</div>

	{#if form?.error}
		<div
			class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-failed"
		>
			{form.error}
		</div>
	{/if}

	<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
		{#each rootHabits as habit (habit.id)}
			<ArchitectHabitCard
				{habit}
				children={getChildren(habit.id)}
				onCreateSubHabit={(parentId) => openCreateModal(parentId)}
				onEditHabit={(h) => openEditModal(h)}
			/>
		{/each}
	</div>

	<ArchivedHabitsSection {archivedHabits} onEditHabit={(h) => openEditModal(h)} />
</div>

{#if activeModal}
	{#key `${activeModal}:${editingHabit?.id ?? createParentId}`}
		<HabitUpsertModal
			mode={activeModal}
			{editingHabit}
			hasSubHabits={editingHabit ? getChildren(editingHabit.id).length > 0 : false}
			initialParentId={createParentId}
			{rootHabits}
			onClose={closeModal}
		/>
	{/key}
{/if}

import { fail } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { CADENCE_DEFAULTS, DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY } from '@domain/constants';
import { CreateHabitInputSchema, MutateHabitStructureSchema } from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	createNewHabit,
	getAllHabits,
	updateHabitStructure
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/habits.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	createHabit: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parentIdRaw = String(fd.get('parentId') ?? '').trim();

		const rawIntervals = fd.get('defaultIntervals');
		const rawMinutes = fd.get('defaultMinutesPerInterval');
		const rawWindow = fd.get('cadenceWindowMinutes');

		const parsed = CreateHabitInputSchema.safeParse({
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			parentId: parentIdRaw ? parentIdRaw : null,
			category: String(fd.get('category') ?? DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY),
			trackingMode: String(fd.get('trackingMode') ?? 'tiered'),
			timeSlot: String(fd.get('timeSlot') ?? 'day'),
			isSituational: fd.get('isSituational') === 'on' || fd.get('isSituational') === 'true',
			scheduleLabel: String(fd.get('scheduleLabel') ?? ''),
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? ''),
			bronzeDesc: String(fd.get('bronzeDesc') ?? ''),
			silverDesc: String(fd.get('silverDesc') ?? ''),
			goldDesc: String(fd.get('goldDesc') ?? ''),
			emergencyMinDesc: String(fd.get('emergencyMinDesc') ?? ''),
			defaultIntervals: rawIntervals !== null ? Number(rawIntervals) : CADENCE_DEFAULTS.INTERVALS,
			defaultMinutesPerInterval:
				rawMinutes !== null ? Number(rawMinutes) : CADENCE_DEFAULTS.MINUTES_PER_INTERVAL,
			cadenceWindowMinutes:
				rawWindow !== null ? Number(rawWindow) : CADENCE_DEFAULTS.WINDOW_MINUTES,
			reason: String(fd.get('reason') ?? 'تعریف طرح جدید')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ورودی‌ها' });
		}

		createNewHabit(parsed.data);
		return { created: true };
	},

	updateHabit: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parentIdRaw = String(fd.get('parentId') ?? '').trim();

		const parsed = MutateHabitStructureSchema.safeParse({
			habitId: String(fd.get('habitId') ?? ''),
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			category: String(fd.get('category') ?? DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY),
			parentId: parentIdRaw ? parentIdRaw : null,
			timeSlot: String(fd.get('timeSlot') ?? 'day'),
			isSituational: fd.get('isSituational') === 'on' || fd.get('isSituational') === 'true',
			status: String(fd.get('status') ?? 'active'),
			scheduleLabel: String(fd.get('scheduleLabel') ?? '') || null,
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? '') || null,
			bronzeDesc: String(fd.get('bronzeDesc') ?? '') || null,
			silverDesc: String(fd.get('silverDesc') ?? '') || null,
			goldDesc: String(fd.get('goldDesc') ?? '') || null,
			emergencyMinDesc: String(fd.get('emergencyMinDesc') ?? '') || null,
			reason: String(fd.get('reason') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'لطفاً دلیل تغییر را وارد کنید' });
		}

		updateHabitStructure(parsed.data);
		return { updated: true };
	}
};

<script lang="ts">
	import type {
		MediaCategory,
		MediaEntity,
		MediaSortBy,
		MediaStatusFilter
	} from '@domain/schemas';
	import MediaHeaderStats from '$lib/components/media/MediaHeaderStats.svelte';
	import MediaUpsertModal from '$lib/components/media/MediaUpsertModal.svelte';
	import MediaItemCard from '$lib/components/media/MediaItemCard.svelte';
	import { Clapperboard, Plus } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let categoryFilter = $state<MediaCategory | 'ALL'>('ALL');
	let statusFilter = $state<MediaStatusFilter>('ALL');
	let sortBy = $state<MediaSortBy>('rating_desc');
	let isModalOpen = $state(false);
	let editingMedia = $state<MediaEntity | null>(null);

	let unseenCount = $derived(data.items.filter((i) => i.status !== 'completed').length);

	let existingSuggestions = $derived.by(() => {
		const map = new Map<string, MediaCategory>();
		for (const m of data.items) {
			if (!map.has(m.title)) {
				map.set(m.title, m.category);
			}
		}
		return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([title, category]) => ({ title, category }));
	});

	function openCreateModal() {
		editingMedia = null;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function openEditModal(item: MediaEntity) {
		editingMedia = item;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function closeModal() {
		isModalOpen = false;
		editingMedia = null;
	}

	let filteredItems = $derived.by(() => {
		const list = data.items.filter((item) => {
			if (categoryFilter !== 'ALL' && item.category !== categoryFilter) return false;
			if (statusFilter === 'UNSEEN' && item.status === 'completed') return false;
			if (statusFilter === 'SEEN' && item.status !== 'completed') return false;
			return true;
		});

		return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
			if (sortBy === 'rating_desc') {
				const rDiff = (b.rating ?? 0) - (a.rating ?? 0);
				if (rDiff !== 0) return rDiff;
				return b.createdAtIso > a.createdAtIso ? 1 : b.createdAtIso < a.createdAtIso ? -1 : 0;
			}
			if (sortBy === 'newest') {
				const dateA = a.finishedAtIso || a.createdAtIso;
				const dateB = b.finishedAtIso || b.createdAtIso;
				return dateB > dateA ? 1 : dateB < dateA ? -1 : 0;
			}
			if (sortBy === 'oldest') {
				const dateA = a.finishedAtIso || a.createdAtIso;
				const dateB = b.finishedAtIso || b.createdAtIso;
				return dateA > dateB ? 1 : dateA < dateB ? -1 : 0;
			}
			return a.title.localeCompare(b.title, 'fa');
		});
	});
</script>

<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
	<MediaHeaderStats
		totalCount={data.items.length}
		{unseenCount}
		bind:categoryFilter
		bind:statusFilter
		bind:sortBy
		onOpenCreate={openCreateModal}
	/>

	{#if form?.error}
		<div
			class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-failed"
		>
			{form.error}
		</div>
	{/if}

	{#if data.items.length === 0}
		<div
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div
				class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted"
			>
				<Clapperboard class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h3 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">
				هنوز فیلم، سریال یا انیمه‌ای ثبت نشده است
			</h3>
			<p class="mt-1.5 text-sm text-fg-muted">
				برای شروع، اولین اثر را وارد آرشیو خود کنید.
			</p>
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => (isModalOpen = true)}
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت اولین اثر</span>
			</button>
		</div>
	{:else if filteredItems.length === 0}
		<div
			class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-fg-muted"
		>
			موردی با این فیلتر یافت نشد.
		</div>
	{:else}
		<div class="space-y-2.5">
			{#each filteredItems as item (item.id)}
				<MediaItemCard {item} onEdit={openEditModal} />
			{/each}
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

{#if isModalOpen}
	{#key editingMedia?.id ?? 'new'}
		<MediaUpsertModal
			{editingMedia}
			{existingSuggestions}
			onClose={closeModal}
		/>
	{/key}
{/if}

import { fail } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import {
	CreateMediaInputSchema,
	MediaStatusSchema,
	UpdateMediaInputSchema
} from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	createMediaRecord,
	deleteMediaRecord,
	getAllMedia,
	updateMediaRecord,
	updateMediaStatus
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/media.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	addMedia: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = CreateMediaInputSchema.safeParse({
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			category: String(fd.get('category') ?? 'movie'),
			status: String(fd.get('status') ?? 'completed'),
			startedJalali: String(fd.get('startedJalali') ?? ''),
			finishedJalali: String(fd.get('finishedJalali') ?? ''),
			rating: Number(fd.get('rating') ?? 5),
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ثبت اثر' });
		}

		createMediaRecord(parsed.data);
		return { added: true };
	},

	updateMedia: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = UpdateMediaInputSchema.safeParse({
			mediaId: String(fd.get('mediaId') ?? ''),
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			category: String(fd.get('category') ?? 'movie'),
			status: String(fd.get('status') ?? 'completed'),
			startedJalali: String(fd.get('startedJalali') ?? ''),
			finishedJalali: String(fd.get('finishedJalali') ?? ''),
			rating: Number(fd.get('rating') ?? 5),
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ویرایش اطلاعات اثر'
			});
		}

		updateMediaRecord(parsed.data);
		return { updated: true };
	},

	changeStatus: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const mediaId = String(fd.get('mediaId') ?? '');
		const statusParsed = MediaStatusSchema.safeParse(fd.get('status'));
		if (!mediaId || !statusParsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: 'وضعیت نامعتبر است' });
		}
		updateMediaStatus(mediaId, statusParsed.data);
		return { updated: true };
	},

	deleteMedia: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const mediaId = String(fd.get('mediaId') ?? '');
		if (mediaId) deleteMediaRecord(mediaId);
		return { deleted: true };
	}
};

<script lang="ts">
	import { goto } from '$app/navigation';
	import {
		JALALI_MONTH_NAMES,
		getTodayIso,
		toPersianDigits
	} from '@domain/calendar';
	import CalendarMonthGrid from '$lib/components/calendar/CalendarMonthGrid.svelte';
	import CalendarDayInspector from '$lib/components/calendar/CalendarDayInspector.svelte';
	import CustomSelect from '$lib/components/ui/CustomSelect.svelte';
	import {
		BellRing,
		BookHeart,
		BookOpen,
		Calendar as CalendarIcon,
		ChevronLeft,
		ChevronRight,
		Clapperboard
	} from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let userSelectedIso = $state<string | null>(null);

	let selectedIso = $derived(
		userSelectedIso && data.days.some((d) => d.iso === userSelectedIso)
			? userSelectedIso
			: data.selectedIso
	);

	let selectedDay = $derived(
		data.days.find((d) => d.iso === selectedIso) ?? data.days[0]
	);

	let prevMonthTarget = $derived(
		data.month === 1
			? { jy: data.year - 1, jm: 12 }
			: { jy: data.year, jm: data.month - 1 }
	);

	let nextMonthTarget = $derived(
		data.month === 12
			? { jy: data.year + 1, jm: 1 }
			: { jy: data.year, jm: data.month + 1 }
	);

	const monthOptions = JALALI_MONTH_NAMES.map((name, idx) => ({
		value: idx + 1,
		label: name
	}));

	let yearOptions = $derived.by(() => {
		const baseYear = data.year;
		const list: Array<{ value: number; label: string }> = [];
		for (let y = baseYear - 5; y <= baseYear + 5; y++) {
			list.push({ value: y, label: toPersianDigits(y) });
		}
		return list;
	});

	async function navigateToMonth(jy: number, jm: number) {
		userSelectedIso = null;
		await goto(`/calendar?jy=${jy}&jm=${jm}`);
	}

	async function goToToday() {
		userSelectedIso = null;
		const todayIso = getTodayIso();
		await goto(`/calendar?date=${todayIso}`);
	}

	function selectDayInGrid(iso: string) {
		userSelectedIso = iso;
	}
</script>

<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:justify-start sm:gap-3">
				<div
					class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
				>
					<CalendarIcon class="h-5 w-5" />
				</div>

				<div>
					<h1 class="text-lg font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">
						تقویم و رویدادها
					</h1>
					<p class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						آرشیو عملکرد روزانه، مناسبت‌ها، خاطرات و یادداشت‌ها
					</p>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:justify-end">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
					<a
						href="/calendar?jy={prevMonthTarget.jy}&jm={prevMonthTarget.jm}"
						data-sveltekit-noscroll
						class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95"
						aria-label="ماه قبل"
						title="ماه قبل"
					>
						<ChevronRight class="h-5 w-5" />
					</a>

					<div class="w-32 sm:w-36">
						<CustomSelect
							value={data.month}
							options={monthOptions}
							onSelect={(val) => navigateToMonth(data.year, Number(val))}
							triggerClass="h-11 text-fg"
						/>
					</div>

					<div class="w-24 sm:w-28">
						<CustomSelect
							value={data.year}
							options={yearOptions}
							onSelect={(val) => navigateToMonth(Number(val), data.month)}
							triggerClass="h-11 font-mono text-fg"
						/>
					</div>

					<a
						href="/calendar?jy={nextMonthTarget.jy}&jm={nextMonthTarget.jm}"
						data-sveltekit-noscroll
						class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95"
						aria-label="ماه بعد"
						title="ماه بعد"
					>
						<ChevronLeft class="h-5 w-5" />
					</a>
				</div>

				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={goToToday}
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text transition hover:opacity-90"
				>
					امروز
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>
	</section>

	{#if form?.error}
		<div
			class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-failed"
		>
			{form.error}
		</div>
	{/if}

	<div class="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
		<div class="space-y-6 lg:col-span-7">
			<CalendarMonthGrid
				leadingEmptyCells={data.leadingEmptyCells}
				days={data.days}
				{selectedIso}
				onSelectDay={selectDayInGrid}
			/>

			{#if selectedDay?.entry && (selectedDay.entry.journalText || selectedDay.entry.reflectionNote || selectedDay.entry.tags.length > 0)}
				<section
					class="space-y-3.5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
						<h3 class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
							<BookHeart class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
							<span>خاطره و یادداشت {selectedDay.fullHumanLabel}</span>
						</h3>
					</div>

					{#if selectedDay.entry.journalText}
						<p
							class="whitespace-pre-line rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4 text-sm leading-8 text-fg"
						>
							{selectedDay.entry.journalText}
						</p>
					{/if}

					{#if selectedDay.entry.reflectionNote}
						<p class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-6 text-fg-secondary">
							<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">علت / یادداشت روز: </strong>
							{selectedDay.entry.reflectionNote}
						</p>
					{/if}

					{#if selectedDay.entry.tags.length > 0}
						<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
							{#each selectedDay.entry.tags as tag (tag)}
								<span
									class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
								>
									#{tag}
								</span>
							{/each}
						</div>
					{/if}
				</section>
			{/if}
		</div>

		<div class="lg:col-span-5">
			{#if selectedDay}
				<CalendarDayInspector day={selectedDay} />
			{/if}
		</div>
	</div>
</div>

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/calendar/+page.ts
================================================================

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import {
	assembleJalaliMonthArchive
} from '@core/calendar-engine';
import {
	getTodayIso,
	isoToJalaliParts
} from '@domain/calendar';
import { buildOfflineCalendarMonthPayload } from '$lib/state/idb-sync-store';
import type { PageLoad } from './$types';

export const load: PageLoad = async ({ url }) => {
	const todayIso = getTodayIso();
	const todayParts = isoToJalaliParts(todayIso);

	const requestedDate = url.searchParams.get('date');
	const dateParts =
		requestedDate && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(requestedDate)
			? isoToJalaliParts(requestedDate)
			: null;

	const jyParam = Number(url.searchParams.get('jy'));
	const jmParam = Number(url.searchParams.get('jm'));

	const targetJy =
		!Number.isNaN(jyParam) && jyParam >= 1300 && jyParam <= 1500
			? jyParam
			: (dateParts?.jy ?? todayParts.jy);

	const targetJm =
		!Number.isNaN(jmParam) && jmParam >= 1 && jmParam <= 12
			? jmParam
			: (dateParts?.jm ?? todayParts.jm);

	if (!browser) {
		return assembleJalaliMonthArchive({
			jy: targetJy,
			jm: targetJm,
			todayIso,
			selectedIso: requestedDate ?? todayIso,
			allHabits: [],
			entriesInMonth: [],
			logsInMonth: [],
			eventsInMonth: [],
			booksInMonth: [],
			mediaInMonth: [],
			upcomingEvents: []
		});
	}

	const offlineMonth = await buildOfflineCalendarMonthPayload(
		targetJy,
		targetJm,
		requestedDate ?? undefined
	);

	if (offlineMonth) {
		return offlineMonth;
	}

	return assembleJalaliMonthArchive({
		jy: targetJy,
		jm: targetJm,
		todayIso,
		selectedIso: requestedDate ?? todayIso,
		allHabits: [],
		entriesInMonth: [],
		logsInMonth: [],
		eventsInMonth: [],
		booksInMonth: [],
		mediaInMonth: [],
		upcomingEvents: []
	});
};

PATH: src/routes/calendar/+page.server.ts

import { fail } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliParts } from '@domain/calendar';
import { CreateCalendarEventInputSchema } from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	createCalendarEvent,
	deleteCalendarEvent,
	getJalaliMonthFullArchive,
	toggleCalendarEventCompletion
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/calendar.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	addEvent: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = CreateCalendarEventInputSchema.safeParse({
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			eventType: String(fd.get('eventType') ?? 'occasion'),
			isAnnual: fd.get('isAnnual') === 'on' || fd.get('isAnnual') === 'true',
			dateIso: String(fd.get('dateIso') ?? ''),
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ثبت رویداد' });
		}

		createCalendarEvent(parsed.data);
		return { added: true };
	},

	toggleEvent: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const eventId = String(fd.get('eventId') ?? '');
		const isCompleted = fd.get('isCompleted') === 'true';
		if (!eventId) return fail(400, { error: 'شناسه نامعتبر است' });

		toggleCalendarEventCompletion(eventId, isCompleted);
		return { toggled: true };
	},

	deleteEvent: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const eventId = String(fd.get('eventId') ?? '');
		if (eventId) deleteCalendarEvent(eventId);
		return { deleted: true };
	}
};

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/api/sync/+server.ts
================================================================

import { json } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { SyncRequestPayloadSchema } from '@domain/schemas';
import { processClientSyncRequest } from '$lib/server/db/repositories/sync.repo';
import type { RequestHandler } from './$types';

export const POST: RequestHandler = async ({ request }) => {
	try {
		const rawBody = await request.json();
		const parsed = SyncRequestPayloadSchema.safeParse(rawBody);

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return json(
				{
					ok: false,
					error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'فرمت بسته همگام‌سازی نامعتبر است'
				},
				{ status: 400 }
			);
		}

		const result = processClientSyncRequest(parsed.data);
		return json(result);
	} catch {
		return json(
			{
				ok: false,
				error: 'خطا در پردازش همگام‌سازی با سرور'
			},
			{ status: 500 }
		);
	}
};

import { json } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { version } from '$app/env';
import type { RequestHandler } from './$types';

export const GET: RequestHandler = async () => {
	return json(
		{
			version,
			timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
		},
		{
			headers: {
				'cache-control': 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate',
				'content-type': 'application/json'
			}
		}
	);
};

<script lang="ts">
	import type { BookEntity } from '@domain/schemas';
	import BooksHeaderStats from '$lib/components/books/BooksHeaderStats.svelte';
	import BookUpsertModal from '$lib/components/books/BookUpsertModal.svelte';
	import BookItemCard from '$lib/components/books/BookItemCard.svelte';
	import { BookOpen, Plus } from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let isModalOpen = $state(false);
	let editingBook = $state<BookEntity | null>(null);

	let completedCount = $derived(data.books.filter((b) => b.status === 'completed').length);
	let readingCount = $derived(data.books.filter((b) => b.status === 'reading').length);

	let existingSuggestions = $derived.by(() => {
		const map = new Map<string, string | null>();
		for (const b of data.books) {
			if (!map.has(b.title)) {
				map.set(b.title, b.author);
			}
		}
		return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([title, author]) => ({ title, author }));
	});

	function openCreateModal() {
		editingBook = null;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function openEditModal(book: BookEntity) {
		editingBook = book;
		isModalOpen = true;
	}

	function closeModal() {
		isModalOpen = false;
		editingBook = null;
	}
</script>

<div class="space-y-8 pb-12">
	<BooksHeaderStats
		{completedCount}
		{readingCount}
		onOpenCreate={openCreateModal}
	/>

	{#if form?.error}
		<div
			class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-failed"
		>
			{form.error}
		</div>
	{/if}

	{#if data.books.length === 0}
		<div
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted">
				<BookOpen class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h3 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز کتابی ثبت نشده است</h3>
			<p class="mt-1.5 text-sm text-fg-muted">
				اولین کتاب خود را در کتابخانه شخصی ثبت کنید.
			</p>
			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => (isModalOpen = true)}
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت اولین کتاب</span>
			</button>
		</div>
	{:else}
		<div class="space-y-2.5">
			{#each data.books as book (book.id)}
				<BookItemCard {book} onEdit={openEditModal} />
			{/each}
		</div>
	{/if}
</div>

{#if isModalOpen}
	{#key editingBook?.id ?? 'new'}
		<BookUpsertModal
			{editingBook}
			{existingSuggestions}
			onClose={closeModal}
		/>
	{/key}
{/if}

import { fail } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import {
	BookStatusSchema,
	CreateBookInputSchema,
	UpdateBookInputSchema
} from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	createBookRecord,
	deleteBookRecord,
	getAllBooks,
	updateBookRecord,
	updateBookStatus
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/books.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	addBook: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = CreateBookInputSchema.safeParse({
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			author: String(fd.get('author') ?? ''),
			status: String(fd.get('status') ?? 'completed'),
			startedJalali: String(fd.get('startedJalali') ?? ''),
			finishedJalali: String(fd.get('finishedJalali') ?? ''),
			rating: Number(fd.get('rating') ?? 5),
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ثبت کتاب' });
		}

		createBookRecord(parsed.data);
		return { added: true };
	},

	updateBook: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = UpdateBookInputSchema.safeParse({
			bookId: String(fd.get('bookId') ?? ''),
			title: String(fd.get('title') ?? ''),
			author: String(fd.get('author') ?? ''),
			status: String(fd.get('status') ?? 'completed'),
			startedJalali: String(fd.get('startedJalali') ?? ''),
			finishedJalali: String(fd.get('finishedJalali') ?? ''),
			rating: Number(fd.get('rating') ?? 5),
			notes: String(fd.get('notes') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ویرایش اطلاعات کتاب'
			});
		}

		updateBookRecord(parsed.data);
		return { updated: true };
	},

	changeStatus: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const bookId = String(fd.get('bookId') ?? '');
		const statusParsed = BookStatusSchema.safeParse(fd.get('status'));
		if (!bookId || !statusParsed.success) {
			return fail(400, { error: 'وضعیت نامعتبر است' });
		}
		updateBookStatus(bookId, statusParsed.data);
		return { updated: true };
	},

	deleteBook: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const bookId = String(fd.get('bookId') ?? '');
		if (bookId) deleteBookRecord(bookId);
		return { deleted: true };
	}
};

<script lang="ts">
	import { enhance } from '$app/forms';
	import { goto } from '$app/navigation';
	import { clientAuth } from '$lib/state/client-auth.svelte';
	import { toPersianDigits } from '@domain/calendar';
	import { syncEngine } from '$lib/state/sync-engine.svelte';
	import { themeState } from '$lib/state/theme.svelte';
	import {
		Check,
		Database,
		Download,
		HardDriveDownload,
		HardDriveUpload,
		KeyRound,
		Lock,
		LogOut,
		Moon,
		Palette,
		RefreshCw,
		Settings,
		ShieldCheck,
		Sparkles,
		Sun
	} from 'lucide-svelte';
	import type { PageProps } from './$types';

	let { data, form }: PageProps = $props();

	let selectedFile = $state<File | null>(null);
	let fileSummary = $state<string | null>(null);
	let currentPassword = $state('');
	let newPassword = $state('');
	let confirmNewPassword = $state('');
	let isExporting = $state(false);
	let isImporting = $state(false);
	let isChangingPassword = $state(false);

	$effect(() => {
		if (form?.exportPayload && form?.filename) {
			const blob = new Blob([form.exportPayload], { type: 'application/json' });
			const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
			const a = document.createElement('a');
			a.href = url;
			a.download = form.filename;
			document.body.appendChild(a);
			a.click();
			document.body.removeChild(a);
			URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
		}
	});

	function handleFileSelected(e: Event) {
		const input = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
		const file = input.files?.[0] ?? null;
		selectedFile = file;

		if (file) {
			fileSummary = `نام فایل: ${file.name} (${Math.round(file.size / 1024)} کیلوبایت)`;
		} else {
			fileSummary = null;
		}
	}
</script>

<div class="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-8 pb-16">
	<div
		class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-7"
	>
		<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
			<div
				class="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
			>
				<Settings class="h-6 w-6" />
			</div>
			<div>
				<h1 class="text-xl font-black text-fg sm:text-2xl">تنظیمات و پشتیبان‌گیری</h1>
				<p class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					مدیریت تم، پشتیبان‌گیری کامل از داده‌ها، امنیت و نشست دستگاه
				</p>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>

	{#if form?.importSuccess}
		<div
			class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft px-5 py-3.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-optimal"
		>
			<Check class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
			<span>
				پشتیبان با موفقیت بازگردانی شد! ({toPersianDigits(form.importedCount)} رکورد به روز شدند)
			</span>
		</div>
	{/if}

	{#if form?.importError}
		<div
			class="rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-5 py-3.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-failed"
		>
			{form.importError}
		</div>
	{/if}

	<!-- بخش ظاهر و رابط کاربری -->
	<section
		class="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7"
	>

					? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft text-accent-text ring-1 ring-accent-border'

					</div>

						<div class="text-xs opacity-80">زمینه روشن با تفکیک بصری نرم</div>
					</div>
				</div>
				{#if !themeState.isDark}
					<Check class="h-4 w-4" />
				{/if}
			</button>

			<button
				type="button"
				onclick={() => {
					if (!themeState.isDark) themeState.toggle();
				}}
				class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border p-4 text-right transition {themeState.isDark
					? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft text-accent-text ring-1 ring-accent-border'
					: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg'}"
			>

						<Moon class="h-5 w-5" />
					</div>
					<div>
						<div class="text-sm font-extrabold">پوسته تاریک (Dark)</div>
						<div class="text-xs opacity-80">زمینه مخملی تیره برای تمرکز شبانه</div>
					</div>
				</div>
				{#if themeState.isDark}
					<Check class="h-4 w-4" />
				{/if}
			</button>
		</div>

		<div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4">
			<div class="space-y-0.5">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
					<span class="text-xs font-black text-fg">وضعیت نگارش و دارایی‌های ظاهری</span>
					<span dir="ltr" class="rounded-lg bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						v-{syncEngine.currentAppVersion.slice(0, 8)}
					</span>
				</div>
				<div class="text-[11px] text-fg-muted">
					فونت‌ها و کدهای برنامه بدون مصرف اینترنت از حافظه دستگاه لود می‌شوند
				</div>
			</div>

			{#if syncEngine.hasAppUpdate}
				<button
					type="button"
					onclick={() => syncEngine.applyAppUpdate()}

			>
