					<span>نصب روی دستگاه</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		{/if}
	</section>

	<!-- بخش پشتیبان‌گیری و خروجی داده‌ها -->
	<section
		class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7"
	>
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-3.5">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
				<Database class="h-5 w-5 text-accent-text" />
				<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg">پشتیبان‌گیری کامل از داده‌ها (JSON)</h2>
			</div>

			<span class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
				{toPersianDigits(data.stats.habitsCount)} طرح • {toPersianDigits(data.stats.entriesCount)} روز ثبت‌شده
			</span>
		</div>

		<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
			<!-- خروجی گرفتن -->
			<div class="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-5">
				<div class="space-y-2">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
						<HardDriveDownload class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
						<span>خروجی و دانلود بکاپ (Export)</span>
					</div>
					<p class="text-xs leading-6 text-fg-secondary">
						یک فایل استاندارد <code class="font-mono text-fg">.json</code> شامل تمام طرح‌ها، عملکرد روزانه، خاطرات، کتاب‌ها، مدیا و یادداشت‌های شما دانلود می‌شود.
					</p>
				</div>

				<form
					method="POST"
					action="?/exportBackup"
					use:enhance={() => {
						isExporting = true;
						return async ({ update }) => {
							isExporting = false;
							await update({ reset: false });
						};
					}}
					class="mt-4"
				>
					<button
						type="submit"
						disabled={isExporting}
						class="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-accent text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
					>
						<Download class="h-4 w-4" />
						<span>{isExporting ? 'در حال تهیه فایل...' : 'دانلود فایل پشتیبان (JSON)'}</span>
					</button>
				</form>
			</div>

			<!-- بازیابی و وارد کردن -->
			<div class="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-5">
				<div class="space-y-2">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
						<HardDriveUpload class="h-4 w-4 text-gold-text" />
						<span>بازیابی فایل بکاپ (Import)</span>
					</div>
					<p class="text-xs leading-6 text-fg-secondary">
						بارگذاری فایل بکاپ برای بازگردانی اطلاعات یا انتقال به یک دستگاه جدید.
					</p>
				</div>

				<form
					method="POST"
					action="?/importBackup"
					enctype="multipart/form-data"
					use:enhance={() => {
						isImporting = true;
						return async ({ update }) => {
							isImporting = false;
							selectedFile = null;
							fileSummary = null;
							await syncEngine.clearLocalDataAndCaches();
							await update();
						};
					}}
					class="mt-4 space-y-3"
				>
					<label
						class="flex h-11 w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg"
					>
						<span class="truncate">{selectedFile ? selectedFile.name : 'انتخاب فایل JSON...'}</span>
						<input
							type="file"
							name="backupFile"
							accept=".json,application/json"
							required
							onchange={handleFileSelected}
							class="hidden"
						/>
					</label>

					<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
						<button
							type="submit"
							disabled={!selectedFile || isImporting}
							class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-xs font-black text-gold-text transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-50"
						>
							<HardDriveUpload class="h-4 w-4" />
							<span>{isImporting ? 'در حال بازیابی...' : 'اعمال و بازگردانی داده‌ها'}</span>
						</button>
					</div>
				</form>
			</div>
		</div>
	</section>

	<!-- بخش امنیت و حساب کاربری -->
	<section
		class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7"
	>
		<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-border pb-3.5">
			<ShieldCheck class="h-5 w-5 text-accent-text" />
			<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg">امنیت و نشست کاربری</h2>
		</div>

		{#if form?.passwordSuccess}
			<div
				class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-optimal"
			>
				<Check class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
				<span>رمز عبور با موفقیت تغییر کرد.</span>
			</div>
		{/if}

		{#if form?.passwordError}
			<div
				class="rounded-xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-failed"
			>
				{form.passwordError}
			</div>
		{/if}

		<form
			method="POST"
			action="?/changePassword"
			use:enhance={() => {
				isChangingPassword = true;
				const passToSave = newPassword;
				return async ({ result, update }) => {
					isChangingPassword = false;
					if (result.type === 'success') {
						await clientAuth.saveClientCredential(passToSave);
						currentPassword = '';
						newPassword = '';
						confirmNewPassword = '';
					}
					await update();
				};
			}}
			class="space-y-3.5"
		>
			<div class="text-xs font-black text-fg">تغییر رمز عبور اصلی</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
				<div>
					<label for="cur-pass" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">رمز فعلی</label>
					<input
						id="cur-pass"
						name="currentPassword"
						type="password"
						required
						dir="ltr"
						bind:value={currentPassword}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="new-pass" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">رمز جدید</label>
					<input
						id="new-pass"
						name="newPassword"
						type="password"
						required
						minlength="4"
						dir="ltr"
						bind:value={newPassword}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="conf-pass" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تکرار رمز جدید</label>
					<input
						id="conf-pass"
						name="confirmNewPassword"
						type="password"
						required
						minlength="4"
						dir="ltr"
						bind:value={confirmNewPassword}
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex justify-end pt-1">
				<button
					type="submit"
					disabled={isChangingPassword}
					class="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-accent px-6 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					<KeyRound class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>{isChangingPassword ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ذخیره رمز جدید'}</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>

		<div class="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
				خروج از نشست این مرورگر و پاک‌سازی حافظه کش
			</div>

			<form
				method="POST"
				action="?/logout"
				use:enhance={() => {
					return async ({ update }) => {
						await clientAuth.logout();
						await syncEngine.clearLocalDataAndCaches();
						await update();
					};
				}}
			>
				<button
					type="submit"
					onclick={async (e) => {
						if (!navigator.onLine) {
							e.preventDefault();
							await clientAuth.logout();
							await syncEngine.clearLocalDataAndCaches();
							await goto('/login');
						}
					}}
					class="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-failed transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
				>
					<LogOut class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>خروج از حساب</span>
				</button>
			</form>
		</div>
	</section>
</div>

PATH: src/routes/settings/+page.ts

import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { getFullWorkspaceFromIdb } from '$lib/state/idb-sync-store';
import type { PageLoad } from './$types';

export const load: PageLoad = async () => {
	const emptyStats = {
		habitsCount: 0,
		entriesCount: 0,
		logsCount: 0,
		booksCount: 0,
		mediaCount: 0,
		notesCount: 0,
		eventsCount: 0
	};

	if (!browser) {
		return { stats: emptyStats, isAuthenticated: true };
	}

	const ws = await getFullWorkspaceFromIdb();
	if (ws) {
		return {
			stats: {
				habitsCount: ws.habits.length,
				entriesCount: ws.entries.length,
				logsCount: ws.logs.length,
				booksCount: ws.books.length,
				mediaCount: ws.media.length,
				notesCount: ws.notes.length,
				eventsCount: ws.calendarEvents.length
			},
			isAuthenticated: true
		};
	}

	return { stats: emptyStats, isAuthenticated: true };
};

================================================================
PATH: src/routes/settings/+page.server.ts
================================================================

import { fail, redirect } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import {
	ChangePasswordInputSchema,
	DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema
} from '@domain/schemas';
import {
	changeMasterPassword,
	revokeAuthSession
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/auth.repo';
import {
	exportDatabaseToJson,
	importDatabaseFromJson
} from '$lib/server/db/repositories/backup.repo';
import { SESSION_COOKIE_NAME } from '$lib/server/db/repositories/auth.repo';
import type { Actions, PageServerLoad } from './$types';

export const actions: Actions = {
	exportBackup: async () => {
		const payload = exportDatabaseToJson();
		return {
			exportPayload: JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2),
			filename: `new-life-backup-${payload.jalaliDate.replace(/\//g, '-')}.json`
		};
	},

	importBackup: async ({ request }) => {
		const fd = await request.formData();
		const backupFile = fd.get('backupFile');
		const importMode = String(fd.get('importMode') ?? 'replace') as 'replace' | 'merge';

		if (!(backupFile instanceof File) || backupFile.size === 0) {
			return fail(400, { importError: 'لطفاً یک فایل معتبر JSON انتخاب کنید' });
		}

		let rawContent: string;
		try {
			rawContent = await backupFile.text();
		} catch {
			return fail(400, { importError: 'خواندن محتوای فایل با خطا مواجه شد' });
		}

		let parsedJson: unknown;
		try {
			parsedJson = JSON.parse(rawContent);
		} catch {
			return fail(400, { importError: 'فرمت فایل JSON نامعتبر است' });
		}

		const validated = DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema.safeParse(parsedJson);
		if (!validated.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				importError: `ساختار فایل پشتیبان معتبر نیست: ${validated.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'داده نامعتبر'}`
			});
		}

		const result = importDatabaseFromJson(validated.data, importMode);

		return {
			importSuccess: true,
			importedCount: result.importedRecordsCount
		};
	},

	changePassword: async ({ request, locals }) => {
		if (!locals.isAuthenticated) {
			return fail(401, { passwordError: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شوید' });
		}

		const fd = await request.formData();
		const parsed = ChangePasswordInputSchema.safeParse({
			currentPassword: String(fd.get('currentPassword') ?? ''),
			newPassword: String(fd.get('newPassword') ?? ''),
			confirmNewPassword: String(fd.get('confirmNewPassword') ?? '')
		});

		if (!parsed.success) {
			return fail(400, {
				passwordError: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'خطا در ورودی‌های رمز عبور'
			});
		}

		const ok = await changeMasterPassword(
			parsed.data.currentPassword,
			parsed.data.newPassword
		);

		if (!ok) {
			return fail(400, { passwordError: 'رمز عبور فعلی نادرست است' });
		}

		return { passwordSuccess: true };
	},

	logout: async ({ cookies, locals }) => {
		if (locals.sessionToken) {
			revokeAuthSession(locals.sessionToken);
		}
		cookies.delete(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, { path: '/' });
		redirect(303, '/login');
	}
};

export interface DayStatusMetaItem {
	value: DayStatusColor;
	label: string;
	shortBadgeLabel: string;
	dotClass: string;
	softBadgeClasses: string;
	activeClasses: string;
}

export const DAY_STATUS_META: Record<DayStatusColor, DayStatusMetaItem> = {
	optimal: {
		value: 'optimal',
		label: 'سبز (مطلوب / افزایش)',
		shortBadgeLabel: 'سبز (مطلوب)',

		softBadgeClasses: 'bg-failed-soft text-failed',
		activeClasses: 'border-failed bg-elevated text-failed'
	}
};

export const DAY_STATUS_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<DayStatusMetaItem> = [
	DAY_STATUS_META.optimal,
	DAY_STATUS_META.partial,
	DAY_STATUS_META.failed
];

export function getDayStatusMeta(status: DayStatusColor | null | undefined): DayStatusMetaItem {
	if (status === 'optimal') return DAY_STATUS_META.optimal;
	if (status === 'partial') return DAY_STATUS_META.partial;
	return DAY_STATUS_META.failed;
}

export function getTierBadgeMeta(tier: number | null | undefined): {
	label: string;
	classes: string;
} {
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		return { label: 'طلا', classes: 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-gold-text' };
	}
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		return { label: 'نقره', classes: 'border-silver-border bg-silver-soft text-silver-text' };
	}
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE) {
		return { label: 'برنز', classes: 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft text-bronze-text' };
	}
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
		return { label: 'پیش نیامد', classes: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary' };
	}
	return { label: 'نشد', classes: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed' };
}

export function getOutcomeBadgeMeta(
	outcome: DayHabitOutcome,
	maxSupportedTier: 1 | 2 | 3 = 3
): {
	label: string;
	badgeClass: string;
} {
	if (outcome === 'gold') {
		return {
			label: maxSupportedTier === 1 ? 'انجام کامل' : 'سطح طلا',
			badgeClass: 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-gold-text'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'silver') {
		return {
			label: 'سطح نقره',
			badgeClass: 'border-silver-border bg-silver-soft text-silver-text'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'bronze') {
		return {
			label: 'سطح برنز (حداقل)',
			badgeClass: 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft text-bronze-text'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'failed') {
		return {
			label: 'انجام نشد',
			badgeClass: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'skipped') {
		return {
			label: 'پیش نیامد',
			badgeClass: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary'
		};
	}
	return {
		label: 'ثبت‌نشده',
		badgeClass: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-muted'
	};
}

----------------------------------------------------------------

import * as jalaali from 'jalaali-js';

export interface JalaliParts {
	jy: number;
	jm: number;
	jd: number;
}

const PERSIAN_MONTHS = [
	'فروردین',
	'اردیبهشت',
	'خرداد',
	'تیر',
	'مرداد',
	'شهریور',
	'مهر',
	'آبان',
	'آذر',
	'دی',
	'بهمن',
	'اسفند'
] as const;

export const JALALI_MONTH_NAMES = PERSIAN_MONTHS;

export const JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS = [
	'شنبه',
	'یکشنبه',
	'دوشنبه',
	'سه‌شنبه',
	'چهارشنبه',
	'پنجشنبه',
	'جمعه'
] as const;

export const JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT = [
	'ش',
	'۱ش',
	'۲ش',
	'۳ش',
	'۴ش',
	'۵ش',
	'ج'
] as const;

const TEHRAN_ISO_FORMATTER = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
	timeZone: 'Asia/Tehran',
	year: 'numeric',
	month: '2-digit',
	day: '2-digit'
});

export function getPersianWeekdayIndex(date: Date): number {
	return (date.getDay() + 1) % 7;
}

export function padZero(num: number, size = 2): string {
	return String(num).padStart(size, '0');
}

export function normalizeDigits(input: string): string {
	return input
		.replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (d) => String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d)))
		.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => String('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)));
}

export function toPersianDigits(input: string | number): string {
	return String(input)
		.replace(/[0-9]/g, (d) => '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'[Number(d)])
		.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'['٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)]);
}

export function dateToIso(date: Date): string {
	const y = date.getFullYear();
	const m = padZero(date.getMonth() + 1);
	const d = padZero(date.getDate());
	return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

export function isoToDate(iso: string): Date {
	const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
	return new Date(y, (m || 1) - 1, d || 1, 12, 0, 0);
}

export function getTodayIso(): string {
	return TEHRAN_ISO_FORMATTER.format(new Date());
}

export function getYesterdayIso(): string {
	return shiftIsoDate(getTodayIso(), -1);
}

export function shiftIsoDate(iso: string, deltaDays: number): string {
	const dt = isoToDate(iso);
	dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + deltaDays);
	return dateToIso(dt);
}

export function isoToJalaliParts(iso: string): JalaliParts {
	const [gy, gm, gd] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
	return jalaali.toJalaali(gy, gm, gd);
}

export function jalaliPartsToIso(parts: JalaliParts): string | null {
	if (!jalaali.isValidJalaaliDate(parts.jy, parts.jm, parts.jd)) {
		return null;
	}
	const { gy, gm, gd } = jalaali.toGregorian(parts.jy, parts.jm, parts.jd);
	return `${gy}-${padZero(gm)}-${padZero(gd)}`;
}

export function isoToJalaliSlash(iso: string): string {
	const { jy, jm, jd } = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
	return `${jy}/${padZero(jm)}/${padZero(jd)}`;
}

export function jalaliSlashToIso(jalaliStr: string): string | null {
	const cleaned = normalizeDigits(jalaliStr.trim());
	const match = cleaned.match(/^(\d{4})[/-](\d{1,2})[/-](\d{1,2})$/);
	if (!match) return null;
	const jy = Number(match[1]);
	const jm = Number(match[2]);
	const jd = Number(match[3]);
	return jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd });
}

export function resolveCompletedRangeDates(params: {
	startedJalali?: string | null;
	finishedJalali?: string | null;
	isCompleted: boolean;
	todayIso?: string;
}): {
	startedAtIso: string | null;
	finishedAtIso: string | null;
} {
	const todayIso = params.todayIso ?? getTodayIso();
	const startedAtIso = params.startedJalali?.trim()
		? jalaliSlashToIso(params.startedJalali.trim())
		: null;
	const finishedAtIso = params.finishedJalali?.trim()
		? jalaliSlashToIso(params.finishedJalali.trim())
		: params.isCompleted
			? todayIso
			: null;

	return { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso };
}

export function formatJalaliHuman(
	iso: string,
	referenceTodayIso?: string
): {
	weekday: string;
	weekdayShort: string;
	day: number;
	monthName: string;
	year: number;
	fullLabel: string;
	relativeBadge: string | null;
} {
	const dt = isoToDate(iso);
	const weekdayIdx = getPersianWeekdayIndex(dt);
	const weekday = JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[weekdayIdx];
	const weekdayShort = JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT[weekdayIdx];
	const { jy, jm, jd } = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
	const monthName = PERSIAN_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? '';

	const todayIso = referenceTodayIso ?? getTodayIso();
	const yesterdayIso = shiftIsoDate(todayIso, -1);
	const tomorrowIso = shiftIsoDate(todayIso, 1);

	let relativeBadge: string | null = null;
	if (iso === yesterdayIso) relativeBadge = 'دیروز';
	else if (iso === todayIso) relativeBadge = 'امروز';
	else if (iso === tomorrowIso) relativeBadge = 'فردا';

	return {
		weekday,
		weekdayShort,
		day: jd,
		monthName,
		year: jy,
		fullLabel: `${weekday} ${toPersianDigits(jd)} ${monthName} ${toPersianDigits(jy)}`,
		relativeBadge
	};
}

export interface CalendarScrubberDay<TStatus extends string = string> {
	iso: string;
	jd: number;
	weekdayShort: string;
	monthName: string;
	isSelected: boolean;
	visualState: TStatus;
}

export function buildRecentCalendarDays<TStatus extends string>(params: {
	currentDateIso: string;
	todayIso: string;
	savedStatusByIso: Map<string, TStatus>;
	liveStatus: TStatus;
	useLiveStatusForSelected: boolean;
	defaultStatus: TStatus;
	daysCount?: number;
}): CalendarScrubberDay<TStatus>[] {
	const {
		currentDateIso,
		todayIso,
		savedStatusByIso,
		liveStatus,
		useLiveStatusForSelected,
		defaultStatus,
		daysCount = 14
	} = params;

	const anchorIso = currentDateIso > todayIso ? currentDateIso : todayIso;
	const list: CalendarScrubberDay<TStatus>[] = [];

	for (let i = daysCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
		const iso = shiftIsoDate(anchorIso, -i);
		const dt = isoToDate(iso);
		const { jm, jd } = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
		const isSelected = iso === currentDateIso;

		let visualState = defaultStatus;
		if (isSelected && useLiveStatusForSelected) {
			visualState = liveStatus;
		} else {
			const saved = savedStatusByIso.get(iso);
			if (saved) visualState = saved;
		}

		list.push({
			iso,
			jd,
			weekdayShort: JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT[getPersianWeekdayIndex(dt)],
			monthName: PERSIAN_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? '',
			isSelected,
			visualState
		});
	}

	return list;
}

export function getJalaliMonthLength(jy: number, jm: number): number {
	return jalaali.jalaaliMonthLength(jy, jm);
}

export function getDaysUntilIso(fromIso: string, targetIso: string): number {
	const d1 = isoToDate(fromIso);
	const d2 = isoToDate(targetIso);
	return Math.round((d2.getTime() - d1.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
}

export interface JalaliMonthGridCell {
	iso: string;
	jy: number;
	jm: number;
	jd: number;
	weekdayLabel: string;
	isToday: boolean;
}

export function buildJalaliMonthGrid(
	jy: number,
	jm: number,
	todayIso = getTodayIso()
): {
	year: number;
	month: number;
	monthName: string;
	leadingEmptyCells: number;
	days: JalaliMonthGridCell[];
} {
	const monthLength = jalaali.jalaaliMonthLength(jy, jm);
	const firstDayIso = jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd: 1 }) ?? todayIso;
	const firstDate = isoToDate(firstDayIso);
	// Convert JS getDay (0=Sun..6=Sat) to Persian week index (0=Sat..6=Fri)
	const leadingEmptyCells = getPersianWeekdayIndex(firstDate);

	const days: JalaliMonthGridCell[] = [];
	for (let jd = 1; jd <= monthLength; jd++) {
		const iso = jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd }) ?? todayIso;
		const weekdayIdx = (leadingEmptyCells + jd - 1) % 7;
		days.push({
			iso,
			jy,
			jm,
			jd,
			weekdayLabel: JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[weekdayIdx],
			isToday: iso === todayIso
		});
	}

	return {
		year: jy,
		month: jm,
		monthName: PERSIAN_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? '',
		leadingEmptyCells,
		days
	};
}

PATH: src/domain/tags.ts

export function parseTagsJson(rawJson: string | null | undefined): string[] {
	if (!rawJson) return [];
	try {
		const parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson);
		return Array.isArray(parsed)
			? parsed.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string')
			: [];
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}

	CADENCE_DEFAULTS,

	TIER_SCORES
} from './constants';

export const TrackingModeSchema = z.enum(['tiered', 'interval_cadence']);
export type TrackingMode = z.infer<typeof TrackingModeSchema>;

export const AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema = z.enum(['7d', '30d', '90d', 'all']);
export type AnalyticsTimeRange = z.infer<typeof AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema>;

export const HabitStatusSchema = z.enum(['active', 'paused', 'archived']);
export type HabitStatus = z.infer<typeof HabitStatusSchema>;

export const DayStatusColorSchema = z.enum(['optimal', 'partial', 'failed']);
export type DayStatusColor = z.infer<typeof DayStatusColorSchema>;

export const LifecycleEventTypeSchema = z.enum([
	'CREATED',
	'UPDATED_RULES',
	'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT',
	'PROMOTED_TO_ROOT',
	'CADENCE_UPDATED',
	'STATUS_CHANGED',
	'DELETED'
]);
export type LifecycleEventType = z.infer<typeof LifecycleEventTypeSchema>;

export type BookStatus = z.infer<typeof BookStatusSchema>;

export type MediaCategory = z.infer<typeof MediaCategorySchema>;

export const MediaStatusFilterSchema = z.enum(['ALL', 'UNSEEN', 'SEEN']);
export type MediaStatusFilter = z.infer<typeof MediaStatusFilterSchema>;

export const MediaSortBySchema = z.enum(['rating_desc', 'newest', 'oldest', 'title_asc']);
export type MediaSortBy = z.infer<typeof MediaSortBySchema>;

export const NoteSmartFilterSchema = z.enum(['ALL', 'HAS_LINK', 'HAS_NUMBER']);

export const HabitTimeSlotSchema = z.enum(['morning', 'day', 'night']);

	'birthday',
	'deadline',
	'occasion',
	'reminder'
]);

export const HabitEntitySchema = z.object({
	id: z.string(),

	trackingMode: TrackingModeSchema,

	notes: z.string().nullable(),
	bronzeDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	silverDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	goldDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	emergencyMinDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	defaultIntervals: z.number().int().min(0),
	defaultMinutesPerInterval: z.number().min(0),
	cadenceWindowMinutes: z.number().int().min(CADENCE_DEFAULTS.MIN_WINDOW_MINUTES),
	sortOrder: z.number().int().default(0),
	createdAtIso: z.string(),
	updatedAtIso: z.string().nullable().optional()
});
export type HabitEntity = z.infer<typeof HabitEntitySchema>;

});

export const CreateHabitInputSchema = z.object({

export const MutateHabitStructureSchema = z.object({
	habitId: z.string(),
	title: z.string().min(2),
	category: z.string().default(DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY),
	parentId: z.string().nullable(),
	timeSlot: HabitTimeSlotSchema.optional(),
	isSituational: z.boolean().default(false),
	status: HabitStatusSchema,
	scheduleLabel: z.string().nullable(),
	notes: z.string().nullable(),
	bronzeDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	silverDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	goldDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	emergencyMinDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	reason: z.string().min(2, 'نوشتن دلیل تغییر برای ثبت در تاریخچه الزامی است')
});
export type MutateHabitStructureInput = z.infer<typeof MutateHabitStructureSchema>;

export const BookEntitySchema = z.object({
	id: z.string(),
	title: z.string(),
	author: z.string().nullable(),
	status: BookStatusSchema,
	startedAtIso: z.string().nullable(),
	finishedAtIso: z.string().nullable(),
	rating: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).nullable(),
	notes: z.string().nullable(),
	createdAtIso: z.string(),
	updatedAtIso: z.string().nullable().optional()
});
export type BookEntity = z.infer<typeof BookEntitySchema>;

export const SetupPasswordInputSchema = z
	.object({
		password: z.string().min(4, 'رمز عبور باید حداقل ۴ کاراکتر باشد'),
		confirmPassword: z.string().min(4, 'تکرار رمز عبور الزامی است')
	})
	.refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
		message: 'رمز عبور و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند',
		path: ['confirmPassword']
	});

export const LoginInputSchema = z.object({
	password: z.string().min(1, 'لطفاً رمز عبور را وارد کنید')
});

export const ChangePasswordInputSchema = z
	.object({
		currentPassword: z.string().min(1, 'رمز عبور فعلی الزامی است'),
		newPassword: z.string().min(4, 'رمز عبور جدید باید حداقل ۴ کاراکتر باشد'),
		confirmNewPassword: z.string().min(4, 'تکرار رمز عبور جدید الزامی است')
	})
	.refine((data) => data.newPassword === data.confirmNewPassword, {
		message: 'رمز عبور جدید و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند',
		path: ['confirmNewPassword']
	});

export const SyncOutboxMutationSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: SaveDailyEntryInputSchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: SaveCadencePlanBatchSchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_BOOK'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: BookEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_BOOK'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ bookId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_MEDIA'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: MediaEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_MEDIA'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ mediaId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_NOTE'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: NoteEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_NOTE'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ noteId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: CalendarEventEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_CALENDAR_EVENT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ eventId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_HABIT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: HabitEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: LifecycleEventEntitySchema
	})
]);
export type SyncOutboxMutation = z.infer<typeof SyncOutboxMutationSchema>;

export const SyncRequestPayloadSchema = z.object({
	clientId: z.string().default('default-client'),

	mutations: z.array(SyncOutboxMutationSchema).default([])
});

		<meta name="mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
		<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
		<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="black-translucent" />
		<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title" content="ردیابی طرح‌ها" />
		<link rel="manifest" href="/manifest.webmanifest" />
		<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/favicon.svg" />
		<script>
			(function () {
				try {
					var stored = localStorage.getItem('newlife_theme');
					var isDark = stored
						? stored === 'dark'
						: window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
					var root = document.documentElement;
					root.classList.toggle('dark', isDark);
					root.style.colorScheme = isDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
				} catch (_) {}
			})();
		</script>
		<link
			rel="preload"
			href="/Vazirmatn.woff2"
			as="font"
			type="font/woff2"
			crossorigin="anonymous"
		/>
		%sveltekit.head%
	</head>
	<body data-sveltekit-preload-data="hover">
		<div style="display: contents">%sveltekit.body%</div>
	</body>
</html>

================================================================
PATH: vite.config.ts

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import adapter from '@sveltejs/adapter-node';
import { sveltekit } from '@sveltejs/kit/vite';
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import path from 'node:path';

export default defineConfig({
	resolve: {
		alias: {
			'@domain': path.resolve('./src/domain'),
			'@core': path.resolve('./src/core'),
			'$lib': path.resolve('./src/lib')
		}
	},
	plugins: [
		tailwindcss(),
		sveltekit({
			compilerOptions: {
				// Force runes mode for the project, except for libraries. Can be removed in svelte 6.
				runes: ({ filename }) =>
					filename.split(/[/\\]/).includes('node_modules') ? undefined : true
			},
			adapter: adapter()
		})
	]
});

PATH: docker-compose.yml

services:
  new-life:
    image: ${IMAGE_NAME:-new-life-local:latest}
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: new-life-app
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - '127.0.0.1:3090:3000'
    environment:
      - NODE_ENV=production
      - HOST=0.0.0.0
      - PORT=3000
      - ORIGIN=${ORIGIN:-http://localhost:3000}
      - ADDRESS_HEADER=x-forwarded-for
      - XFF_DEPTH=1
      - DATABASE_URL=/app/data/local.db
    volumes:
      - ./data:/app/data
